 325: /*
 326:  * =========================================
 327:  * Option Helpers
 328:  * =========================================
 329:  */
 330: 
 331: function findOption(options, value) {
 332:   return options.find((option) => option.value === value) || null;
 333: }
 334: 
 335: export function getEmploymentTypeOption(value) {
 336:   return findOption(EMPLOYMENT_TYPE_OPTIONS, value);
 337: }
 338: 
 339: export function getEmploymentTypeLabel(value) {
 340:   return getEmploymentTypeOption(value)?.label || "";
 341: }
 342: 
 343: export function getWorkArrangementOption(value) {
 344:   return findOption(WORK_ARRANGEMENT_OPTIONS, value);
 345: }
 346: 
 347: export function getWorkArrangementLabel(value) {
 348:   return getWorkArrangementOption(value)?.label || "";
 349: }
 350: 
 351: export function getExperienceCategoryOption(value) {
 352:   return findOption(EXPERIENCE_CATEGORY_OPTIONS, value);
 353: }
 354: 
 355: export function getExperienceCategoryLabel(value) {
 356:   return getExperienceCategoryOption(value)?.label || "";
 357: }
 358: 
 359: export function getExperienceStatusOption(value) {
 360:   return findOption(EXPERIENCE_STATUS_OPTIONS, value);
 361: }
 362: 
 363: export function getExperienceStatusLabel(value) {
 364:   return getExperienceStatusOption(value)?.label || "";
 365: }
 366: 
 367: export function isValidEmploymentType(value) {
 368:   return Boolean(getEmploymentTypeOption(value));
 369: }
 370: 
 371: export function isValidWorkArrangement(value) {
 372:   return Boolean(getWorkArrangementOption(value));
 373: }
 374: 
 375: export function isValidExperienceCategory(value) {
 376:   return Boolean(getExperienceCategoryOption(value));
 377: }
 378: 
 379: export function isValidExperienceStatus(value) {
 380:   return Boolean(getExperienceStatusOption(value));
 381: }

   1: import {
   2:   createContext,
   3:   useCallback,
   4:   useContext,
   5:   useEffect,
   6:   useMemo,
   7:   useState,
   8: } from "react";
   9: 
  10: import {
  11:   archiveExperience as archiveExperienceService,
  12:   createExperience as createExperienceService,
  13:   deleteExperience as deleteExperienceService,
  14:   getExperienceById as getExperienceByIdService,
  15:   getExperiences as getExperiencesService,
  16:   restoreExperience as restoreExperienceService,
  17:   updateExperience as updateExperienceService,
  18:   validateExperienceDraft as validateExperienceDraftService,
  19: } from "../services/Experience/experienceService.js";
  20: 
  21: /*
  22:  * =========================================
  23:  * Context
  24:  * =========================================
  25:  */
  26: 
  27: const ExperienceDataContext = createContext(null);
  28: 
  29: /*
  30:  * =========================================
  31:  * Default Status
  32:  * =========================================
  33:  */
  34: 
  35: const INITIAL_OPERATION_STATE = {
  36:   type: "",
  37:   experienceId: "",
  38:   status: "idle",
  39: };
  40: 
  41: /*
  42:  * =========================================
  43:  * Error Normalization
  44:  * =========================================
  45:  */
  46: 
  47: function normalizeContextError(error) {
  48:   return {
  49:     message:
  50:       error?.publicMessage ||
  51:       error?.message ||
  52:       "An unexpected experience-management error occurred.",
  53: 
  54:     code: error?.code || "EXPERIENCE_OPERATION_FAILED",
  55: 
  56:     status: Number(error?.status) || 500,
  57: 
  58:     errors: Array.isArray(error?.errors) ? error.errors : [],
  59: 
  60:     fieldErrors:
  61:       error?.fieldErrors && typeof error.fieldErrors === "object"
  62:         ? error.fieldErrors
  63:         : {},
  64: 
  65:     validation: error?.validation || null,
  66: 
  67:     originalError: error,
  68:   };
  69: }
  70: 
  71: /*
  72:  * =========================================
  73:  * Provider
  74:  * =========================================
  75:  */
  76: 
  77: export function ExperienceDataProvider({ children }) {
  78:   const [experiences, setExperiences] = useState([]);
  79: 
  80:   const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  81: 
  82:   const [loadStatus, setLoadStatus] = useState("idle");
  83: 
  84:   const [saveStatus, setSaveStatus] = useState("idle");
  85: 
  86:   const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  87: 
  88:   const [operation, setOperation] = useState(INITIAL_OPERATION_STATE);
  89: 
  90:   /*
  91:    * =========================================
  92:    * Error Management
  93:    * =========================================
  94:    */
  95: 
  96:   const clearError = useCallback(() => {
  97:     setError(null);
  98:   }, []);
  99: 
 100:   const resetSaveStatus = useCallback(() => {
 101:     setSaveStatus("idle");
 102:   }, []);
 103: 
 104:   const resetOperation = useCallback(() => {
 105:     setOperation(INITIAL_OPERATION_STATE);
 106:   }, []);
 107: 
 108:   /*
 109:    * =========================================
 110:    * Load Experiences
 111:    * =========================================
 112:    */
 113: 
 114:   const refreshExperiences = useCallback(
 115:     async ({ includeArchived = true } = {}) => {
 116:       try {
 117:         setIsLoading(true);
 118:         setLoadStatus("loading");
 119:         setError(null);
 120: 
 121:         const loadedExperiences = await getExperiencesService({
 122:           includeArchived,
 123:         });
 124: 
 125:         setExperiences(
 126:           Array.isArray(loadedExperiences) ? loadedExperiences : [],
 127:         );
 128: 
 129:         setLoadStatus("success");
 130: 
 131:         return loadedExperiences;
 132:       } catch (loadError) {
 133:         const normalizedError = normalizeContextError(loadError);
 134: 
 135:         console.error("Unable to load experiences:", loadError);
 136: 
 137:         setExperiences([]);
 138:         setLoadStatus("error");
 139:         setError(normalizedError);
 140: 
 141:         throw loadError;
 142:       } finally {
 143:         setIsLoading(false);
 144:       }
 145:     },
 146:     [],
 147:   );
 148: 
 149:   /*
 150:    * =========================================
 151:    * Initial Load
 152:    * =========================================
 153:    */
 154: 
 155:   useEffect(() => {
 156:     let isActive = true;
 157: 
 158:     const loadInitialExperiences = async () => {
 159:       try {
 160:         setIsLoading(true);
 161:         setLoadStatus("loading");
 162:         setError(null);
 163: 
 164:         const loadedExperiences = await getExperiencesService({
 165:           includeArchived: true,
 166:         });
 167: 
 168:         if (!isActive) {
 169:           return;
 170:         }
 171: 
 172:         setExperiences(
 173:           Array.isArray(loadedExperiences) ? loadedExperiences : [],
 174:         );
 175: 
 176:         setLoadStatus("success");
 177:       } catch (loadError) {
 178:         if (!isActive) {
 179:           return;
 180:         }
 181: 
 182:         console.error("Unable to load experiences:", loadError);
 183: 
 184:         setExperiences([]);
 185:         setLoadStatus("error");
 186: 
 187:         setError(normalizeContextError(loadError));
 188:       } finally {
 189:         if (isActive) {
 190:           setIsLoading(false);
 191:         }
 192:       }
 193:     };
 194: 
 195:     loadInitialExperiences();
 196: 
 197:     return () => {
 198:       isActive = false;
 199:     };
 200:   }, []);
 201: 
 202:   /*
 203:    * =========================================
 204:    * Get Experience
 205:    * =========================================
 206:    */
 207: 
 208:   const getExperienceById = useCallback(
 209:     async (experienceId) => {
 210:       /*
 211:        * First use the existing context state to
 212:        * avoid an unnecessary service request.
 213:        */
 214: 
 215:       const existingExperience = experiences.find(
 216:         (experience) => experience.id === experienceId,
 217:       );
 218: 
 219:       if (existingExperience) {
 220:         return existingExperience;
 221:       }
 222: 
 223:       try {
 224:         setError(null);
 225: 
 226:         return await getExperienceByIdService(experienceId);
 227:       } catch (getError) {
 228:         console.error("Unable to get experience:", getError);
 229: 
 230:         setError(normalizeContextError(getError));
 231: 
 232:         throw getError;
 233:       }
 234:     },
 235:     [experiences],
 236:   );
 237: 
 238:   /*
 239:    * =========================================
 240:    * Create Experience
 241:    * =========================================
 242:    */
 243: 
 244:   const createExperience = useCallback(async (experienceData) => {
 245:     try {
 246:       setSaveStatus("saving");
 247:       setError(null);
 248: 
 249:       setOperation({
 250:         type: "create",
 251:         experienceId: "",
 252:         status: "loading",
 253:       });
 254: 
 255:       const createdExperience = await createExperienceService(experienceData);
 256: 
 257:       setExperiences((currentExperiences) => [
 258:         createdExperience,
 259: 
 260:         ...currentExperiences.filter(
 261:           (experience) => experience.id !== createdExperience.id,
 262:         ),
 263:       ]);
 264: 
 265:       setSaveStatus("success");
 266: 
 267:       setOperation({
 268:         type: "create",
 269:         experienceId: createdExperience.id,
 270:         status: "success",
 271:       });
 272: 
 273:       return createdExperience;
 274:     } catch (createError) {
 275:       console.error("Unable to create experience:", createError);
 276: 
 277:       setSaveStatus("error");
 278: 
 279:       setOperation({
 280:         type: "create",
 281:         experienceId: "",
 282:         status: "error",
 283:       });
 284: 
 285:       setError(normalizeContextError(createError));
 286: 
 287:       throw createError;
 288:     }
 289:   }, []);
 290: 
 291:   /*
 292:    * =========================================
 293:    * Update Experience
 294:    * =========================================
 295:    */
 296: 
 297:   const updateExperience = useCallback(async (experienceId, updates) => {
 298:     try {
 299:       setSaveStatus("saving");
 300:       setError(null);
 301: 
 302:       setOperation({
 303:         type: "update",
 304:         experienceId,
 305:         status: "loading",
 306:       });
 307: 
 308:       const updatedExperience = await updateExperienceService(
 309:         experienceId,
 310:         updates,
 311:       );
 312: 
 313:       setExperiences((currentExperiences) =>
 314:         currentExperiences.map((experience) =>
 315:           experience.id === experienceId ? updatedExperience : experience,
 316:         ),
 317:       );
 318: 
 319:       setSaveStatus("success");
 320: 
 321:       setOperation({
 322:         type: "update",
 323:         experienceId,
 324:         status: "success",
 325:       });
 326: 
 327:       return updatedExperience;
 328:     } catch (updateError) {
 329:       console.error("Unable to update experience:", updateError);
 330: 
 331:       setSaveStatus("error");
 332: 
 333:       setOperation({
 334:         type: "update",
 335:         experienceId,
 336:         status: "error",
 337:       });
 338: 
 339:       setError(normalizeContextError(updateError));
 340: 
 341:       throw updateError;
 342:     }
 343:   }, []);
 344: 
 345:   /*
 346:    * =========================================
 347:    * Archive Experience
 348:    * =========================================
 349:    */
 350: 
 351:   const archiveExperience = useCallback(async (experienceId) => {
 352:     try {
 353:       setSaveStatus("saving");
 354:       setError(null);
 355: 
 356:       setOperation({
 357:         type: "archive",
 358:         experienceId,
 359:         status: "loading",
 360:       });
 361: 
 362:       const archivedExperience = await archiveExperienceService(experienceId);
 363: 
 364:       setExperiences((currentExperiences) =>
 365:         currentExperiences.map((experience) =>
 366:           experience.id === experienceId ? archivedExperience : experience,
 367:         ),
 368:       );
 369: 
 370:       setSaveStatus("success");
 371: 
 372:       setOperation({
 373:         type: "archive",
 374:         experienceId,
 375:         status: "success",
 376:       });
 377: 
 378:       return archivedExperience;
 379:     } catch (archiveError) {
 380:       console.error("Unable to archive experience:", archiveError);
 381: 
 382:       setSaveStatus("error");
 383: 
 384:       setOperation({
 385:         type: "archive",
 386:         experienceId,
 387:         status: "error",
 388:       });
 389: 
 390:       setError(normalizeContextError(archiveError));
 391: 
 392:       throw archiveError;
 393:     }
 394:   }, []);
 395: 
 396:   /*
 397:    * =========================================
 398:    * Restore Experience
 399:    * =========================================
 400:    */
 401: 
 402:   const restoreExperience = useCallback(async (experienceId) => {
 403:     try {
 404:       setSaveStatus("saving");
 405:       setError(null);
 406: 
 407:       setOperation({
 408:         type: "restore",
 409:         experienceId,
 410:         status: "loading",
 411:       });
 412: 
 413:       const restoredExperience = await restoreExperienceService(experienceId);
 414: 
 415:       setExperiences((currentExperiences) =>
 416:         currentExperiences.map((experience) =>
 417:           experience.id === experienceId ? restoredExperience : experience,
 418:         ),
 419:       );
 420: 
 421:       setSaveStatus("success");
 422: 
 423:       setOperation({
 424:         type: "restore",
 425:         experienceId,
 426:         status: "success",
 427:       });
 428: 
 429:       return restoredExperience;
 430:     } catch (restoreError) {
 431:       console.error("Unable to restore experience:", restoreError);
 432: 
 433:       setSaveStatus("error");
 434: 
 435:       setOperation({
 436:         type: "restore",
 437:         experienceId,
 438:         status: "error",
 439:       });
 440: 
 441:       setError(normalizeContextError(restoreError));
 442: 
 443:       throw restoreError;
 444:     }
 445:   }, []);
 446: 
 447:   /*
 448:    * =========================================
 449:    * Delete Experience
 450:    * =========================================
 451:    */
 452: 
 453:   const deleteExperience = useCallback(async (experienceId) => {
 454:     try {
 455:       setSaveStatus("saving");
 456:       setError(null);
 457: 
 458:       setOperation({
 459:         type: "delete",
 460:         experienceId,
 461:         status: "loading",
 462:       });
 463: 
 464:       const deletionResult = await deleteExperienceService(experienceId);
 465: 
 466:       setExperiences((currentExperiences) =>
 467:         currentExperiences.filter(
 468:           (experience) => experience.id !== experienceId,
 469:         ),
 470:       );
 471: 
 472:       setSaveStatus("success");
 473: 
 474:       setOperation({
 475:         type: "delete",
 476:         experienceId,
 477:         status: "success",
 478:       });
 479: 
 480:       return deletionResult;
 481:     } catch (deleteError) {
 482:       console.error("Unable to delete experience:", deleteError);
 483: 
 484:       setSaveStatus("error");
 485: 
 486:       setOperation({
 487:         type: "delete",
 488:         experienceId,
 489:         status: "error",
 490:       });
 491: 
 492:       setError(normalizeContextError(deleteError));
 493: 
 494:       throw deleteError;
 495:     }
 496:   }, []);
 497: 
 498:   /*
 499:    * =========================================
 500:    * Validate Draft
 501:    * =========================================
 502:    */
 503: 
 504:   const validateExperienceDraft = useCallback(async (experienceData) => {
 505:     try {
 506:       return await validateExperienceDraftService(experienceData);
 507:     } catch (validationError) {
 508:       console.error("Unable to validate experience:", validationError);
 509: 
 510:       setError(normalizeContextError(validationError));
 511: 
 512:       throw validationError;
 513:     }
 514:   }, []);
 515: 
 516:   /*
 517:    * =========================================
 518:    * Derived Collections
 519:    * =========================================
 520:    */
 521: 
 522:   const activeExperiences = useMemo(
 523:     () => experiences.filter((experience) => experience.status !== "archived"),
 524:     [experiences],
 525:   );
 526: 
 527:   const archivedExperiences = useMemo(
 528:     () => experiences.filter((experience) => experience.status === "archived"),
 529:     [experiences],
 530:   );
 531: 
 532:   const currentExperiences = useMemo(
 533:     () =>
 534:       activeExperiences.filter(
 535:         (experience) => experience.dates?.isCurrent === true,
 536:       ),
 537:     [activeExperiences],
 538:   );
 539: 
 540:   const previousExperiences = useMemo(
 541:     () =>
 542:       activeExperiences.filter(
 543:         (experience) => experience.dates?.isCurrent !== true,
 544:       ),
 545:     [activeExperiences],
 546:   );
 547: 
 548:   /*
 549:    * =========================================
 550:    * Context Value
 551:    * =========================================
 552:    */
 553: 
 554:   const contextValue = useMemo(
 555:     () => ({
 556:       /*
 557:        * Records
 558:        */
 559: 
 560:       experiences,
 561:       activeExperiences,
 562:       archivedExperiences,
 563:       currentExperiences,
 564:       previousExperiences,
 565: 
 566:       /*
 567:        * Status
 568:        */
 569: 
 570:       isLoading,
 571:       loadStatus,
 572:       saveStatus,
 573:       operation,
 574:       error,
 575: 
 576:       /*
 577:        * Read operations
 578:        */
 579: 
 580:       refreshExperiences,
 581:       getExperienceById,
 582: 
 583:       /*
 584:        * Write operations
 585:        */
 586: 
 587:       createExperience,
 588:       updateExperience,
 589:       archiveExperience,
 590:       restoreExperience,
 591:       deleteExperience,
 592: 
 593:       /*
 594:        * Validation
 595:        */
 596: 
 597:       validateExperienceDraft,
 598: 
 599:       /*
 600:        * Status management
 601:        */
 602: 
 603:       clearError,
 604:       resetSaveStatus,
 605:       resetOperation,
 606:     }),
 607:     [
 608:       experiences,
 609:       activeExperiences,
 610:       archivedExperiences,
 611:       currentExperiences,
 612:       previousExperiences,
 613:       isLoading,
 614:       loadStatus,
 615:       saveStatus,
 616:       operation,
 617:       error,
 618:       refreshExperiences,
 619:       getExperienceById,
 620:       createExperience,
 621:       updateExperience,
 622:       archiveExperience,
 623:       restoreExperience,
 624:       deleteExperience,
 625:       validateExperienceDraft,
 626:       clearError,
 627:       resetSaveStatus,
 628:       resetOperation,
 629:     ],
 630:   );
 631: 
 632:   return (
 633:     <ExperienceDataContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
 634:       {children}
 635:     </ExperienceDataContext.Provider>
 636:   );
 637: }
 638: 
 639: /*
 640:  * =========================================
 641:  * Context Hook
 642:  * =========================================
 643:  */
 644: 
 645: export function useExperienceData() {
 646:   const context = useContext(ExperienceDataContext);
 647: 
 648:   if (!context) {
 649:     throw new Error(
 650:       "useExperienceData must be used inside ExperienceDataProvider.",
 651:     );
 652:   }
 653: 
 654:   return context;
 655: }

   1: import {
   2:   createContext,
   3:   useCallback,
   4:   useContext,
   5:   useEffect,
   6:   useMemo,
   7:   useState,
   8: } from "react";
   9: 
  10: import {
  11:   archiveProject as archiveProjectService,
  12:   createProject as createProjectService,
  13:   deleteProject as deleteProjectService,
  14:   getProjects,
  15:   restoreProject as restoreProjectService,
  16:   updateProject as updateProjectService,
  17: } from "../services/Project/projectService.js";
  18: 
  19: import { PROJECT_STORAGE_KEY } from "../services/Project/projectStorage.js";
  20: import { fetchProjects } from "../services/Project/projectApiService.js";
  21: 
  22: /*
  23:  * =========================================
  24:  * Project Data Context
  25:  * =========================================
  26:  */
  27: 
  28: const ProjectDataContext = createContext(null);
  29: 
  30: /*
  31:  * =========================================
  32:  * Error Normalization
  33:  * =========================================
  34:  */
  35: 
  36: function normalizeContextError(error) {
  37:   return {
  38:     name: error?.name || "ProjectError",
  39: 
  40:     code: error?.code || "PROJECT_OPERATION_ERROR",
  41: 
  42:     message:
  43:       error?.publicMessage ||
  44:       error?.message ||
  45:       "The Project Library operation could not be completed.",
  46: 
  47:     fieldErrors: error?.fieldErrors || error?.validation?.fieldErrors || {},
  48: 
  49:     errors: error?.errors || error?.validation?.errors || [],
  50: 
  51:     warnings: error?.warnings || error?.validation?.warnings || [],
  52: 
  53:     projectId: error?.projectId || error?.details?.projectId || "",
  54:   };
  55: }
  56: 
  57: /*
  58:  * =========================================
  59:  * Warning Normalization
  60:  * =========================================
  61:  */
  62: 
  63: function normalizeOperationWarnings(result) {
  64:   const operationWarnings = Array.isArray(result?.warnings)
  65:     ? result.warnings
  66:     : [];
  67: 
  68:   const assetCleanupWarning = result?.assetCleanupWarning;
  69: 
  70:   if (!assetCleanupWarning) {
  71:     return operationWarnings;
  72:   }
  73: 
  74:   const cleanupMessage =
  75:     typeof assetCleanupWarning === "string"
  76:       ? assetCleanupWarning
  77:       : assetCleanupWarning?.publicMessage ||
  78:         assetCleanupWarning?.message ||
  79:         "The Project was saved, but one or more unused files could not be removed.";
  80: 
  81:   return [
  82:     ...operationWarnings,
  83: 
  84:     {
  85:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_CLEANUP_WARNING",
  86:       field: "media",
  87:       message: cleanupMessage,
  88:     },
  89:   ];
  90: }
  91: 
  92: /*
  93:  * =========================================
  94:  * Project Data Provider
  95:  * =========================================
  96:  */
  97: 
  98: export function ProjectDataProvider({ children }) {
  99:   const [projects, setProjects] = useState([]);
 100: 
 101:   const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
 102: 
 103:   const [loadStatus, setLoadStatus] = useState("idle");
 104: 
 105:   const [saveStatus, setSaveStatus] = useState("idle");
 106: 
 107:   const [operation, setOperation] = useState(null);
 108: 
 109:   const [error, setError] = useState(null);
 110: 
 111:   const [warnings, setWarnings] = useState([]);
 112: 
 113:   const [backendStatus, setBackendStatus] = useState("idle");
 114: 
 115:   const [backendError, setBackendError] = useState(null);
 116: 
 117:   /*
 118:    * =========================================
 119:    * Load Project Records
 120:    * =========================================
 121:    */
 122: 
 123:   const refreshBackendProjects = useCallback(async (options = {}) => {
 124:   setBackendStatus("loading");
 125:   setBackendError(null);
 126: 
 127:   try {
 128:     const records = await fetchProjects(options);
 129: 
 130:     if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
 131:       throw new TypeError("The Project API did not return a Project array.");
 132:     }
 133: 
 134:     setBackendStatus("success");
 135: 
 136:     // Do not replace browser-local Project records.
 137:     return records;
 138:   } catch (caughtError) {
 139:     setBackendStatus("error");
 140:     setBackendError(normalizeContextError(caughtError));
 141:     throw caughtError;
 142:   }
 143: }, []);
 144:   const refreshProjects = useCallback(
 145:     async ({ includeArchived = true, preserveCurrentRecords = false } = {}) => {
 146:       setIsLoading(true);
 147:       setLoadStatus("loading");
 148: 
 149:       try {
 150:         const records = await fetchProjects();
 151: 
 152:         if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
 153:           throw new TypeError(
 154:             "The Project API did not return a Project array."
 155:           );
 156:         }
 157: 
 158:         const filteredRecords = includeArchived
 159:           ? records
 160:           : records.filter(
 161:               (project) => project.recordStatus !== "archived"
 162:             );
 163: 
 164:         setProjects(filteredRecords);
 165:         setLoadStatus("success");
 166:         setError(null);
 167: 
 168:         return filteredRecords;
 169:       } catch (caughtError) {
 170:         const normalizedError = normalizeContextError(caughtError);
 171: 
 172:         if (!preserveCurrentRecords) {
 173:           setProjects([]);
 174:         }
 175: 
 176:         setLoadStatus("error");
 177:         setError(normalizedError);
 178: 
 179:         throw caughtError;
 180:       } finally {
 181:         setIsLoading(false);
 182:       }
 183:     },
 184:     [],
 185:   );
 186: 
 187:   /*
 188:    * =========================================
 189:    * Silent Local Synchronization
 190:    * =========================================
 191:    *
 192:    * Used after a successful mutation. This
 193:    * avoids showing the full loading state
 194:    * every time a Project is saved.
 195:    */
 196: 
 197:   const synchronizeProjects = useCallback(async () => {
 198:     const records = await fetchProjects();
 199: 
 200:     if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
 201:       throw new TypeError(
 202:         "The Project API did not return a Project array."
 203:       );
 204:     }
 205: 
 206:     setProjects(records);
 207:     setLoadStatus("success");
 208: 
 209:     return records;
 210:   }, []);
 211: 
 212:   /*
 213:    * =========================================
 214:    * Initial Load
 215:    * =========================================
 216:    */
 217: 
 218:   useEffect(() => {
 219:     refreshProjects().catch(() => {
 220:       /*
 221:        * The normalized loading error is already
 222:        * stored in the Context.
 223:        */
 224:     });
 225:   }, [refreshProjects]);
 226: 
 227:   /*
 228:    * =========================================
 229:    * Cross-Tab Synchronization
 230:    * =========================================
 231:    */
 232: 
 233:   useEffect(() => {
 234:     if (typeof window === "undefined") {
 235:       return undefined;
 236:     }
 237: 
 238:     const handleStorageChange = (event) => {
 239:       if (
 240:         event.storageArea !== window.localStorage ||
 241:         event.key !== PROJECT_STORAGE_KEY
 242:       ) {
 243:         return;
 244:       }
 245: 
 246:       refreshProjects({
 247:         includeArchived: true,
 248:         preserveCurrentRecords: true,
 249:       }).catch(() => {
 250:         /*
 251:          * Keep the current in-memory collection
 252:          * when another tab writes invalid data.
 253:          */
 254:       });
 255:     };
 256: 
 257:     window.addEventListener("storage", handleStorageChange);
 258: 
 259:     return () => {
 260:       window.removeEventListener("storage", handleStorageChange);
 261:     };
 262:   }, [refreshProjects]);
 263: 
 264:   /*
 265:    * =========================================
 266:    * Shared Operation Runner
 267:    * =========================================
 268:    */
 269: 
 270:   const runOperation = useCallback(
 271:     async (nextOperation, action) => {
 272:       setOperation({
 273:         ...nextOperation,
 274:         status: "loading",
 275:       });
 276: 
 277:       setSaveStatus("saving");
 278:       setError(null);
 279:       setWarnings([]);
 280: 
 281:       try {
 282:         const result = await action();
 283: 
 284:         /*
 285:          * The service has already completed the
 286:          * Project metadata and IndexedDB asset
 287:          * transaction at this point.
 288:          */
 289: 
 290:         await synchronizeProjects();
 291: 
 292:         const operationWarnings = normalizeOperationWarnings(result);
 293: 
 294:         setWarnings(operationWarnings);
 295:         setSaveStatus("success");
 296: 
 297:         setOperation({
 298:           ...nextOperation,
 299:           status: "success",
 300:         });
 301: 
 302:         /*
 303:          * Create and update services return:
 304:          *
 305:          * {
 306:          *   project,
 307:          *   warnings,
 308:          *   assetCleanupWarning
 309:          * }
 310:          *
 311:          * Archive, restore, and delete may
 312:          * return the Project directly.
 313:          */
 314: 
 315:         return result?.project || result;
 316:       } catch (caughtError) {
 317:         const normalizedError = normalizeContextError(caughtError);
 318: 
 319:         setError(normalizedError);
 320:         setWarnings(normalizedError.warnings);
 321:         setSaveStatus("error");
 322: 
 323:         setOperation({
 324:           ...nextOperation,
 325:           status: "error",
 326:         });
 327: 
 328:         throw caughtError;
 329:       }
 330:     },
 331:     [synchronizeProjects],
 332:   );
 333: 
 334:   /*
 335:    * =========================================
 336:    * Create Project
 337:    * =========================================
 338:    */
 339: 
 340:   const createProject = useCallback(
 341:     (projectData, options = {}) =>
 342:       runOperation(
 343:         {
 344:           type: "create",
 345:           projectId: projectData?.id || "",
 346:         },
 347:         () => createProjectService(projectData, options),
 348:       ),
 349:     [runOperation],
 350:   );
 351: 
 352:   /*
 353:    * =========================================
 354:    * Update Project
 355:    * =========================================
 356:    */
 357: 
 358:   const updateProject = useCallback(
 359:     (projectId, projectData, options = {}) =>
 360:       runOperation(
 361:         {
 362:           type: "update",
 363:           projectId,
 364:         },
 365:         () => updateProjectService(projectId, projectData, options),
 366:       ),
 367:     [runOperation],
 368:   );
 369: 
 370:   /*
 371:    * =========================================
 372:    * Archive Project
 373:    * =========================================
 374:    */
 375: 
 376:   const archiveProject = useCallback(
 377:     (projectOrId) => {
 378:       const projectId =
 379:         typeof projectOrId === "string" ? projectOrId : projectOrId?.id;
 380: 
 381:       return runOperation(
 382:         {
 383:           type: "archive",
 384:           projectId,
 385:         },
 386:         () => archiveProjectService(projectId),
 387:       );
 388:     },
 389:     [runOperation],
 390:   );
 391: 
 392:   /*
 393:    * =========================================
 394:    * Restore Project
 395:    * =========================================
 396:    */
 397: 
 398:   const restoreProject = useCallback(
 399:     (projectOrId) => {
 400:       const projectId =
 401:         typeof projectOrId === "string" ? projectOrId : projectOrId?.id;
 402: 
 403:       return runOperation(
 404:         {
 405:           type: "restore",
 406:           projectId,
 407:         },
 408:         () => restoreProjectService(projectId),
 409:       );
 410:     },
 411:     [runOperation],
 412:   );
 413: 
 414:   /*
 415:    * =========================================
 416:    * Delete Project
 417:    * =========================================
 418:    */
 419: 
 420:   const deleteProject = useCallback(
 421:     (projectOrId) => {
 422:       const projectId =
 423:         typeof projectOrId === "string" ? projectOrId : projectOrId?.id;
 424: 
 425:       return runOperation(
 426:         {
 427:           type: "delete",
 428:           projectId,
 429:         },
 430:         () => deleteProjectService(projectId),
 431:       );
 432:     },
 433:     [runOperation],
 434:   );
 435: 
 436:   /*
 437:    * =========================================
 438:    * Utility Actions
 439:    * =========================================
 440:    */
 441: 
 442:   const getProjectById = useCallback(
 443:     (projectId, { includeArchived = true } = {}) => {
 444:       const project =
 445:         projects.find((record) => record.id === projectId) || null;
 446: 
 447:       if (project?.recordStatus === "archived" && !includeArchived) {
 448:         return null;
 449:       }
 450: 
 451:       return project;
 452:     },
 453:     [projects],
 454:   );
 455: 
 456:   const clearError = useCallback(() => {
 457:     setError(null);
 458:   }, []);
 459: 
 460:   const clearWarnings = useCallback(() => {
 461:     setWarnings([]);
 462:   }, []);
 463: 
 464:   const resetSaveStatus = useCallback(() => {
 465:     setSaveStatus("idle");
 466:     setOperation(null);
 467:   }, []);
 468: 
 469:   /*
 470:    * =========================================
 471:    * Derived Project Collections
 472:    * =========================================
 473:    */
 474: 
 475:   const activeProjects = useMemo(
 476:     () => projects.filter((project) => project.recordStatus !== "archived"),
 477:     [projects],
 478:   );
 479: 
 480:   const archivedProjects = useMemo(
 481:     () => projects.filter((project) => project.recordStatus === "archived"),
 482:     [projects],
 483:   );
 484: 
 485:   /*
 486:    * =========================================
 487:    * Context Value
 488:    * =========================================
 489:    */
 490: 
 491:   const value = useMemo(
 492:     () => ({
 493:       projects,
 494:       activeProjects,
 495:       archivedProjects,
 496: 
 497:       isLoading,
 498:       isSaving: saveStatus === "saving",
 499: 
 500:       loadStatus,
 501:       saveStatus,
 502:       operation,
 503: 
 504:       error,
 505:       warnings,
 506: 
 507:        backendStatus,
 508:     backendError,
 509: 
 510:       refreshProjects,
 511:       refreshBackendProjects,
 512:       getProjectById,
 513: 
 514:       createProject,
 515:       updateProject,
 516:       archiveProject,
 517:       restoreProject,
 518:       deleteProject,
 519: 
 520:       clearError,
 521:       clearWarnings,
 522:       resetSaveStatus,
 523:     }),
 524:     [
 525:       projects,
 526:       activeProjects,
 527:       archivedProjects,
 528:       isLoading,
 529:       loadStatus,
 530:       saveStatus,
 531:       operation,
 532:       error,
 533:       warnings,
 534:        backendStatus,
 535:     backendError,
 536:       refreshProjects,
 537:       refreshBackendProjects,
 538:       getProjectById,
 539:       createProject,
 540:       updateProject,
 541:       archiveProject,
 542:       restoreProject,
 543:       deleteProject,
 544:       clearError,
 545:       clearWarnings,
 546:       resetSaveStatus,
 547:     ],
 548:   );
 549: 
 550:   return (
 551:     <ProjectDataContext.Provider value={value}>
 552:       {children}
 553:     </ProjectDataContext.Provider>
 554:   );
 555: }
 556: 
 557: /*
 558:  * =========================================
 559:  * Project Data Hook
 560:  * =========================================
 561:  */
 562: 
 563: export function useProjectData() {
 564:   const context = useContext(ProjectDataContext);
 565: 
 566:   if (!context) {
 567:     throw new Error("useProjectData must be used inside ProjectDataProvider.");
 568:   }
 569: 
 570:   return context;
 571: }
 572: 
 573: export default ProjectDataContext;
 574:

   1: import {
   2:   EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS,
   3:   isValidEmploymentType,
   4:   isValidExperienceCategory,
   5:   isValidExperienceStatus,
   6:   isValidWorkArrangement,
   7: } from "../config/experienceConfig.js";
   8: 
   9: /*
  10:  * =========================================
  11:  * Model Version
  12:  * =========================================
  13:  *
  14:  * This allows saved browser data and future
  15:  * database records to be migrated safely.
  16:  */
  17: 
  18: export const EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION = 2;
  19: 
  20: /*
  21:  * =========================================
  22:  * ID Creation
  23:  * =========================================
  24:  */
  25: 
  26: export function createExperienceId(prefix = "experience") {
  27:   if (
  28:     typeof crypto !== "undefined" &&
  29:     typeof crypto.randomUUID === "function"
  30:   ) {
  31:     return `${prefix}-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
  32:   }
  33: 
  34:   return `${prefix}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
  35: }
  36: 
  37: /*
  38:  * =========================================
  39:  * Primitive Normalization
  40:  * =========================================
  41:  */
  42: 
  43: function normalizeText(value, maximumLength = Infinity) {
  44:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  45:     return "";
  46:   }
  47: 
  48:   return value.trim().slice(0, maximumLength);
  49: }
  50: 
  51: function normalizeBoolean(value, defaultValue = false) {
  52:   return typeof value === "boolean" ? value : defaultValue;
  53: }
  54: 
  55: function normalizeNullableNumber(value) {
  56:   if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined) {
  57:     return null;
  58:   }
  59: 
  60:   const numberValue = Number(value);
  61: 
  62:   if (!Number.isFinite(numberValue)) {
  63:     return null;
  64:   }
  65: 
  66:   return Math.max(0, Math.trunc(numberValue));
  67: }
  68: 
  69: function normalizeDate(value) {
  70:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
  71:     return "";
  72:   }
  73: 
  74:   const trimmedValue = value.trim();
  75: 
  76:   if (!trimmedValue) {
  77:     return "";
  78:   }
  79: 
  80:   /*
  81:    * The form uses YYYY-MM values, but this also
  82:    * permits YYYY-MM-DD records imported later.
  83:    */
  84: 
  85:   if (
  86:     !/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))?$/.test(trimmedValue)
  87:   ) {
  88:     return "";
  89:   }
  90: 
  91:   return trimmedValue;
  92: }
  93: 
  94: function normalizeIdentifierList(values, maximumItems) {
  95:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
  96:     return [];
  97:   }
  98: 
  99:   return [
 100:     ...new Set(
 101:       values
 102:         .map((value) => {
 103:           if (typeof value === "string") {
 104:             return value.trim();
 105:           }
 106: 
 107:           return String(value?.id || "").trim();
 108:         })
 109:         .filter(Boolean),
 110:     ),
 111:   ].slice(0, maximumItems);
 112: }
 113: 
 114: /*
 115:  * =========================================
 116:  * Responsibility Model
 117:  * =========================================
 118:  */
 119: 
 120: export function createResponsibility(value = {}) {
 121:   const source =
 122:     typeof value === "string"
 123:       ? {
 124:           text: value,
 125:         }
 126:       : value || {};
 127: 
 128:   return {
 129:     id: normalizeText(source.id, 200) || createExperienceId("responsibility"),
 130: 
 131:     text: normalizeText(
 132:       source.text || source.description,
 133:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.responsibilityText,
 134:     ),
 135: 
 136:     order:
 137:       Number.isInteger(source.order) && source.order >= 0 ? source.order : 0,
 138:   };
 139: }
 140: 
 141: function normalizeResponsibilities(values) {
 142:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
 143:     return [];
 144:   }
 145: 
 146:   return values
 147:     .map(createResponsibility)
 148:     .filter((responsibility) => responsibility.text)
 149:     .slice(0, EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumResponsibilities)
 150:     .map((responsibility, index) => ({
 151:       ...responsibility,
 152:       order: index,
 153:     }));
 154: }
 155: 
 156: /*
 157:  * =========================================
 158:  * Achievement Model
 159:  * =========================================
 160:  */
 161: 
 162: export function createAchievement(value = {}) {
 163:   const source =
 164:     typeof value === "string"
 165:       ? {
 166:           text: value,
 167:         }
 168:       : value || {};
 169: 
 170:   return {
 171:     id: normalizeText(source.id, 200) || createExperienceId("achievement"),
 172: 
 173:     text: normalizeText(
 174:       source.text || source.description || source.value,
 175:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.achievementText,
 176:     ),
 177: 
 178:     metric: normalizeText(
 179:       source.metric,
 180:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.achievementMetric,
 181:     ),
 182: 
 183:     order:
 184:       Number.isInteger(source.order) && source.order >= 0 ? source.order : 0,
 185:   };
 186: }
 187: 
 188: function normalizeAchievements(values) {
 189:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
 190:     return [];
 191:   }
 192: 
 193:   return values
 194:     .map(createAchievement)
 195:     .filter((achievement) => achievement.text)
 196:     .slice(0, EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumAchievements)
 197:     .map((achievement, index) => ({
 198:       ...achievement,
 199:       order: index,
 200:     }));
 201: }
 202: /*
 203:  * =========================================
 204:  * Experience Skill Model
 205:  * =========================================
 206:  */
 207: 
 208: export function createExperienceSkill(value = {}) {
 209:   const source =
 210:     typeof value === "string"
 211:       ? {
 212:           nameSnapshot: value,
 213:         }
 214:       : value && typeof value === "object"
 215:         ? value
 216:         : {};
 217: 
 218:   const sourceProficiency =
 219:     source.proficiency && typeof source.proficiency === "object"
 220:       ? source.proficiency.level
 221:       : source.proficiency;
 222: 
 223:   return {
 224:     id: normalizeText(source.id, 200) || createExperienceId("experience-skill"),
 225: 
 226:     /*
 227:      * Permanent relationship to the central
 228:      * Skill Library.
 229:      */
 230: 
 231:     skillId: normalizeText(source.skillId || source.profileSkillId, 200),
 232: 
 233:     /*
 234:      * The snapshot keeps the Experience readable
 235:      * if the central Skill record is renamed,
 236:      * archived, deleted, or temporarily unavailable.
 237:      */
 238: 
 239:     nameSnapshot: normalizeText(
 240:       source.nameSnapshot || source.name || source.label || source.value,
 241:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillName,
 242:     ),
 243: 
 244:     /*
 245:      * Classification snapshots are optional.
 246:      * The current central Skill record remains the
 247:      * preferred source when it is available.
 248:      */
 249: 
 250:     categorySnapshot: normalizeText(
 251:       source.categorySnapshot || source.category,
 252:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillCategory,
 253:     ),
 254: 
 255:     typeSnapshot: normalizeText(
 256:       source.typeSnapshot || source.type,
 257:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillType,
 258:     ),
 259: 
 260:     /*
 261:      * Experience-specific proficiency can differ
 262:      * from the general Skill Library proficiency.
 263:      */
 264: 
 265:     level: normalizeText(
 266:       source.level || sourceProficiency,
 267:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillLevel,
 268:     ),
 269: 
 270:     /*
 271:      * Optional explanation of how the skill was
 272:      * applied specifically in this Experience.
 273:      */
 274: 
 275:     usageDescription: normalizeText(
 276:       source.usageDescription || source.description,
 277:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillUsageDescription,
 278:     ),
 279: 
 280:     order:
 281:       Number.isInteger(Number(source.order)) && Number(source.order) >= 0
 282:         ? Number(source.order)
 283:         : 0,
 284:   };
 285: }
 286: 
 287: function normalizeExperienceSkills(values) {
 288:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
 289:     return [];
 290:   }
 291: 
 292:   const normalizedSkills = values
 293:     .map(createExperienceSkill)
 294:     .filter((skill) => skill.skillId || skill.nameSnapshot);
 295: 
 296:   const seenIdentities = new Set();
 297: 
 298:   return normalizedSkills
 299:     .filter((skill) => {
 300:       const identity = skill.skillId
 301:         ? `id:${skill.skillId}`
 302:         : `name:${skill.nameSnapshot
 303:             .normalize("NFKC")
 304:             .toLocaleLowerCase()
 305:             .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
 306:             .trim()}`;
 307: 
 308:       if (seenIdentities.has(identity)) {
 309:         return false;
 310:       }
 311: 
 312:       seenIdentities.add(identity);
 313: 
 314:       return true;
 315:     })
 316:     .slice(0, EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumSkills)
 317:     .map((skill, index) => ({
 318:       ...skill,
 319:       order: index,
 320:     }));
 321: }
 322: 
 323: /*
 324:  * =========================================
 325:  * Experience Technology Model
 326:  * =========================================
 327:  */
 328: 
 329: export function createExperienceTechnology(value = {}) {
 330:   const source =
 331:     typeof value === "string"
 332:       ? {
 333:           nameSnapshot: value,
 334:         }
 335:       : value && typeof value === "object"
 336:         ? value
 337:         : {};
 338: 
 339:   const sourceProficiency =
 340:     source.proficiency && typeof source.proficiency === "object"
 341:       ? source.proficiency.level
 342:       : source.proficiency;
 343: 
 344:   const nameSnapshot = normalizeText(
 345:     source.nameSnapshot || source.name || source.label || source.value,
 346:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyName,
 347:   );
 348: 
 349:   return {
 350:     id:
 351:       normalizeText(source.id, 200) ||
 352:       createExperienceId("experience-technology"),
 353: 
 354:     /*
 355:      * Permanent relationship to the central
 356:      * Skill Library.
 357:      */
 358: 
 359:     skillId: normalizeText(source.skillId || source.profileSkillId, 200),
 360: 
 361:     /*
 362:      * Snapshot retained for historical display.
 363:      */
 364: 
 365:     nameSnapshot,
 366: 
 367:     /*
 368:      * Retained as a compatibility alias for older
 369:      * Experience display and validation code.
 370:      */
 371: 
 372:     name: nameSnapshot,
 373: 
 374:     /*
 375:      * Technology-specific relationship metadata.
 376:      */
 377: 
 378:     category: normalizeText(
 379:       source.categorySnapshot || source.category,
 380:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyCategory,
 381:     ),
 382: 
 383:     proficiency: normalizeText(
 384:       source.level || sourceProficiency,
 385:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyProficiency,
 386:     ),
 387: 
 388:     usageDescription: normalizeText(
 389:       source.usageDescription || source.description,
 390:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyUsageDescription,
 391:     ),
 392: 
 393:     order:
 394:       Number.isInteger(Number(source.order)) && Number(source.order) >= 0
 395:         ? Number(source.order)
 396:         : 0,
 397:   };
 398: }
 399: 
 400: function normalizeTechnologies(values) {
 401:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
 402:     return [];
 403:   }
 404: 
 405:   const normalizedTechnologies = values
 406:     .map(createExperienceTechnology)
 407:     .filter((technology) => technology.skillId || technology.nameSnapshot);
 408: 
 409:   const seenIdentities = new Set();
 410: 
 411:   return normalizedTechnologies
 412:     .filter((technology) => {
 413:       const normalizedName = technology.nameSnapshot
 414:         .normalize("NFKC")
 415:         .toLocaleLowerCase()
 416:         .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
 417:         .trim();
 418: 
 419:       const identity = technology.skillId
 420:         ? `id:${technology.skillId}`
 421:         : `name:${normalizedName}`;
 422: 
 423:       if (seenIdentities.has(identity)) {
 424:         return false;
 425:       }
 426: 
 427:       seenIdentities.add(identity);
 428: 
 429:       return true;
 430:     })
 431:     .slice(0, EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumTechnologies)
 432:     .map((technology, index) => ({
 433:       ...technology,
 434:       order: index,
 435:     }));
 436: }

 446: 
 447:   return {
 448:     modelVersion: EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION,
 449:

 451:

 456:       employmentType: "full-time",
 457:       workArrangement: "on-site",

 462:       website: "",
 463:       industry: "",
 464:     },
 465: 
 466:     location: {
 467:       city: "",
 468:       stateRegion: "",
 469:       country: "",
 470:       displayValue: "",
 471:     },
 472: 
 473:     dates: {
 474:       startDate: "",
 475:       endDate: "",
 476:       isCurrent: false,
 477:     },
 478: 
 479:     overview: "",
 480: 
 481:     overviewMeta: {
 482:       source: "manual",
 483:       status: "empty",
 484:       generatedAt: null,
 485:       contextFingerprint: "",
 486:       isStale: false,
 487:     },
 488: 
 489:     responsibilities: [],
 490:

 492: 
 493:     skills: [],

 507:       description: "",
 508:     },

 512:      * and should never be displayed automatically

 525:       showWorkArrangement: true,
 526:       showResponsibilities: true,
 527:       showAchievements: true,
 528:       showSkills: true,
 529:       showTechnologies: true,
 530:     },
 531: 
 532:     status: "active",
 533: 
 534:     createdAt: timestamp,
 535:     updatedAt: timestamp,
 536:   };
 537: }

 539: /*
 540:  * =========================================
 541:  * Experience Normalization
 542:  * =========================================
 543:  */
 544: 
 545: export function normalizeExperience(value = {}) {
 546:   const defaults = createEmptyExperience();
 547:   const source = value && typeof value === "object" ? value : {};
 548: 
 549:   const position =
 550:     source.position && typeof source.position === "object"
 551:       ? source.position
 552:       : {};
 553: 
 554:   const organization =
 555:     source.organization && typeof source.organization === "object"
 556:       ? source.organization
 557:       : {};
 558: 
 559:   const location =
 560:     source.location && typeof source.location === "object"
 561:       ? source.location
 562:       : {};
 563: 
 564:   const dates =
 565:     source.dates && typeof source.dates === "object" ? source.dates : {};
 566: 
 567:   const leadership =
 568:     source.leadership && typeof source.leadership === "object"
 569:       ? source.leadership
 570:       : {};
 571: 
 572:   const privateInformation =
 573:     source.privateInformation && typeof source.privateInformation === "object"
 574:       ? source.privateInformation
 575:       : {};
 576: 
 577:   const visibility =
 578:     source.visibility && typeof source.visibility === "object"
 579:       ? source.visibility
 580:       : {};
 581: 
 582:   const category = isValidExperienceCategory(source.category)
 583:     ? source.category
 584:     : defaults.category;
 585: 
 586:   const employmentType = isValidEmploymentType(position.employmentType)
 587:     ? position.employmentType
 588:     : defaults.position.employmentType;
 589: 
 590:   const workArrangement = isValidWorkArrangement(position.workArrangement)
 591:     ? position.workArrangement
 592:     : defaults.position.workArrangement;
 593: 
 594:   const status = isValidExperienceStatus(source.status)
 595:     ? source.status
 596:     : defaults.status;
 597: 
 598:   const isCurrent = normalizeBoolean(dates.isCurrent, false);
 599: 
 600:   const overviewMeta =
 601:     source.overviewMeta && typeof source.overviewMeta === "object"
 602:       ? source.overviewMeta
 603:       : {};
 604: 
 605:   return {
 606:     modelVersion: EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION,
 607: 
 608:     id: normalizeText(source.id, 200) || defaults.id,
 609: 
 610:     category,
 611: 
 612:     position: {
 613:       title: normalizeText(
 614:         position.title || source.positionTitle || source.title || source.role,
 615:         EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.positionTitle,
 616:       ),
 617: 
 618:       employmentType,
 619: 
 620:       workArrangement,
 621:     },
 622: 
 623:     organization: {
 624:       name: normalizeText(
 625:         organization.name ||
 626:           source.organizationName ||
 627:           source.company ||
 628:           source.employer,
 629:         EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.organizationName,
 630:       ),
 631: 
 632:       website: normalizeText(
 633:         organization.website || source.organizationWebsite,
 634:         EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.organizationWebsite,
 635:       ),
 636: 
 637:       industry: normalizeText(
 638:         organization.industry || source.industry,
 639:         EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.industry,
 640:       ),
 641:     },
 642: 
 643:     location: {
 644:       city: normalizeText(location.city, EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.city),
 645: 
 646:       stateRegion: normalizeText(
 647:         location.stateRegion || location.state,
 648:         EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.stateRegion,
 649:       ),
 650: 
 651:       country: normalizeText(location.country, EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.country),
 652: 
 653:       displayValue: normalizeText(
 654:         location.displayValue ||
 655:           source.locationValue ||
 656:           (typeof source.location === "string" ? source.location : ""),
 657:         EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.locationDisplayValue,
 658:       ),
 659:     },
 660: 
 661:     dates: {
 662:       startDate: normalizeDate(dates.startDate || source.startDate),
 663: 
 664:       endDate: isCurrent ? "" : normalizeDate(dates.endDate || source.endDate),
 665: 
 666:       isCurrent,
 667:     },
 668: 
 669:     overview: normalizeText(
 670:       source.overview || source.description,
 671:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.overview,
 672:     ),
 673: 
 674:     overviewMeta: {
 675:       source: overviewMeta.source === "ai" ? "ai" : "manual",
 676: 
 677:       status:
 678:         normalizeText(overviewMeta.status, 50) ||
 679:         (source.overview ? "draft" : "empty"),
 680: 
 681:       generatedAt: normalizeText(overviewMeta.generatedAt, 100),
 682: 
 683:       contextFingerprint: normalizeText(overviewMeta.contextFingerprint, 300),
 684: 
 685:       isStale: overviewMeta.isStale === true,
 686:     },
 687: 
 688:     responsibilities: normalizeResponsibilities(source.responsibilities),
 689: 
 690:     achievements: normalizeAchievements(
 691:       source.achievements || source.highlights,
 692:     ),
 693: 
 694:     skills: normalizeExperienceSkills(source.skills),
 695: 
 696:     technologies: normalizeTechnologies(source.technologies || source.tools),
 697: 
 698:     relatedProjectIds: normalizeIdentifierList(
 699:       source.relatedProjectIds || source.projectIds,
 700:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedProjects,
 701:     ),
 702: 
 703:     leadership: {
 704:       hasLeadershipResponsibilities: normalizeBoolean(
 705:         leadership.hasLeadershipResponsibilities,
 706:         false,
 707:       ),
 708: 
 709:       peopleManaged: normalizeNullableNumber(leadership.peopleManaged),
 710: 
 711:       description: normalizeText(
 712:         leadership.description,
 713:         EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.leadershipDescription,
 714:       ),
 715:     },
 716: 
 717:     privateInformation: {
 718:       reasonForLeaving: normalizeText(
 719:         privateInformation.reasonForLeaving || source.reasonForLeaving,
 720:         EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.reasonForLeaving,
 721:       ),
 722: 
 723:       notes: normalizeText(
 724:         privateInformation.notes || source.privateNotes,
 725:         EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.privateNotes,
 726:       ),
 727:     },
 728: 
 729:     visibility: {
 730:       showOrganizationWebsite: normalizeBoolean(
 731:         visibility.showOrganizationWebsite,
 732:         defaults.visibility.showOrganizationWebsite,
 733:       ),
 734: 
 735:       showLocation: normalizeBoolean(
 736:         visibility.showLocation,
 737:         defaults.visibility.showLocation,
 738:       ),
 739: 
 740:       showEmploymentType: normalizeBoolean(
 741:         visibility.showEmploymentType,
 742:         defaults.visibility.showEmploymentType,
 743:       ),
 744: 
 745:       showWorkArrangement: normalizeBoolean(
 746:         visibility.showWorkArrangement,
 747:         defaults.visibility.showWorkArrangement,
 748:       ),
 749: 
 750:       showResponsibilities: normalizeBoolean(
 751:         visibility.showResponsibilities,
 752:         defaults.visibility.showResponsibilities,
 753:       ),
 754: 
 755:       showAchievements: normalizeBoolean(
 756:         visibility.showAchievements,
 757:         defaults.visibility.showAchievements,
 758:       ),
 759: 
 760:       showSkills: normalizeBoolean(
 761:         visibility.showSkills,
 762:         defaults.visibility.showSkills,
 763:       ),
 764: 
 765:       showTechnologies: normalizeBoolean(
 766:         visibility.showTechnologies,
 767:         defaults.visibility.showTechnologies,
 768:       ),
 769:     },
 770: 
 771:     status,
 772: 
 773:     createdAt: normalizeText(source.createdAt, 100) || defaults.createdAt,
 774: 
 775:     updatedAt: normalizeText(source.updatedAt, 100) || defaults.updatedAt,
 776:   };
 777: }
 778: 
 779: /*
 780:  * =========================================
 781:  * New Experience
 782:  * =========================================
 783:  */
 784: 
 785: export function createExperience(values = {}) {
 786:   const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
 787: 
 788:   return normalizeExperience({
 789:     ...values,
 790: 
 791:     id: normalizeText(values.id, 200) || createExperienceId(),
 792: 
 793:     createdAt: normalizeText(values.createdAt, 100) || timestamp,
 794: 
 795:     updatedAt: timestamp,
 796:   });
 797: }

 801:  * Updated Experience

 803:  */
 804: 
 805: export function updateExperienceModel(currentExperience, updates = {}) {
 806:   return normalizeExperience({
 807:     ...currentExperience,
 808:     ...updates,
 809: 
 810:     position: {
 811:       ...currentExperience?.position,
 812:       ...updates.position,
 813:     },
 814: 
 815:     organization: {
 816:       ...currentExperience?.organization,
 817:       ...updates.organization,
 818:     },
 819: 
 820:     location: {
 821:       ...currentExperience?.location,
 822:       ...updates.location,
 823:     },
 824: 
 825:     dates: {
 826:       ...currentExperience?.dates,
 827:       ...updates.dates,
 828:     },
 829: 
 830:     overviewMeta: {
 831:       ...currentExperience?.overviewMeta,
 832:       ...updates?.overviewMeta,
 833:     },
 834: 
 835:     leadership: {
 836:       ...currentExperience?.leadership,
 837:       ...updates.leadership,
 838:     },
 839: 
 840:     privateInformation: {
 841:       ...currentExperience?.privateInformation,
 842:       ...updates.privateInformation,
 843:     },
 844: 
 845:     visibility: {
 846:       ...currentExperience?.visibility,
 847:       ...updates.visibility,
 848:     },
 849: 
 850:     id: currentExperience?.id,
 851: 
 852:     createdAt: currentExperience?.createdAt,
 853: 
 854:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 855:   });
 856: }
 857: 
 858: /*
 859:  * =========================================
 860:  * Collection Normalization
 861:  * =========================================
 862:  */
 863: 
 864: export function normalizeExperienceCollection(values) {
 865:   if (!Array.isArray(values)) {
 866:     return [];
 867:   }
 868: 
 869:   const normalizedExperiences = values.map(normalizeExperience);
 870: 
 871:   return normalizedExperiences.filter(
 872:     (experience, index, collection) =>
 873:       collection.findIndex((candidate) => candidate.id === experience.id) ===
 874:       index,
 875:   );
 876: }
 877: 
 878: /*
 879:  * =========================================
 880:  * Public Experience
 881:  * =========================================
 882:  *
 883:  * Removes private career-management information
 884:  * before an experience is used publicly.
 885:  */
 886: 
 887: export function createPublicExperience(value) {
 888:   const experience = normalizeExperience(value);
 889: 
 890:   const { privateInformation, ...publicExperience } = experience;
 891: 
 892:   return publicExperience;
 893: }

   1: import "./ExperienceCard.css";

   3: /*
   4:  * =========================================
   5:  * Display Helpers
   6:  * =========================================
   7:  */
   8: 
   9: function getText(value) {
  10:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
  11: }
  12: 
  13: function formatOptionValue(value) {
  14:   return getText(value)
  15:     .split("-")
  16:     .filter(Boolean)
  17:     .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + part.slice(1))
  18:     .join(" ");
  19: }
  20: 
  21: function formatMonthYear(value) {
  22:   if (!value || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) {
  23:     return "";
  24:   }
  25: 
  26:   const [year, month] = value.split("-").map(Number);
  27: 
  28:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, 1));
  29: 
  30:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
  31:     month: "short",
  32:     year: "numeric",
  33:     timeZone: "UTC",
  34:   }).format(date);
  35: }
  36: 
  37: function getDateRange(experience) {
  38:   const startDate = formatMonthYear(experience.dates?.startDate);
  39: 
  40:   const endDate = experience.dates?.isCurrent
  41:     ? "Present"
  42:     : formatMonthYear(experience.dates?.endDate);
  43: 
  44:   if (startDate && endDate) {
  45:     return `${startDate} â€“ ${endDate}`;
  46:   }
  47: 
  48:   return startDate || endDate || "Dates not provided";
  49: }
  50: 
  51: function getLocation(experience) {
  52:   const location = experience.location || {};
  53: 
  54:   return [location.city, location.state, location.country]
  55:     .map(getText)
  56:     .filter(Boolean)
  57:     .join(", ");
  58: }
  59: 
  60: function getResponsibilityText(responsibility) {
  61:   if (typeof responsibility === "string") {
  62:     return responsibility.trim();
  63:   }
  64: 
  65:   return getText(responsibility?.text);
  66: }
  67: 
  68: function getAchievementInformation(achievement) {
  69:   if (typeof achievement === "string") {
  70:     return {
  71:       text: achievement.trim(),
  72:       metric: "",
  73:     };
  74:   }
  75: 
  76:   return {
  77:     text: getText(achievement?.text),
  78:     metric: getText(achievement?.metric || achievement?.measurableResult),
  79:   };
  80: }
  81: 
  82: function getSkillName(skill) {
  83:   if (typeof skill === "string") {
  84:     return skill.trim();
  85:   }
  86: 
  87:   return getText(skill?.nameSnapshot || skill?.name || skill?.label);
  88: }
  89: 
  90: function getTechnologyName(technology) {
  91:   if (typeof technology === "string") {
  92:     return technology.trim();
  93:   }
  94: 
  95:   return getText(
  96:     technology?.nameSnapshot ||
  97:       technology?.name ||
  98:       technology?.label ||
  99:       technology?.value,
 100:   );
 101: }

 114: 
 115:         <h4>{title}</h4>

 129: function ExperienceCard({
 130:   experience,
 131:   isExpanded = false,
 132:   isWorking = false,
 133:   onToggleView,
 134:   onEdit,
 135:   onArchive,
 136:   onRestore,
 137:   onDelete,

 140:     getText(experience.position?.title) || "Untitled Position";

 143:     getText(experience.organization?.name) || "Organization not provided";

 156:     .map(getTechnologyName)

 158:

 160:

 162: 
 163:   const location = getLocation(experience);
 164:

 166:

 168:

 170:     experience.position?.workArrangement,
 171:   );
 172: 
 173:   return (
 174:     <article
 175:       className={`experience-card ${
 176:         isExpanded ? "experience-card--expanded" : ""
 177:       } ${isArchived ? "experience-card--archived" : ""}`}
 178:     >
 179:       <header className="experience-card-summary">
 180:         <div className="experience-card-marker">
 181:           <span aria-hidden="true">{isArchived ? "â–¡" : "âœ¦"}</span>
 182:         </div>
 183: 
 184:         <div className="experience-card-heading">
 185:           <div className="experience-card-status-row">
 186:             <span
 187:               className={`experience-card-status ${
 188:                 experience.dates?.isCurrent
 189:                   ? "experience-card-status--current"
 190:                   : ""
 191:               }`}
 192:             >
 193:               {isArchived
 194:                 ? "Archived"
 195:                 : experience.dates?.isCurrent
 196:                   ? "Current Position"
 197:                   : "Previous Position"}
 198:             </span>
 199: 
 200:             {experience.visibility?.showOnResume && (
 201:               <span className="experience-card-usage-badge">RÃ©sumÃ©</span>
 202:             )}
 203: 
 204:             {experience.visibility?.showOnPortfolio && (
 205:               <span className="experience-card-usage-badge">Portfolio</span>
 206:             )}
 207:           </div>
 208: 
 209:           <h3>{positionTitle}</h3>
 210: 
 211:           <p>{organizationName}</p>
 212: 
 213:           <div className="experience-card-primary-meta">
 214:             <span>{getDateRange(experience)}</span>
 215: 
 216:             {location && <span>{location}</span>}
 217: 
 218:             {employmentType && <span>{employmentType}</span>}
 219: 
 220:             {workArrangement && <span>{workArrangement}</span>}
 221:           </div>
 222:         </div>
 223: 
 224:         <div className="experience-card-actions">
 225:           <button
 226:             type="button"
 227:             className="experience-card-view-button"
 228:             onClick={() => onToggleView?.(experience.id)}
 229:             disabled={isWorking}
 230:             aria-expanded={isExpanded}
 231:           >
 232:             {isExpanded ? "Hide Details" : "View"}
 233:           </button>
 234: 
 235:           {!isArchived && (
 236:             <button
 237:               type="button"
 238:               onClick={() => onEdit?.(experience)}
 239:               disabled={isWorking}
 240:             >
 241:               Edit
 242:             </button>
 243:           )}
 244: 
 245:           {isArchived ? (
 246:             <button
 247:               type="button"
 248:               onClick={() => onRestore?.(experience)}
 249:               disabled={isWorking}
 250:             >
 251:               Restore
 252:             </button>
 253:           ) : (
 254:             <button
 255:               type="button"
 256:               onClick={() => onArchive?.(experience)}
 257:               disabled={isWorking}
 258:             >
 259:               Archive
 260:             </button>
 261:           )}
 262: 
 263:           <button
 264:             type="button"
 265:             className="experience-card-delete-button"
 266:             onClick={() => onDelete?.(experience)}
 267:             disabled={isWorking}
 268:           >
 269:             Delete
 270:           </button>
 271:         </div>
 272:       </header>
 273: 
 274:       {isWorking && (
 275:         <div className="experience-card-operation" role="status">
 276:           <span aria-hidden="true" />
 277: 
 278:           <small>Updating experience...</small>
 279:         </div>
 280:       )}
 281: 
 282:       {isExpanded && (
 283:         <div className="experience-card-details">
 284:           {overview && (
 285:             <ExperienceDetailGroup
 286:               eyebrow="Professional Focus"
 287:               title="Experience Overview"
 288:               className="experience-card-detail-group--full"
 289:             >
 290:               <p className="experience-card-overview">{overview}</p>
 291: 
 292:               {experience.overviewMeta?.source === "ai" && (
 293:                 <span className="experience-card-ai-label">AI assisted</span>
 294:               )}
 295:             </ExperienceDetailGroup>
 296:           )}
 297: 
 298:           {responsibilities.length > 0 && (
 299:             <ExperienceDetailGroup
 300:               eyebrow="Primary Work"
 301:               title="Responsibilities"
 302:             >
 303:               <ul>
 304:                 {responsibilities.map((responsibility, index) => (
 305:                   <li key={`${responsibility}-${index}`}>{responsibility}</li>
 306:                 ))}
 307:               </ul>
 308:             </ExperienceDetailGroup>
 309:           )}
 310: 
 311:           {achievements.length > 0 && (
 312:             <ExperienceDetailGroup
 313:               eyebrow="Professional Impact"
 314:               title="Achievements and Results"
 315:             >
 316:               <ul>
 317:                 {achievements.map((achievement, index) => (
 318:                   <li key={`${achievement.text}-${index}`}>
 319:                     <span>{achievement.text}</span>
 320: 
 321:                     {achievement.metric && (
 322:                       <strong>{achievement.metric}</strong>
 323:                     )}
 324:                   </li>
 325:                 ))}
 326:               </ul>
 327:             </ExperienceDetailGroup>
 328:           )}
 329: 
 330:           {skills.length > 0 && (
 331:             <ExperienceDetailGroup eyebrow="Capabilities" title="Skills">
 332:               <div className="experience-card-tags">
 333:                 {skills.map((skill, index) => (
 334:                   <span key={`${skill}-${index}`}>{skill}</span>
 335:                 ))}
 336:               </div>
 337:             </ExperienceDetailGroup>
 338:           )}
 339: 
 340:           {technologies.length > 0 && (
 341:             <ExperienceDetailGroup
 342:               eyebrow="Tools and Systems"
 343:               title="Technologies"
 344:             >
 345:               <div className="experience-card-tags">
 346:                 {technologies.map((technology, index) => (
 347:                   <span key={`${technology}-${index}`}>{technology}</span>
 348:                 ))}
 349:               </div>
 350:             </ExperienceDetailGroup>
 351:           )}
 352: 
 353:           {leadershipDescription && (
 354:             <ExperienceDetailGroup
 355:               eyebrow="Collaboration"
 356:               title="Leadership"
 357:               className="experience-card-detail-group--full"
 358:             >
 359:               <p>{leadershipDescription}</p>
 360: 
 361:               {experience.leadership?.peopleManaged !== null &&
 362:                 experience.leadership?.peopleManaged !== undefined &&
 363:                 experience.leadership?.peopleManaged !== "" && (
 364:                   <small>
 365:                     People managed: {experience.leadership.peopleManaged}
 366:                   </small>
 367:                 )}
 368:             </ExperienceDetailGroup>
 369:           )}
 370: 
 371:           {!overview &&
 372:             responsibilities.length === 0 &&
 373:             achievements.length === 0 &&
 374:             skills.length === 0 &&
 375:             technologies.length === 0 &&
 376:             !leadershipDescription && (
 377:               <div className="experience-card-no-details">
 378:                 <p>
 379:                   This experience does not contain additional display
 380:                   information.
 381:                 </p>
 382:               </div>
 383:             )}
 384: 
 385:           <footer className="experience-card-details-footer">
 386:             <button
 387:               type="button"
 388:               onClick={() => onToggleView?.(experience.id)}
 389:               disabled={isWorking}
 390:             >
 391:               Hide Details
 392:             </button>
 393: 
 394:             {!isArchived && (
 395:               <button
 396:                 type="button"
 397:                 className="experience-card-details-edit"
 398:                 onClick={() => onEdit?.(experience)}
 399:                 disabled={isWorking}
 400:               >
 401:                 Edit This Experience
 402:               </button>
 403:             )}
 404:           </footer>
 405:         </div>
 406:       )}
 407:     </article>
 408:   );
 409: }
 410: 
 411: export default ExperienceCard;
