FILE: src\pages\Experience\components\ExperienceForm\ExperienceForm.jsx
===========================================================================
   1: import { useEffect, useId, useState } from "react";
   2: 
   3: import {
   4:   EMPLOYMENT_TYPE_OPTIONS,
   5:   EXPERIENCE_CATEGORY_OPTIONS,
   6:   EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS,
   7:   EXPERIENCE_STATUS_OPTIONS,
   8:   WORK_ARRANGEMENT_OPTIONS,
   9: } from "../../../../config/experienceConfig.js";
  10: 
  11: import {
  12:   createEmptyExperience,
  13:   normalizeExperience,
  14: } from "../../../../models/experienceModel.js";
  15: 
  16: import { validateExperience } from "../../../../services/Experience/experienceValidation.js";
  17: 
  18: import ResponsibilityEditor from "../ResponsibilityEditor/ResponsibilityEditor.jsx";
  19: import AchievementEditor from "../AchievementEditor/AchievementEditor.jsx";
  20: import ExperienceSkillsEditor from "../ExperienceSkillsEditor/ExperienceSkillsEditor.jsx";
  21: import ExperienceOverviewBuilder from "../ExperienceOverviewBuilder/ExperienceOverviewBuilder.jsx";
  22: import ExperienceTechnologyEditor from "./ExperienceTechnologyEditor/ExperienceTechnologyEditor.jsx";
  23: 
  24: import "./ExperienceForm.css";
  25: 
  26: /*
  27:  * =========================================
  28:  * Form Data Creation
  29:  * =========================================
  30:  */
  31: 
  32: function createFormData(initialExperience) {
  33:   return initialExperience
  34:     ? normalizeExperience(initialExperience)
  35:     : createEmptyExperience();
  36: }
  37: 
  38: /*
  39:  * =========================================
  40:  * Experience Form
  41:  * =========================================
  42:  */
  43: 
  44: function ExperienceForm({
  45:   initialExperience = null,
  46:   isSaving = false,
  47:   onSubmit,
  48:   onCancel,
  49: }) {
  50:   const formId = useId();
  51: 
  52:   const [formData, setFormData] = useState(() =>
  53:     createFormData(initialExperience),
  54:   );
  55: 
  56:   const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState({});
  57: 
  58:   const [warnings, setWarnings] = useState([]);
  59: 
  60:   const [submissionError, setSubmissionError] = useState("");
  61: 
  62:   const isEditing = Boolean(initialExperience?.id);
  63: 
  64:   /*
  65:    * =========================================
  66:    * Synchronize Editing Record
  67:    * =========================================
  68:    */
  69: 
  70:   useEffect(() => {
  71:     setFormData(createFormData(initialExperience));
  72: 
  73:     setFieldErrors({});
  74:     setWarnings([]);
  75:     setSubmissionError("");
  76:   }, [initialExperience]);
  77: 
  78:   /*
  79:    * =========================================
  80:    * Error Management
  81:    * =========================================
  82:    */
  83: 
  84:   const clearFieldError = (fieldName) => {
  85:     setFieldErrors((currentErrors) => {
  86:       if (!currentErrors[fieldName]) {
  87:         return currentErrors;
  88:       }
  89: 
  90:       const nextErrors = {
  91:         ...currentErrors,
  92:       };
  93: 
  94:       delete nextErrors[fieldName];
  95: 
  96:       return nextErrors;
  97:     });
  98: 
  99:     setSubmissionError("");
 100:   };
 101: 
 102:   /*
 103:    * =========================================
 104:    * Top-Level Field Update
 105:    * =========================================
 106:    */
 107: 
 108:   const updateTopLevelField = (fieldName, value) => {
 109:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
 110:       ...currentData,
 111:       [fieldName]: value,
 112:     }));
 113: 
 114:     clearFieldError(fieldName);
 115:   };
 116: 
 117:   /*
 118:    * =========================================
 119:    * Nested Field Update
 120:    * =========================================
 121:    */
 122: 
 123:   const updateNestedField = (sectionName, fieldName, value) => {
 124:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
 125:       ...currentData,
 126: 
 127:       [sectionName]: {
 128:         ...currentData[sectionName],
 129:         [fieldName]: value,
 130:       },
 131:     }));
 132: 
 133:     clearFieldError(`${sectionName}.${fieldName}`);
 134:   };
 135: 
 136:   /*
 137:    * =========================================
 138:    * Current Position
 139:    * =========================================
 140:    */
 141: 
 142:   const handleCurrentPositionChange = (event) => {
 143:     const { checked } = event.target;
 144: 
 145:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
 146:       ...currentData,
 147: 
 148:       dates: {
 149:         ...currentData.dates,
 150:         isCurrent: checked,
 151: 
 152:         /*
 153:          * A current position cannot have an
 154:          * end date.
 155:          */
 156: 
 157:         endDate: checked ? "" : currentData.dates.endDate,
 158:       },
 159:     }));
 160: 
 161:     clearFieldError("dates.isCurrent");
 162:     clearFieldError("dates.endDate");
 163:   };
 164: 
 165:   /*
 166:    * =========================================
 167:    * Leadership Toggle
 168:    * =========================================
 169:    */
 170: 
 171:   const handleLeadershipChange = (event) => {
 172:     const { checked } = event.target;
 173: 
 174:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
 175:       ...currentData,
 176: 
 177:       leadership: {
 178:         ...currentData.leadership,
 179: 
 180:         hasLeadershipResponsibilities: checked,
 181:       },
 182:     }));
 183: 
 184:     clearFieldError("leadership.hasLeadershipResponsibilities");
 185:   };
 186: 
 187:   /*
 188:    * =========================================
 189:    * Collection Update
 190:    * =========================================
 191:    */
 192: 
 193:   const updateCollectionField = (fieldName, values) => {
 194:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
 195:       ...currentData,
 196:       [fieldName]: values,
 197:     }));
 198: 
 199:     setFieldErrors((currentErrors) => {
 200:       const nextErrors = {};
 201: 
 202:       Object.entries(currentErrors).forEach(([errorField, message]) => {
 203:         if (
 204:           errorField !== fieldName &&
 205:           !errorField.startsWith(`${fieldName}.`)
 206:         ) {
 207:           nextErrors[errorField] = message;
 208:         }
 209:       });
 210: 
 211:       return nextErrors;
 212:     });
 213: 
 214:     setSubmissionError("");
 215:   };
 216: 
 217:   /*
 218:    * =========================================
 219:    * Form Submission
 220:    * =========================================
 221:    */
 222: 
 223:   const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
 224:     event.preventDefault();
 225: 
 226:     setSubmissionError("");
 227: 
 228:     const validationResult = validateExperience(formData);
 229: 
 230:     setFieldErrors(validationResult.fieldErrors);
 231: 
 232:     setWarnings(validationResult.warnings);
 233: 
 234:     if (!validationResult.isValid) {
 235:       setSubmissionError(
 236:         `${validationResult.errors.length} ${
 237:           validationResult.errors.length === 1
 238:             ? "problem must"
 239:             : "problems must"
 240:         } be corrected before saving.`,
 241:       );
 242: 
 243:       return;
 244:     }
 245: 
 246:     try {
 247:       await onSubmit?.(formData);
 248:     } catch (error) {
 249:       const serviceFieldErrors =
 250:         error?.fieldErrors || error?.validation?.fieldErrors || {};
 251: 
 252:       setFieldErrors(serviceFieldErrors);
 253: 
 254:       setSubmissionError(
 255:         error?.publicMessage ||
 256:           error?.message ||
 257:           "The experience could not be saved.",
 258:       );
 259:     }
 260:   };
 261: 
 262:   /*
 263:    * =========================================
 264:    * Form IDs
 265:    * =========================================
 266:    */
 267: 
 268:   const getFieldId = (fieldName) => `${formId}-${fieldName}`;
 269: 
 270:   const handleOverviewUpdate = ({ overview, overviewMeta }) => {
 271:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
 272:       ...currentData,
 273:       overview,
 274:       overviewMeta,
 275:     }));
 276: 
 277:     clearFieldError("overview");
 278:     setSubmissionError("");
 279:   };
 280: 
 281:   /*
 282:    * =========================================
 283:    * Technologies Update
 284:    * =========================================
 285:    */
 286: 
 287:   const handleTechnologiesChange = (nextTechnologies) => {
 288:     setFormData((currentData) => ({
 289:       ...currentData,
 290: 
 291:       technologies: nextTechnologies,
 292:     }));
 293: 
 294:     clearFieldError("technologies");
 295:     setSubmissionError("");
 296:   };
 297: 
 298:   return (
 299:     <form className="experience-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
 300:       {/* =====================================
 301:           Role and Organization
 302:           ===================================== */}
 303: 
 304:       <section
 305:         className="experience-form-section"
 306:         aria-labelledby={`${formId}-position-section-title`}
 307:       >
 308:         <header className="experience-form-section-header">
 309:           <span aria-hidden="true" />
 310: 
 311:           <div>
 312:             <small>Position</small>
 313: 
 314:             <h3 id={`${formId}-position-section-title`}>
 315:               Role and Organization
 316:             </h3>
 317: 
 318:             <p>
 319:               Identify the role, organization, experience category, and
 320:               employment arrangement.
 321:             </p>
 322:           </div>
 323:         </header>
 324:

1008:         </section>
1009:       )}
1010: 
1011:       {/* =====================================
1012:           Submission Error
1013:           ===================================== */}
1014: 
1015:       {submissionError && (

1020:         </div>
1021:       )}
1022: 
1023:       {/* =====================================
1024:           Form Actions

1026:

1035:         </button>

1038:           type="submit"
1039:           className="experience-form-save-button"

1043:             ? "Saving..."
1044:             : isEditing
1045:               ? "Save Changes"
1046:               : "Save Experience"}

1049:     </form>
1050:   );
1051: }

1055:  * Form Field
1056:  * =========================================
1057:  */
1058: 
1059: function FormField({
1060:   id,
1061:   label,
1062:   required = false,
1063:   error = "",
1064:   help = "",
1065:   count = "",
1066:   className = "",
1067:   children,
1068: }) {
1069:   return (
1070:     <div
1071:       className={`experience-form-field ${className} ${
1072:         error ? "experience-form-field--error" : ""
1073:       }`}
1074:     >
1075:       <div className="experience-form-label-row">
1076:         <label htmlFor={id}>
1077:           {label}
1078: 
1079:           {required && <span aria-hidden="true">*</span>}
1080:         </label>
1081: 
1082:         {count && <small>{count}</small>}
1083:       </div>
1084: 
1085:       {children}
1086: 
1087:       {help && !error && <small className="experience-form-help">{help}</small>}
1088: 
1089:       {error && (
1090:         <p className="experience-form-field-error" role="alert">
1091:           {error}
1092:         </p>
1093:       )}
1094:     </div>
1095:   );
1096: }
1097: 
1098: /*
1099:  * =========================================
1100:  * Visibility Option
1101:  * =========================================
1102:  */
1103: 
1104: function VisibilityOption({ label, description, checked, onChange }) {
1105:   return (
1106:     <label className="experience-form-visibility-option">
1107:       <input
1108:         type="checkbox"
1109:         checked={checked}
1110:         onChange={(event) => onChange?.(event.target.checked)}
1111:       />
1112: 
1113:       <span>
1114:         <strong>{label}</strong>
1115: 
1116:         <small>{description}</small>
1117:       </span>
1118:     </label>
1119:   );
1120: }

   4:   getSkillCategoryLabel,

  20: const EMPTY_EDITOR = {
  21:   selectedSkillId: "",
  22:   name: "",
  23:   category: "",
  24:   proficiency: "",
  25:   usageDescription: "",
  26: };
  27: 
  28: /*
  29:  * =========================================
  30:  * Helpers

  33: 
  34: function createRelationshipId() {
  35:   if (typeof crypto !== "undefined" && crypto.randomUUID) {
  36:     return crypto.randomUUID();
  37:   }
  38: 
  39:   return `experience-technology-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()
  40:     .toString(36)
  41:     .slice(2)}`;
  42: }
  43: 
  44: function normalizeName(value) {
  45:   return String(value || "")
  46:     .trim()
  47:     .toLocaleLowerCase();
  48: }
  49: 
  50: function getTechnologyName(technology) {
  51:   if (typeof technology === "string") {
  52:     return technology.trim();
  53:   }
  54: 
  55:   return String(
  56:     technology?.nameSnapshot ||
  57:       technology?.name ||
  58:       technology?.label ||
  59:       technology?.value ||
  60:       "",
  61:   ).trim();
  62: }
  63: 
  64: function normalizeTechnologyRelationship(technology, index) {
  65:   if (typeof technology === "string") {
  66:     return {
  67:       id: `legacy-technology-${index}`,
  68:       skillId: "",
  69:       nameSnapshot: technology.trim(),
  70:       name: technology.trim(),
  71:       category: "",
  72:       proficiency: "",
  73:       usageDescription: "",
  74:       order: index,
  75:     };
  76:   }
  77: 
  78:   const name = getTechnologyName(technology);
  79: 
  80:   return {
  81:     id: technology?.id || `technology-${index}`,
  82:     skillId: technology?.skillId || technology?.profileSkillId || "",
  83:     nameSnapshot: technology?.nameSnapshot || name,
  84:     name,
  85:     category: technology?.category || "",
  86:     proficiency: technology?.proficiency || technology?.level || "",
  87:     usageDescription:
  88:       technology?.usageDescription || technology?.description || "",
  89:     order: Number.isFinite(Number(technology?.order))
  90:       ? Number(technology.order)
  91:       : index,
  92:   };
  93: }
  94: 
  95: function getLibrarySkillCategory(skill) {
  96:   return skill?.category || skill?.type || "";
  97: }
  98: 
  99: /*
 100:  * =========================================
 101:  * Technology Editor
 102:  * =========================================
 103:  */
 104: 
 105: function ExperienceTechnologyEditor({
 106:   technologies = [],
 107:   fieldError = "",
 108:   disabled = false,
 109:   onChange,
 110: }) {
 111:   const { activeSkills = [], findOrCreateSkill } = useSkillData();
 112: 
 113:   const [editor, setEditor] = useState(EMPTY_EDITOR);
 114:   const [editingRelationshipId, setEditingRelationshipId] = useState("");
 115:   const [searchValue, setSearchValue] = useState("");
 116:   const [editorError, setEditorError] = useState("");
 117:   const [isSavingTechnology, setIsSavingTechnology] = useState(false);
 118: 
 119:   /*
 120:    * =========================================
 121:    * Normalized Relationships
 122:    * =========================================
 123:    */
 124: 
 125:   const normalizedTechnologies = useMemo(
 126:     () =>
 127:       (technologies || [])
 128:         .map(normalizeTechnologyRelationship)
 129:         .filter((technology) => technology.nameSnapshot)
 130:         .sort((first, second) => first.order - second.order),
 131:     [technologies],
 132:   );
 133: 
 134:   const selectedNames = useMemo(
 135:     () =>
 136:       new Set(
 137:         normalizedTechnologies.map((technology) =>
 138:           normalizeName(technology.nameSnapshot),
 139:         ),
 140:       ),
 141:     [normalizedTechnologies],
 142:   );
 143: 
 144:   /*
 145:    * =========================================
 146:    * Library Search
 147:    * =========================================
 148:    */
 149: 
 150:   const matchingLibrarySkills = useMemo(() => {
 151:     const normalizedSearch = normalizeName(searchValue);
 152: 
 153:     return activeSkills
 154:       .filter((skill) => {
 155:         if (!skill?.name) {
 156:           return false;
 157:         }
 158: 
 159:         if (!normalizedSearch) {
 160:           return true;
 161:         }
 162: 
 163:         const searchableValue = [
 164:           skill.name,
 165:           skill.category,
 166:           skill.type,
 167:           ...(skill.aliases || []),
 168:         ]
 169:           .filter(Boolean)
 170:           .join(" ")
 171:           .toLocaleLowerCase();
 172: 
 173:         return searchableValue.includes(normalizedSearch);
 174:       })
 175:       .sort((first, second) => first.name.localeCompare(second.name))
 176:       .slice(0, 25);
 177:   }, [activeSkills, searchValue]);
 178: 
 179:   /*
 180:    * =========================================
 181:    * Editor State
 182:    * =========================================
 183:    */
 184: 
 185:   const updateEditor = (updates) => {
 186:     setEditor((currentEditor) => ({
 187:       ...currentEditor,
 188:       ...updates,
 189:     }));
 190: 
 191:     setEditorError("");
 192:   };
 193: 
 194:   const resetEditor = () => {
 195:     setEditor(EMPTY_EDITOR);
 196:     setEditingRelationshipId("");
 197:     setSearchValue("");
 198:     setEditorError("");
 199:   };
 200: 
 201:   /*
 202:    * =========================================
 203:    * Select Library Skill
 204:    * =========================================
 205:    */
 206: 
 207:   const handleLibrarySelection = (event) => {
 208:     const skillId = event.target.value;
 209: 
 210:     if (!skillId) {
 211:       updateEditor({
 212:         selectedSkillId: "",
 213:         name: "",
 214:         category: "",
 215:       });
 216: 
 217:       return;
 218:     }
 219: 
 220:     const selectedSkill = activeSkills.find((skill) => skill.id === skillId);
 221: 
 222:     if (!selectedSkill) {
 223:       setEditorError("The selected Skill Library item could not be found.");
 224: 
 225:       return;
 226:     }
 227: 
 228:     updateEditor({
 229:       selectedSkillId: selectedSkill.id,
 230:       name: selectedSkill.name,
 231:       category: getLibrarySkillCategory(selectedSkill),
 232:     });
 233:   };
 234: 
 235:   /*
 236:    * =========================================
 237:    * Duplicate Detection
 238:    * =========================================
 239:    */
 240: 
 241:   const isDuplicateTechnology = (name, skillId = "") => {
 242:     const normalizedCandidateName = normalizeName(name);
 243: 
 244:     return normalizedTechnologies.some((technology) => {
 245:       if (technology.id === editingRelationshipId) {
 246:         return false;
 247:       }
 248: 
 249:       if (skillId && technology.skillId && technology.skillId === skillId) {
 250:         return true;
 251:       }
 252: 
 253:       return normalizeName(technology.nameSnapshot) === normalizedCandidateName;
 254:     });
 255:   };
 256: 
 257:   /*
 258:    * =========================================
 259:    * Add or Update Technology
 260:    * =========================================
 261:    */
 262: 
 263:   const handleSaveTechnology = async () => {
 264:     if (disabled || isSavingTechnology) {
 265:       return;
 266:     }
 267: 
 268:     const technologyName = editor.name.trim();
 269: 
 270:     if (!technologyName) {
 271:       setEditorError("Select a Skill Library item or enter a technology name.");
 272: 
 273:       return;
 274:     }
 275: 
 276:     if (isDuplicateTechnology(technologyName, editor.selectedSkillId)) {
 277:       setEditorError(
 278:         "This technology has already been added to the experience.",
 279:       );
 280: 
 281:       return;
 282:     }
 283: 
 284:     try {
 285:       setIsSavingTechnology(true);
 286:       setEditorError("");
 287: 
 288:       let librarySkill = activeSkills.find(
 289:         (skill) => skill.id === editor.selectedSkillId,
 290:       );
 291: 
 292:       /*
 293:        * A manually entered technology is saved in the
 294:        * central Skill Library before being attached to
 295:        * this experience.
 296:        */
 297: 
 298:       if (!librarySkill) {
 299:         const skillResult = await findOrCreateSkill({
 300:           name: technologyName,
 301: 
 302:           category: editor.category || "tools-platforms",
 303: 
 304:           type: "tool",
 305: 
 306:           proficiency: {
 307:             level: editor.proficiency || "",
 308:             yearsOfExperience: null,
 309:             lastUsedDate: "",
 310:           },
 311: 
 312:           language: {
 313:             proficiency: "",
 314:           },
 315: 
 316:           description: editor.usageDescription.trim(),
 317: 
 318:           aliases: [],
 319: 
 320:           source: "manual",
 321: 
 322:           sourceContext: "experience-technology-editor",
 323: 
 324:           notes: "",
 325: 
 326:           status: "active",
 327:         });
 328: 
 329:         librarySkill = skillResult?.skill || skillResult;
 330:       }
 331: 
 332:       const savedSkill = librarySkill;
 333: 
 334:       if (!savedSkill?.id) {
 335:         throw new Error(
 336:           "The technology could not be saved in the Skill Library.",
 337:         );
 338:       }
 339: 
 340:       const relationship = {
 341:         id: editingRelationshipId || createRelationshipId(),
 342: 
 343:         skillId: savedSkill.id,
 344: 
 345:         /*
 346:          * The snapshot keeps the experience readable
 347:          * if the library item is renamed or unavailable.
 348:          */
 349: 
 350:         nameSnapshot: savedSkill.name || technologyName,
 351: 
 352:         name: savedSkill.name || technologyName,
 353: 
 354:         category: editor.category || getLibrarySkillCategory(savedSkill) || "",
 355: 
 356:         proficiency: editor.proficiency,
 357: 
 358:         usageDescription: editor.usageDescription.trim(),
 359: 
 360:         order: editingRelationshipId
 361:           ? (normalizedTechnologies.find(
 362:               (technology) => technology.id === editingRelationshipId,
 363:             )?.order ?? normalizedTechnologies.length)
 364:           : normalizedTechnologies.length,
 365:       };
 366: 
 367:       const nextTechnologies = editingRelationshipId
 368:         ? normalizedTechnologies.map((technology) =>
 369:             technology.id === editingRelationshipId ? relationship : technology,
 370:           )
 371:         : [...normalizedTechnologies, relationship];
 372: 
 373:       onChange?.(
 374:         nextTechnologies.map((technology, index) => ({
 375:           ...technology,
 376:           order: index,
 377:         })),
 378:       );
 379: 
 380:       resetEditor();
 381:     } catch (saveError) {
 382:       console.error("Unable to save experience technology:", {
 383:         error: saveError,
 384:         errors: saveError?.errors,
 385:         fieldErrors: saveError?.fieldErrors,
 386:         validation: saveError?.validation,
 387:       });
 388: 
 389:       const firstValidationMessage =
 390:         saveError?.errors?.[0]?.message ||
 391:         Object.values(saveError?.fieldErrors || {})[0];
 392: 
 393:       setEditorError(
 394:         firstValidationMessage ||
 395:           saveError?.publicMessage ||
 396:           saveError?.message ||
 397:           "The technology could not be saved.",
 398:       );
 399:     } finally {
 400:       setIsSavingTechnology(false);
 401:     }
 402:   };
 403: 
 404:   /*
 405:    * =========================================
 406:    * Edit
 407:    * =========================================
 408:    */
 409: 
 410:   const handleEdit = (technology) => {
 411:     setEditingRelationshipId(technology.id);
 412: 
 413:     setEditor({
 414:       selectedSkillId: technology.skillId || "",
 415:       name: technology.nameSnapshot,
 416:       category: technology.category || "",
 417:       proficiency: technology.proficiency || "",
 418:       usageDescription: technology.usageDescription || "",
 419:     });
 420: 
 421:     setSearchValue(technology.nameSnapshot);
 422:     setEditorError("");
 423:   };
 424: 
 425:   /*
 426:    * =========================================
 427:    * Remove
 428:    * =========================================
 429:    */
 430: 
 431:   const handleRemove = (relationshipId) => {
 432:     const nextTechnologies = normalizedTechnologies
 433:       .filter((technology) => technology.id !== relationshipId)
 434:       .map((technology, index) => ({
 435:         ...technology,
 436:         order: index,
 437:       }));
 438: 
 439:     onChange?.(nextTechnologies);
 440: 
 441:     if (editingRelationshipId === relationshipId) {
 442:       resetEditor();
 443:     }
 444:   };
 445: 
 446:   /*
 447:    * =========================================
 448:    * Reorder
 449:    * =========================================
 450:    */
 451: 
 452:   const handleMove = (relationshipId, direction) => {
 453:     const currentIndex = normalizedTechnologies.findIndex(
 454:       (technology) => technology.id === relationshipId,
 455:     );
 456: 
 457:     const nextIndex = currentIndex + direction;
 458: 
 459:     if (
 460:       currentIndex < 0 ||
 461:       nextIndex < 0 ||
 462:       nextIndex >= normalizedTechnologies.length
 463:     ) {
 464:       return;
 465:     }
 466: 
 467:     const reorderedTechnologies = [...normalizedTechnologies];
 468: 
 469:     const [movedTechnology] = reorderedTechnologies.splice(currentIndex, 1);
 470: 
 471:     reorderedTechnologies.splice(nextIndex, 0, movedTechnology);
 472: 
 473:     onChange?.(
 474:       reorderedTechnologies.map((technology, index) => ({
 475:         ...technology,
 476:         order: index,
 477:       })),
 478:     );
 479:   };
 480: 
 481:   return (
 482:     <section className="experience-form-section experience-technology-editor">
 483:       <header className="experience-form-section-header">
 484:         <span aria-hidden="true" />
 485: 
 486:         <div>
 487:           <small>Tools and Technology</small>
 488: 
 489:           <h3>Technologies and Tools</h3>
 490: 
 491:           <p>
 492:             Add the software, platforms, frameworks, tools, systems, hardware,
 493:             or equipment used in this experience.
 494:           </p>
 495:         </div>
 496:       </header>
 497: 
 498:       <div className="experience-technology-editor-workspace">
 499:         <section className="experience-technology-editor-panel">
 500:           <header>
 501:             <div>
 502:               <span>
 503:                 {editingRelationshipId ? "Edit Technology" : "Add Technology"}
 504:               </span>
 505: 
 506:               <h4>Select or Create a Library Item</h4>
 507:             </div>
 508: 
 509:             {editingRelationshipId && (
 510:               <button type="button" onClick={resetEditor} disabled={disabled}>
 511:                 Cancel Edit
 512:               </button>
 513:             )}
 514:           </header>
 515: 
 516:           <div className="experience-technology-editor-fields">
 517:             <div className="experience-technology-editor-field experience-technology-editor-field--full">
 518:               <label htmlFor="experience-technology-search">
 519:                 Search Skill Library
 520:               </label>
 521: 
 522:               <input
 523:                 id="experience-technology-search"
 524:                 type="search"
 525:                 value={searchValue}
 526:                 onChange={(event) => setSearchValue(event.target.value)}
 527:                 disabled={disabled}

 529:               />
 530:             </div>

 532:             <div className="experience-technology-editor-field experience-technology-editor-field--full">
 533:               <label htmlFor="experience-technology-library">
 534:                 Existing Library Item
 535:               </label>

 538:                 id="experience-technology-library"

 551:                     <option
 552:                       key={skill.id}

 555:                         alreadySelected && skill.id !== editor.selectedSkillId
 556:                       }
 557:                     >
 558:                       {skill.name}
 559:                       {skill.category ? ` â€” ${skill.category}` : ""}
 560:                       {alreadySelected && skill.id !== editor.selectedSkillId
 561:                         ? " â€” Already added"
 562:                         : ""}
 563:                     </option>
 564:                   );
 565:                 })}
 566:               </select>
 567: 
 568:               <small>
 569:                 Selecting a library item does not create a duplicate Skill
 570:                 record.
 571:               </small>

 741: 
 742:                     {technology.usageDescription && (
 743:                       <p>{technology.usageDescription}</p>
 744:                     )}
 745: 
 746:                     <small>
 747:                       {technology.skillId
 748:                         ? "Connected to Skill Library"
 749:                         : "Legacy technology"}
 750:                     </small>
 751:                   </div>
 752: 
 753:                   <div className="experience-technology-editor-item-actions">
 754:                     <button
 755:                       type="button"
 756:                       onClick={() => handleMove(technology.id, -1)}
 757:                       disabled={disabled || index === 0}
 758:                       aria-label={`Move ${technology.nameSnapshot} up`}
 759:                     >
 760:                       â†‘
 761:                     </button>
 762: 
 763:                     <button
 764:                       type="button"
 765:                       onClick={() => handleMove(technology.id, 1)}
 766:                       disabled={
 767:                         disabled || index === normalizedTechnologies.length - 1
 768:                       }
 769:                       aria-label={`Move ${technology.nameSnapshot} down`}
 770:                     >
 771:                       â†“
 772:                     </button>
 773: 
 774:                     <button
 775:                       type="button"
 776:                       onClick={() => handleEdit(technology)}
 777:                       disabled={disabled}
 778:                     >
 779:                       Edit
 780:                     </button>
 781: 
 782:                     <button
 783:                       type="button"
 784:                       className="experience-technology-editor-remove"
 785:                       onClick={() => handleRemove(technology.id)}
 786:                       disabled={disabled}
 787:                       aria-label={`Remove ${technology.nameSnapshot}`}
 788:                     >
 789:                       Remove
 790:                     </button>
 791:                   </div>
 792:                 </article>
 793:               ))}
 794:             </div>
 795:           ) : (
 796:             <div className="experience-technology-editor-empty">
 797:               <span aria-hidden="true">âŒ˜</span>
 798: 
 799:               <h5>No technologies added</h5>
 800: 
 801:               <p>
 802:                 Add tools and technologies that were actually used during this
 803:                 experience.
 804:               </p>
 805:             </div>
 806:           )}
 807:         </section>
 808:       </div>
 809: 
 810:       {fieldError && (
 811:         <p className="experience-technology-editor-field-error" role="alert">
 812:           {fieldError}
 813:         </p>
 814:       )}
 815:     </section>
 816:   );
 817: }
 818: 
 819: export default ExperienceTechnologyEditor;

   1: import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

   3: import { prepareExperienceCollection } from "../../../../services/Experience/experienceUtils.js";

   6: import ExperienceFilters from "../ExperienceFilters/ExperienceFilters.jsx";
   7: 
   8: import "./ExperienceList.css";
   9: 
  10: const DEFAULT_FILTERS = {
  11:   category: "all",
  12:   employmentType: "all",
  13:   workArrangement: "all",
  14:   timeline: "all",
  15:   hasAchievements: false,
  16:   hasSkillsOrTechnologies: false,
  17: };
  18: 
  19: function ExperienceList({
  20:   experiences = [],
  21:   operation = {},
  22:   onEdit,
  23:   onArchive,
  24:   onRestore,
  25:   onDelete,
  26: }) {
  27:   const [expandedExperienceId, setExpandedExperienceId] = useState("");
  28: 
  29:   const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  30: 
  31:   const [filters, setFilters] = useState(DEFAULT_FILTERS);
  32: 
  33:   /*
  34:    * Current positions appear first. Previous
  35:    * positions follow from most recently ended
  36:    * to oldest.
  37:    */
  38: 
  39:   const [sortOption, setSortOption] = useState("newest");
  40: 
  41:   const displayedExperiences = useMemo(
  42:     () =>
  43:       prepareExperienceCollection({
  44:         experiences,
  45:         query,
  46:         filters,
  47:         sortOption,
  48:       }),
  49:     [experiences, query, filters, sortOption],
  50:   );
  51: 
  52:   const hasActiveFilters =
  53:     Boolean(query.trim()) ||
  54:     filters.category !== "all" ||
  55:     filters.employmentType !== "all" ||
  56:     filters.workArrangement !== "all" ||
  57:     filters.timeline !== "all" ||
  58:     filters.hasAchievements ||
  59:     filters.hasSkillsOrTechnologies;
  60: 
  61:   const handleToggleView = (experienceId) => {
  62:     setExpandedExperienceId((currentId) =>
  63:       currentId === experienceId ? "" : experienceId,
  64:     );
  65:   };
  66: 
  67:   const handleFilterChange = (field, value) => {
  68:     setFilters((currentFilters) => ({
  69:       ...currentFilters,
  70:       [field]: value,
  71:     }));
  72: 
  73:     setExpandedExperienceId("");
  74:   };
  75: 
  76:   const handleQueryChange = (value) => {
  77:     setQuery(value);
  78:     setExpandedExperienceId("");
  79:   };
  80: 
  81:   const handleSortChange = (value) => {
  82:     setSortOption(value);
  83:     setExpandedExperienceId("");
  84:   };
  85: 
  86:   const handleClearFilters = () => {
  87:     setQuery("");
  88:     setFilters(DEFAULT_FILTERS);
  89:     setSortOption("newest");
  90:     setExpandedExperienceId("");
  91:   };
  92: 
  93:   return (
  94:     <div className="experience-list-container">
  95:       <ExperienceFilters
  96:         query={query}
  97:         filters={filters}
  98:         sortOption={sortOption}
  99:         resultCount={displayedExperiences.length}
 100:         totalCount={experiences.length}
 101:         hasActiveFilters={hasActiveFilters}
 102:         onQueryChange={handleQueryChange}
 103:         onFilterChange={handleFilterChange}
 104:         onSortChange={handleSortChange}
 105:         onClear={handleClearFilters}
 106:       />
 107: 
 108:       {displayedExperiences.length > 0 ? (
 109:         <div className="experience-list">
 110:           {displayedExperiences.map((experience) => {
 111:             const isWorking =
 112:               operation?.experienceId === experience.id &&
 113:               operation?.status === "loading";
 114: 
 115:             return (
 116:               <ExperienceCard
 117:                 key={experience.id}
 118:                 experience={experience}
 119:                 isExpanded={expandedExperienceId === experience.id}
 120:                 isWorking={isWorking}
 121:                 onToggleView={handleToggleView}
 122:                 onEdit={onEdit}
 123:                 onArchive={onArchive}
 124:                 onRestore={onRestore}
 125:                 onDelete={onDelete}
 126:               />
 127:             );
 128:           })}
 129:         </div>
 130:       ) : (
 131:         <div className="experience-list-empty">
 132:           <span aria-hidden="true">âŒ•</span>
 133: 
 134:           <h3>No matching experiences</h3>
 135: 
 136:           <p>
 137:             Change the search text or filter selections to display more
 138:             Experience records.
 139:           </p>
 140: 
 141:           <button type="button" onClick={handleClearFilters}>
 142:             Clear Search and Filters
 143:           </button>
 144:         </div>
 145:       )}
 146:     </div>
 147:   );
 148: }
 149: 
 150: export default ExperienceList;

   1: import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
   2: 
   3: import { API_ENDPOINTS } from "../../../../config/api.js";
   4: 
   5: import { EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS } from "../../../../config/experienceConfig.js";
   6: 
   7: import { useSkillData } from "../../../../context/SkillDataContext.jsx";
   8: 
   9: import ExperienceOverviewReadiness from "./ExperienceOverviewReadiness/ExperienceOverviewReadiness.jsx";

  12: import "./ExperienceOverviewBuilder.css";
  13: 
  14: const EMPTY_OVERVIEW_META = {
  15:   source: "manual",
  16:   status: "empty",
  17:   generatedAt: null,
  18:   contextFingerprint: "",
  19:   isStale: false,
  20: };
  21: 
  22: /*
  23:  * =========================================
  24:  * Context Helpers
  25:  * =========================================
  26:  */
  27: 
  28: function getResponsibilityText(value) {
  29:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : value?.text?.trim() || "";
  30: }
  31: 
  32: function normalizeAchievement(value) {
  33:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  34:     return {
  35:       text: value.trim(),
  36:       metric: "",
  37:     };
  38:   }
  39: 
  40:   return {
  41:     text: value?.text?.trim() || "",
  42:     metric: value?.metric?.trim() || "",
  43:   };
  44: }
  45: 
  46: function normalizeTechnology(value) {
  47:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  48:     return {
  49:       name: value.trim(),
  50:       category: "",
  51:       proficiency: "",
  52:       usageDescription: "",
  53:     };
  54:   }
  55: 
  56:   return {
  57:     name: String(
  58:       value?.nameSnapshot || value?.name || value?.value || value?.label || "",
  59:     ).trim(),
  60: 
  61:     category: String(value?.category || "").trim(),
  62: 
  63:     proficiency: String(value?.proficiency || value?.level || "").trim(),
  64: 
  65:     usageDescription: String(
  66:       value?.usageDescription || value?.description || "",
  67:     ).trim(),
  68:   };
  69: }
  70: 
  71: function getWordCount(value) {
  72:   return value.trim() ? value.trim().split(/\s+/).length : 0;
  73: }
  74: 
  75: /*
  76:  * =========================================
  77:  * Context Fingerprint
  78:  * =========================================
  79:  */
  80: 
  81: function createContextFingerprint(context) {
  82:   const fingerprintValue = JSON.stringify({
  83:     positionTitle: context.positionTitle,
  84:     organizationIndustry: context.organizationIndustry,
  85:     employmentType: context.employmentType,
  86:     workArrangement: context.workArrangement,
  87:     responsibilities: context.responsibilities,
  88:     achievements: context.achievements,
  89:     skills: context.skills,
  90:     technologies: context.technologies,
  91:     leadership: context.leadership,
  92:   });
  93: 
  94:   let hash = 0;
  95: 
  96:   for (let index = 0; index < fingerprintValue.length; index += 1) {
  97:     hash = (hash << 5) - hash + fingerprintValue.charCodeAt(index);
  98: 
  99:     hash |= 0;
 100:   }
 101: 
 102:   return `experience-overview-${Math.abs(hash).toString(36)}`;
 103: }
 104: 
 105: /*
 106:  * =========================================
 107:  * Readiness
 108:  * =========================================
 109:  */
 110: 
 111: function getOverviewReadiness(context) {
 112:   const details = [
 113:     {
 114:       id: "position",
 115:       label: "Position",
 116:       complete: Boolean(context.positionTitle),
 117:       weight: 2,
 118:       value: context.positionTitle || "Not provided",
 119:     },
 120:     {
 121:       id: "responsibilities",
 122:       label: "Responsibilities",
 123:       complete: context.responsibilities.length > 0,
 124:       weight: 3,
 125:       value: `${context.responsibilities.length} added`,
 126:     },
 127:     {
 128:       id: "achievements",
 129:       label: "Achievements",
 130:       complete: context.achievements.length > 0,
 131:       weight: 3,
 132:       value: `${context.achievements.length} added`,
 133:     },
 134:     {
 135:       id: "skills",
 136:       label: "Skills",
 137:       complete: context.skills.length > 0,
 138:       weight: 2,
 139:       value: `${context.skills.length} selected`,
 140:     },
 141:     {
 142:       id: "technologies",
 143:       label: "Technologies",
 144:       complete: context.technologies.length > 0,
 145:       weight: 2,
 146:       value: `${context.technologies.length} selected`,
 147:     },
 148:     {
 149:       id: "leadership",
 150:       label: "Leadership",
 151:       complete: Boolean(
 152:         context.leadership.description ||
 153:         context.leadership.hasLeadershipResponsibilities,
 154:       ),
 155:       weight: 1,
 156:       value:
 157:         context.leadership.description ||
 158:         context.leadership.hasLeadershipResponsibilities
 159:           ? "Provided"
 160:           : "Not provided",
 161:     },
 162:   ];
 163: 
 164:   const maximumScore = details.reduce(
 165:     (total, detail) => total + detail.weight,
 166:     0,
 167:   );
 168: 
 169:   const score = details.reduce(
 170:     (total, detail) => total + (detail.complete ? detail.weight : 0),
 171:     0,
 172:   );
 173: 
 174:   if (score >= maximumScore) {
 175:     return {
 176:       level: "excellent",
 177:       label: "Excellent",
 178:       description:
 179:         "All major context areas are available for a detailed and accurate overview.",
 180:       score,
 181:       maximumScore,
 182:       details,
 183:     };
 184:   }
 185: 
 186:   if (score >= 9) {
 187:     return {
 188:       level: "strong",
 189:       label: "Strong",
 190:       description:
 191:         "The available information provides strong context for an accurate overview.",
 192:       score,
 193:       maximumScore,
 194:       details,
 195:     };
 196:   }
 197: 
 198:   if (score >= 4) {
 199:     return {
 200:       level: "developing",
 201:       label: "Developing",
 202:       description:
 203:         "The AI can generate an overview, but more responsibilities, achievements, or skills would improve it.",
 204:       score,
 205:       maximumScore,
 206:       details,
 207:     };
 208:   }
 209: 
 210:   return {
 211:     level: "basic",
 212:     label: "Basic",
 213:     description:
 214:       "Add more professional context for a specific and meaningful overview.",
 215:     score,
 216:     maximumScore,
 217:     details,
 218:   };
 219: }
 220: 
 221: /*
 222:  * =========================================
 223:  * Overview Builder
 224:  * =========================================
 225:  */
 226: 
 227: function ExperienceOverviewBuilder({
 228:   experience = {},
 229:   fieldError = "",
 230:   disabled = false,
 231:   onChange,
 232: }) {
 233:   const sectionTitleId = useId();
 234:   const textareaId = useId();
 235: 
 236:   const abortControllerRef = useRef(null);
 237: 
 238:   const { skillsById } = useSkillData();
 239: 
 240:   const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState([]);
 241: 
 242:   const [warnings, setWarnings] = useState([]);
 243: 
 244:   const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");
 245: 
 246:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
 247: 
 248:   const overview = experience.overview || "";
 249: 
 250:   const overviewMeta = {
 251:     ...EMPTY_OVERVIEW_META,
 252:     ...experience.overviewMeta,
 253:   };
 254: 
 255:   /*
 256:    * =========================================
 257:    * Generation Context
 258:    * =========================================
 259:    */
 260: 
 261:   const generationContext = useMemo(
 262:     () => ({
 263:       positionTitle: experience.position?.title || "",
 264: 
 265:       organizationName: experience.organization?.name || "",
 266: 
 267:       organizationIndustry: experience.organization?.industry || "",
 268: 
 269:       experienceCategory: experience.category || "",
 270: 
 271:       employmentType: experience.position?.employmentType || "",
 272: 
 273:       workArrangement: experience.position?.workArrangement || "",
 274: 
 275:       responsibilities: (experience.responsibilities || [])
 276:         .map(getResponsibilityText)
 277:         .filter(Boolean),
 278: 
 279:       achievements: (experience.achievements || [])
 280:         .map(normalizeAchievement)
 281:         .filter((achievement) => achievement.text),
 282: 
 283:       skills: (experience.skills || [])
 284:         .map((relationship) => ({
 285:           name:
 286:             skillsById.get(relationship.skillId)?.name ||
 287:             relationship.nameSnapshot ||
 288:             "",
 289: 
 290:           nameSnapshot: relationship.nameSnapshot || "",
 291: 
 292:           level: relationship.level || "",
 293:         }))
 294:         .filter((skill) => skill.name || skill.nameSnapshot),
 295: 
 296:       technologies: (experience.technologies || [])
 297:         .map(normalizeTechnology)
 298:         .filter((technology) => technology.name),
 299: 
 300:       leadership: {
 301:         hasLeadershipResponsibilities:
 302:           experience.leadership?.hasLeadershipResponsibilities === true,
 303: 
 304:         peopleManaged:
 305:           experience.leadership?.peopleManaged === "" ||
 306:           experience.leadership?.peopleManaged === null ||
 307:           experience.leadership?.peopleManaged === undefined
 308:             ? null
 309:             : Number(experience.leadership.peopleManaged),
 310: 
 311:         description: experience.leadership?.description || "",
 312:       },
 313: 
 314:       existingOverview: overview,
 315: 
 316:       generationRules: {
 317:         numberOfSuggestions: 3,
 318:         recommendedWords: "50â€“120 words",
 319:         useOnlyProvidedFacts: true,
 320:         doNotInventMetrics: true,
 321:       },
 322:     }),
 323:     [experience, overview, skillsById],
 324:   );
 325: 
 326:   const contextFingerprint = useMemo(
 327:     () => createContextFingerprint(generationContext),
 328:     [generationContext],
 329:   );
 330: 
 331:   const readiness = useMemo(
 332:     () => getOverviewReadiness(generationContext),
 333:     [generationContext],
 334:   );
 335: 
 336:   const canGenerate = readiness.score > 0;
 337: 
 338:   const isGenerating = status === "loading";
 339: 
 340:   const isStale =
 341:     overviewMeta.source === "ai" &&
 342:     Boolean(overviewMeta.contextFingerprint) &&
 343:     overviewMeta.contextFingerprint !== contextFingerprint;
 344: 
 345:   /*
 346:    * =========================================
 347:    * Request Cleanup
 348:    * =========================================
 349:    */
 350: 
 351:   useEffect(() => {
 352:     return () => {
 353:       abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
 354:     };
 355:   }, []);
 356: 
 357:   /*
 358:    * =========================================
 359:    * Manual Editing
 360:    * =========================================
 361:    */
 362: 
 363:   const handleOverviewChange = (event) => {
 364:     const nextOverview = event.target.value;
 365: 
 366:     onChange?.({
 367:       overview: nextOverview,
 368: 
 369:       overviewMeta: {
 370:         source: "manual",
 371:         status: nextOverview.trim() ? "draft" : "empty",
 372:         generatedAt: null,
 373:         contextFingerprint: "",
 374:         isStale: false,
 375:       },
 376:     });
 377: 
 378:     setError("");
 379:   };
 380: 
 381:   /*
 382:    * =========================================
 383:    * Generate
 384:    * =========================================
 385:    */
 386: 
 387:   const handleGenerate = async () => {
 388:     if (!canGenerate || disabled || isGenerating) {
 389:       return;
 390:     }
 391: 
 392:     abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
 393: 
 394:     const controller = new AbortController();
 395: 
 396:     abortControllerRef.current = controller;
 397: 
 398:     try {
 399:       setStatus("loading");
 400:       setError("");
 401:       setWarnings([]);
 402: 
 403:       const response = await fetch(API_ENDPOINTS.generateExperienceOverview, {
 404:         method: "POST",
 405: 
 406:         headers: {
 407:           "Content-Type": "application/json",
 408:         },
 409: 
 410:         body: JSON.stringify(generationContext),
 411: 
 412:         signal: controller.signal,
 413:       });
 414: 
 415:       const responseData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
 416: 
 417:       if (!response.ok) {
 418:         throw new Error(
 419:           responseData.message || "The Experience Overview request failed.",
 420:         );
 421:       }
 422: 
 423:       const generatedSuggestions = Array.isArray(responseData.suggestions)
 424:         ? responseData.suggestions.filter((suggestion) =>
 425:             suggestion?.text?.trim(),
 426:           )
 427:         : [];
 428: 
 429:       if (generatedSuggestions.length !== 3) {
 430:         throw new Error(
 431:           "The AI did not return three usable Experience Overview suggestions.",
 432:         );
 433:       }
 434: 
 435:       setSuggestions(generatedSuggestions);
 436: 
 437:       setWarnings(
 438:         Array.isArray(responseData.warnings) ? responseData.warnings : [],
 439:       );
 440: 
 441:       setStatus("success");
 442:     } catch (generationError) {
 443:       if (generationError.name === "AbortError") {
 444:         return;
 445:       }
 446: 
 447:       console.error("Unable to generate Experience Overview:", generationError);
 448: 
 449:       setSuggestions([]);
 450:       setStatus("error");
 451: 
 452:       setError(
 453:         generationError.message ||
 454:           "The Experience Overview could not be generated.",
 455:       );
 456:     } finally {
 457:       if (abortControllerRef.current === controller) {
 458:         abortControllerRef.current = null;
 459:       }
 460:     }
 461:   };
 462: 
 463:   /*
 464:    * =========================================
 465:    * Accept Suggestion
 466:    * =========================================
 467:    */
 468: 
 469:   const handleAcceptSuggestion = (suggestion) => {
 470:     onChange?.({
 471:       overview: suggestion.text,
 472: 
 473:       overviewMeta: {
 474:         source: "ai",
 475:         status: "accepted",
 476:         generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 477:         contextFingerprint,
 478:         isStale: false,
 479:       },
 480:     });
 481: 
 482:     setSuggestions([]);
 483:     setWarnings([]);
 484:     setError("");
 485:     setStatus("accepted");
 486:   };
 487: 
 488:   /*
 489:    * =========================================
 490:    * Dismiss and Clear
 491:    * =========================================
 492:    */
 493: 
 494:   const handleDismiss = () => {
 495:     setSuggestions([]);
 496:     setWarnings([]);
 497:     setError("");
 498:     setStatus("idle");
 499:   };
 500: 
 501:   const handleClear = () => {
 502:     onChange?.({
 503:       overview: "",
 504:       overviewMeta: {
 505:         ...EMPTY_OVERVIEW_META,
 506:       },
 507:     });
 508: 
 509:     handleDismiss();
 510:   };
 511: 
 512:   return (
 513:     <section
 514:       className="experience-form-section experience-overview-builder"
 515:       aria-labelledby={sectionTitleId}
 516:     >
 517:       <header className="experience-form-section-header">
 518:         <span aria-hidden="true" />
 519: 
 520:         <div>
 521:           <small>Overview</small>
 522: 
 523:           <h3 id={sectionTitleId}>Experience Overview</h3>
 524: 
 525:           <p>Explain the purpose, scope, and professional focus of the role.</p>
 526:         </div>
 527:       </header>
 528: 
 529:       <ExperienceOverviewReadiness readiness={readiness} />
 530: 
 531:       {isStale && (
 532:         <div className="experience-overview-stale" role="status">
 533:           <div>
 534:             <strong>Supporting information has changed</strong>
 535: 
 536:             <p>
 537:               This AI-generated overview was created from older experience
 538:               information.
 539:             </p>
 540:           </div>
 541: 
 542:           <button
 543:             type="button"
 544:             onClick={handleGenerate}
 545:             disabled={disabled || isGenerating}
 546:           >
 547:             Regenerate
 548:           </button>
 549:         </div>
 550:       )}
 551: 
 552:       <div
 553:         className={`experience-overview-editor ${
 554:           fieldError ? "experience-overview-editor--error" : ""
 555:         }`}
 556:       >
 557:         <div className="experience-overview-label-row">
 558:           <label htmlFor={textareaId}>Professional Overview</label>
 559: 
 560:           <span>
 561:             {getWordCount(overview)} words
 562:             {" Â· "}
 563:             {overview.length}/{EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.overview}
 564:           </span>
 565:         </div>
 566: 
 567:         <textarea
 568:           id={textareaId}
 569:           value={overview}
 570:           onChange={handleOverviewChange}
 571:           rows={7}
 572:           maxLength={EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.overview}
 573:           disabled={disabled}
 574:           placeholder={`Explain the role's purpose, scope, responsibilities, and professional value.
 575: 
 576: Example: Supported business-critical systems and maintained reliable technology services across the organization.`}
 577:         />
 578: 
 579:         {fieldError && <p role="alert">{fieldError}</p>}
 580: 
 581:         <footer>
 582:           <div>
 583:             <span>
 584:               {overviewMeta.source === "ai" ? "AI Assisted" : "Manual"}
 585:             </span>
 586: 
 587:             <small>Recommended length: 50â€“120 words.</small>
 588:           </div>
 589: 
 590:           <div>
 591:             {overview && (
 592:               <button type="button" onClick={handleClear} disabled={disabled}>
 593:                 Clear
 594:               </button>
 595:             )}
 596: 
 597:             <button
 598:               type="button"
 599:               className="experience-overview-generate-button"
 600:               onClick={handleGenerate}
 601:               disabled={!canGenerate || disabled || isGenerating}
 602:             >
 603:               {isGenerating
 604:                 ? "Generating..."
 605:                 : suggestions.length > 0
 606:                   ? "Regenerate"
 607:                   : "Generate with AI"}
 608:             </button>
 609:           </div>
 610:         </footer>
 611:       </div>
 612: 
 613:       {!canGenerate && (
 614:         <p className="experience-overview-generation-help">
 615:           Add a position, responsibility, achievement, skill, or leadership
 616:           detail before generating an overview.
 617:         </p>
 618:       )}
 619: 
 620:       {isGenerating && (
 621:         <div className="experience-overview-loading" role="status">
 622:           <span aria-hidden="true" />
 623: 
 624:           <div>
 625:             <strong>Generating Experience Overviews</strong>
 626: 
 627:             <p>The AI is reviewing the supported professional information.</p>
 628:           </div>
 629:         </div>
 630:       )}
 631: 
 632:       {error && (
 633:         <div className="experience-overview-error" role="alert">
 634:           <strong>Unable to generate the overview</strong>
 635: 
 636:           <p>{error}</p>
 637:         </div>
 638:       )}
 639: 
 640:       {warnings.length > 0 && (
 641:         <div className="experience-overview-warnings">
 642:           <strong>Generation notes</strong>
 643: 
 644:           <ul>
 645:             {warnings.map((warning, index) => (
 646:               <li key={`${warning}-${index}`}>{warning}</li>
 647:             ))}
 648:           </ul>
 649:         </div>
 650:       )}
 651: 
 652:       <ExperienceOverviewSuggestions
 653:         suggestions={suggestions}
 654:         disabled={disabled || isGenerating}
 655:         onAccept={handleAcceptSuggestion}
 656:         onDismiss={handleDismiss}
 657:       />
 658:     </section>
 659:   );
 660: }
 661: 
 662: export default ExperienceOverviewBuilder;

   1: import "./ExperienceOverviewSuggestions.css";
   2: 
   3: function getWordCount(value) {
   4:   return value?.trim() ? value.trim().split(/\s+/).length : 0;
   5: }
   6: 
   7: function ExperienceOverviewSuggestions({
   8:   suggestions = [],
   9:   disabled = false,
  10:   onAccept,
  11:   onDismiss,
  12: }) {
  13:   if (suggestions.length === 0) {
  14:     return null;
  15:   }
  16: 
  17:   return (
  18:     <section
  19:       className="experience-overview-suggestions"
  20:       aria-labelledby="experience-overview-suggestions-title"
  21:     >
  22:       <header>
  23:         <div>
  24:           <span>AI Suggestions</span>
  25: 
  26:           <h4 id="experience-overview-suggestions-title">
  27:             Choose an Experience Overview
  28:           </h4>
  29: 
  30:           <p>
  31:             Review each suggestion carefully before adding it to your
  32:             professional record.
  33:           </p>
  34:         </div>
  35: 
  36:         <button type="button" onClick={onDismiss} disabled={disabled}>
  37:           Dismiss
  38:         </button>
  39:       </header>
  40: 
  41:       <div className="experience-overview-suggestions-list">
  42:         {suggestions.map((suggestion, index) => (
  43:           <article key={suggestion.id}>
  44:             <header>
  45:               <div>
  46:                 <span>Option {index + 1}</span>
  47: 
  48:                 <h5>{suggestion.label}</h5>
  49:               </div>
  50: 
  51:               <span>{getWordCount(suggestion.text)} words</span>
  52:             </header>
  53: 
  54:             <p>{suggestion.text}</p>
  55: 
  56:             <button
  57:               type="button"
  58:               onClick={() => onAccept?.(suggestion)}
  59:               disabled={disabled}
  60:             >
  61:               Use This Overview
  62:             </button>
  63:           </article>
  64:         ))}
  65:       </div>
  66:     </section>
  67:   );
  68: }
  69: 
  70: export default ExperienceOverviewSuggestions;

FILE: src\pages\Experience\components\ExperienceSkillsEditor\ExperienceSkillsEditor.jsx
===========================================================================
   1: import { useId, useMemo } from "react";
   2: 
   3: import {
   4:   SKILL_PROFICIENCY_OPTIONS,
   5:   getSkillCategoryLabel,
   6:   getSkillTypeLabel,
   7: } from "../../../../config/skillConfig.js";
   8: 
   9: import { EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS } from "../../../../config/experienceConfig.js";
  10: 
  11: import { useSkillData } from "../../../../context/SkillDataContext.jsx";
  12: 
  13: import { createExperienceSkill } from "../../../../models/experienceModel.js";
  14: 
  15: import SkillSelector from "../../../skills/components/SkillSelector/SkillSelector.jsx";
  16: 
  17: import ExperienceSkillSuggestions from "./ExperienceSkillSuggestions/ExperienceSkillSuggestions.jsx";
  18: 
  19: import "./ExperienceSkillsEditor.css";
  20: 
  21: /*
  22:  * =========================================
  23:  * Relationship Ordering
  24:  * =========================================
  25:  */
  26: 
  27: function normalizeSkillOrder(skills = []) {
  28:   return skills.map((skill, index) => ({
  29:     ...skill,
  30:     order: index,
  31:   }));
  32: }
  33: 
  34: /*
  35:  * =========================================
  36:  * Relationship Display
  37:  * =========================================
  38:  */
  39: 
  40: function getRelationshipInformation(relationship, skillsById) {
  41:   const librarySkill = skillsById.get(relationship.skillId);
  42: 
  43:   return {
  44:     librarySkill,
  45: 
  46:     name:
  47:       librarySkill?.name || relationship.nameSnapshot || "Unavailable Skill",
  48: 
  49:     category:
  50:       librarySkill?.category || relationship.categorySnapshot || "other",
  51: 
  52:     type: librarySkill?.type || relationship.typeSnapshot || "professional",
  53: 
  54:     isConnected: Boolean(librarySkill),
  55: 
  56:     isArchived: librarySkill?.status === "archived",
  57:   };
  58: }
  59: 
  60: /*
  61:  * =========================================
  62:  * Experience Skills Editor
  63:  * =========================================
  64:  */
  65: 
  66: function ExperienceSkillsEditor({
  67:   experience = {},
  68:   experienceSkills = [],
  69:   fieldErrors = {},
  70:   disabled = false,
  71:   onChange,
  72: }) {
  73:   const sectionTitleId = useId();
  74: 
  75:   const { activeSkills, skillsById, findOrCreateSkill, isLoading } =
  76:     useSkillData();
  77: 
  78:   const maximumSkills = EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumSkills;
  79: 
  80:   const orderedExperienceSkills = useMemo(
  81:     () =>
  82:       [...experienceSkills].sort(
  83:         (first, second) => (first.order ?? 0) - (second.order ?? 0),
  84:       ),
  85:     [experienceSkills],
  86:   );
  87: 
  88:   const selectedSkillIds = useMemo(
  89:     () =>
  90:       orderedExperienceSkills
  91:         .map((relationship) => relationship.skillId)
  92:         .filter(Boolean),
  93:     [orderedExperienceSkills],
  94:   );
  95: 
  96:   const isWorking = disabled || isLoading;
  97: 
  98:   /*
  99:    * =========================================
 100:    * Attach Library Skill
 101:    * =========================================
 102:    */
 103: 
 104:   const handleSelectSkill = (skill) => {
 105:     if (isWorking || !skill?.id || experienceSkills.length >= maximumSkills) {
 106:       return;
 107:     }
 108: 
 109:     const alreadyAttached = experienceSkills.some(
 110:       (relationship) => relationship.skillId === skill.id,
 111:     );
 112: 
 113:     if (alreadyAttached) {
 114:       return;
 115:     }
 116: 
 117:     const relationship = createExperienceSkill({
 118:       skillId: skill.id,
 119:       nameSnapshot: skill.name,
 120:       categorySnapshot: skill.category,
 121:       typeSnapshot: skill.type,
 122:       level: "",
 123:       usageDescription: "",
 124:       order: experienceSkills.length,
 125:     });
 126: 
 127:     onChange?.(normalizeSkillOrder([...experienceSkills, relationship]));
 128:   };
 129: 
 130:   /*
 131:    * =========================================
 132:    * Create Library Skill
 133:    * =========================================
 134:    *
 135:    * SkillSelector will call handleSelectSkill()
 136:    * after this operation succeeds.
 137:    */
 138: 
 139:   const handleCreateSkill = async (skillData) => {
 140:     return findOrCreateSkill({
 141:       ...skillData,
 142:       source: skillData.source || "manual",
 143:       sourceContext: "experience",
 144:     });
 145:   };
 146: 
 147:   /*
 148:    * =========================================
 149:    * Remove Relationship
 150:    * =========================================
 151:    */
 152: 
 153:   const removeRelationship = (relationshipId) => {
 154:     onChange?.(
 155:       normalizeSkillOrder(
 156:         experienceSkills.filter(
 157:           (relationship) => relationship.id !== relationshipId,
 158:         ),
 159:       ),
 160:     );
 161:   };
 162: 
 163:   const handleRemoveSelectedSkill = (skill) => {
 164:     const relationship = experienceSkills.find(
 165:       (candidate) => candidate.skillId === skill.id,
 166:     );
 167: 
 168:     if (relationship) {
 169:       removeRelationship(relationship.id);
 170:     }
 171:   };
 172: 
 173:   /*
 174:    * =========================================
 175:    * Update Relationship
 176:    * =========================================
 177:    */
 178: 
 179:   const updateRelationship = (relationshipId, updates) => {
 180:     onChange?.(
 181:       normalizeSkillOrder(
 182:         orderedExperienceSkills.map((relationship) =>
 183:           relationship.id === relationshipId
 184:             ? {
 185:                 ...relationship,
 186:                 ...updates,
 187:               }
 188:             : relationship,
 189:         ),
 190:       ),
 191:     );
 192:   };
 193: 
 194:   /*
 195:    * =========================================
 196:    * Reorder Relationship
 197:    * =========================================
 198:    */
 199: 
 200:   const handleMove = (relationshipId, direction) => {
 201:     const currentIndex = orderedExperienceSkills.findIndex(
 202:       (relationship) => relationship.id === relationshipId,
 203:     );
 204: 
 205:     if (currentIndex === -1) {
 206:       return;
 207:     }
 208: 
 209:     const targetIndex =
 210:       direction === "up" ? currentIndex - 1 : currentIndex + 1;
 211: 
 212:     if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= orderedExperienceSkills.length) {
 213:       return;
 214:     }
 215: 
 216:     const reorderedSkills = [...orderedExperienceSkills];
 217: 
 218:     const [movedSkill] = reorderedSkills.splice(currentIndex, 1);
 219: 
 220:     reorderedSkills.splice(targetIndex, 0, movedSkill);
 221: 
 222:     onChange?.(normalizeSkillOrder(reorderedSkills));
 223:   };
 224: 
 225:   return (
 226:     <section
 227:       className="experience-form-section experience-skills-editor"
 228:       aria-labelledby={sectionTitleId}
 229:     >
 230:       <header className="experience-form-section-header">
 231:         <span aria-hidden="true" />
 232: 
 233:         <div>
 234:           <small>Professional Capabilities</small>
 235: 
 236:           <h3 id={sectionTitleId}>Skills Used in This Experience</h3>
 237: 
 238:           <p>
 239:             Select skills from your central Skill Library or create a reusable
 240:             skill and attach it to this Experience.
 241:           </p>
 242:         </div>
 243:       </header>
 244: 
 245:       {/*
 246:        * =========================================
 247:        * Shared Skill Selector
 248:        * =========================================
 249:        */}
 250: 
 251:       <SkillSelector
 252:         skills={activeSkills}
 253:         selectedSkillIds={selectedSkillIds}
 254:         maximumSelections={maximumSkills}
 255:         disabled={isWorking}
 256:         allowCreate
 257:         defaultNewSkillCategory="other"
 258:         defaultNewSkillType="professional"
 259:         title="Select Experience Skills"
 260:         description="Search the central Skill Library or create a new skill used in this professional experience."
 261:         emptyMessage="Add a new skill to begin building the shared Skill Library."
 262:         onSelect={handleSelectSkill}
 263:         onRemove={handleRemoveSelectedSkill}
 264:         onCreateSkill={handleCreateSkill}
 265:       />
 266: 
 267:       {/*
 268:        * =========================================
 269:        * AI Skill Suggestions
 270:        * =========================================
 271:        */}
 272: 
 273:       <ExperienceSkillSuggestions
 274:         experience={experience}
 275:         experienceSkills={experienceSkills}
 276:         maximumExperienceSkills={maximumSkills}
 277:         disabled={isWorking}
 278:         onChange={onChange}
 279:       />
 280: 
 281:       {/*
 282:        * =========================================
 283:        * Selected Relationships
 284:        * =========================================
 285:        */}
 286: 
 287:       <section className="experience-skills-selected">
 288:         <header>
 289:           <div>
 290:             <span>Selected Skills</span>
 291: 
 292:             <h4>Experience Skill Set</h4>
 293: 
 294:             <p>
 295:               Add Experience-specific proficiency and explain how each skill was
 296:               used.
 297:             </p>
 298:           </div>
 299: 
 300:           <strong>{orderedExperienceSkills.length}</strong>
 301:         </header>
 302: 
 303:         {orderedExperienceSkills.length > 0 ? (
 304:           <div className="experience-skills-selected-list">
 305:             {orderedExperienceSkills.map((relationship, index) => {
 306:               const information = getRelationshipInformation(
 307:                 relationship,
 308:                 skillsById,
 309:               );
 310: 
 311:               const relationshipError =
 312:                 fieldErrors[`skills.${index}.skillId`] ||
 313:                 fieldErrors[`skills.${index}.nameSnapshot`];
 314: 
 315:               const levelError = fieldErrors[`skills.${index}.level`];
 316: 
 317:               const usageError =
 318:                 fieldErrors[`skills.${index}.usageDescription`];
 319: 
 320:               const itemHasError = Boolean(
 321:                 relationshipError || levelError || usageError,
 322:               );
 323: 
 324:               return (
 325:                 <article
 326:                   key={relationship.id}
 327:                   className={`experience-skills-selected-item ${
 328:                     itemHasError ? "experience-skills-selected-item--error" : ""
 329:                   }`}
 330:                 >
 331:                   <div className="experience-skills-order">
 332:                     <span>{String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
 333: 
 334:                     <div>
 335:                       <button
 336:                         type="button"
 337:                         onClick={() => handleMove(relationship.id, "up")}
 338:                         disabled={isWorking || index === 0}
 339:                         aria-label={`Move ${information.name} up`}
 340:                       >
 341:                         â†‘
 342:                       </button>
 343: 
 344:                       <button
 345:                         type="button"
 346:                         onClick={() => handleMove(relationship.id, "down")}
 347:                         disabled={
 348:                           isWorking ||
 349:                           index === orderedExperienceSkills.length - 1
 350:                         }
 351:                         aria-label={`Move ${information.name} down`}
 352:                       >
 353:                         â†“
 354:                       </button>
 355:                     </div>
 356:                   </div>
 357: 
 358:                   <div className="experience-skills-selected-content">
 359:                     <div className="experience-skills-selected-labels">
 360:                       <span>{getSkillCategoryLabel(information.category)}</span>
 361: 
 362:                       <span>{getSkillTypeLabel(information.type)}</span>
 363: 
 364:                       {information.isArchived && (
 365:                         <span className="experience-skills-archived-label">
 366:                           Library Skill Archived
 367:                         </span>
 368:                       )}
 369: 
 370:                       {!information.isConnected && (
 371:                         <span className="experience-skills-snapshot-label">
 372:                           Snapshot Only
 373:                         </span>
 374:                       )}
 375:                     </div>
 376: 
 377:                     <h5>{information.name}</h5>
 378: 
 379:                     <small>
 380:                       {information.isConnected
 381:                         ? "Connected to the central Skill Library"
 382:                         : "The central record is unavailable; the saved name snapshot is being displayed."}
 383:                     </small>
 384: 
 385:                     {relationshipError && (
 386:                       <p role="alert">{relationshipError}</p>
 387:                     )}
 388:                   </div>
 389: 
 390:                   <div className="experience-skills-relationship-fields">
 391:                     <div>
 392:                       <label htmlFor={`${relationship.id}-level`}>
 393:                         Proficiency in This Role
 394:                       </label>
 395: 
 396:                       <select
 397:                         id={`${relationship.id}-level`}
 398:                         value={relationship.level || ""}
 399:                         onChange={(event) =>
 400:                           updateRelationship(relationship.id, {
 401:                             level: event.target.value,
 402:                           })
 403:                         }
 404:                         disabled={isWorking}
 405:                         aria-invalid={Boolean(levelError)}
 406:                       >
 407:                         {SKILL_PROFICIENCY_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
 408:                           <option
 409:                             key={option.value || "unspecified"}
 410:                             value={option.value}
 411:                           >
 412:                             {option.label}
 413:                           </option>
 414:                         ))}
 415:                       </select>
 416: 
 417:                       {levelError && <small role="alert">{levelError}</small>}
 418:                     </div>
 419: 
 420:                     <div>
 421:                       <label htmlFor={`${relationship.id}-usage`}>
 422:                         How This Skill Was Used
 423:                         <span>Optional</span>
 424:                       </label>
 425: 
 426:                       <textarea
 427:                         id={`${relationship.id}-usage`}
 428:                         value={relationship.usageDescription || ""}
 429:                         onChange={(event) =>
 430:                           updateRelationship(relationship.id, {
 431:                             usageDescription: event.target.value,
 432:                           })
 433:                         }
 434:                         rows={3}
 435:                         maxLength={
 436:                           EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillUsageDescription
 437:                         }
 438:                         disabled={isWorking}
 439:                         placeholder="Example: Used Active Directory to manage accounts, permissions, and Group Policy."
 440:                         aria-invalid={Boolean(usageError)}
 441:                       />
 442: 
 443:                       <div className="experience-skills-usage-meta">
 444:                         <span>
 445:                           {(relationship.usageDescription || "").length}/
 446:                           {EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillUsageDescription}
 447:                         </span>
 448:                       </div>
 449: 
 450:                       {usageError && <small role="alert">{usageError}</small>}
 451:                     </div>
 452:                   </div>
 453: 
 454:                   <button
 455:                     type="button"
 456:                     className="experience-skills-remove-button"
 457:                     onClick={() => removeRelationship(relationship.id)}
 458:                     disabled={isWorking}
 459:                   >
 460:                     Remove
 461:                   </button>
 462:                 </article>
 463:               );
 464:             })}
 465:           </div>
 466:         ) : (
 467:           <div className="experience-skills-empty">
 468:             <span aria-hidden="true">âœ¦</span>
 469: 
 470:             <h4>No skills selected</h4>
 471: 
 472:             <p>
 473:               Search the central Skill Library, create a skill, or generate
 474:               supported AI suggestions.
 475:             </p>
 476:           </div>
 477:         )}
 478:       </section>
 479:     </section>
 480:   );
 481: }
 482: 
 483: export default ExperienceSkillsEditor;
