  24: function getResponsibilityText(value) {
  25:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  26:     return value.trim();
  27:   }
  28: 
  29:   return value?.text?.trim() || "";
  30: }
  31: 
  32: function normalizeAchievement(value) {
  33:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  34:     return {
  35:       text: value.trim(),
  36:       metric: "",
  37:     };
  38:   }
  39: 
  40:   return {
  41:     text: value?.text?.trim() || "",
  42:     metric: value?.metric?.trim() || "",
  43:   };
  44: }
  45: 
  46: function getRelationshipName(relationship, skillsById) {
  47:   return (
  48:     skillsById.get(relationship.skillId)?.name ||
  49:     relationship.nameSnapshot ||
  50:     ""
  51:   );
  52: }
  53: 
  54: function normalizeRelationshipOrder(relationships) {
  55:   return relationships.map((relationship, index) => ({
  56:     ...relationship,
  57:     order: index,
  58:   }));
  59: }
  60: 
  61: /*
  62:  * =========================================
  63:  * Experience Skill Suggestions
  64:  * =========================================
  65:  */
  66: 
  67: function ExperienceSkillSuggestions({
  68:   experience = {},
  69:   experienceSkills = [],
  70:   maximumExperienceSkills = 50,
  71:   disabled = false,
  72:   onChange,
  73: }) {
  74:   const { skillsById, findOrCreateSkill } = useSkillData();
  75: 
  76:   const abortControllerRef = useRef(null);
  77: 
  78:   const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState([]);
  79: 
  80:   const [selectedNames, setSelectedNames] = useState(() => new Set());
  81: 
  82:   const [warnings, setWarnings] = useState([]);
  83: 
  84:   const [status, setStatus] = useState("idle");
  85: 
  86:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
  87: 
  88:   /*
  89:    * =========================================
  90:    * Attached Skill Information
  91:    * =========================================
  92:    */
  93: 
  94:   const attachedSkillIds = useMemo(
  95:     () =>
  96:       new Set(
  97:         experienceSkills
  98:           .map((relationship) => relationship.skillId)
  99:           .filter(Boolean),
 100:       ),
 101:     [experienceSkills],
 102:   );
 103: 
 104:   const attachedSkillNames = useMemo(
 105:     () =>
 106:       new Set(
 107:         experienceSkills
 108:           .map((relationship) =>
 109:             normalizeSkillNameForComparison(
 110:               getRelationshipName(relationship, skillsById),
 111:             ),
 112:           )
 113:           .filter(Boolean),
 114:       ),
 115:     [experienceSkills, skillsById],
 116:   );
 117: 
 118:   const existingSkillNames = useMemo(
 119:     () =>
 120:       experienceSkills
 121:         .map((relationship) => getRelationshipName(relationship, skillsById))
 122:         .filter(Boolean),
 123:     [experienceSkills, skillsById],
 124:   );
 125: 
 126:   /*
 127:    * =========================================
 128:    * Generation Readiness
 129:    * =========================================
 130:    */
 131: 
 132:   const generationContext = useMemo(
 133:     () => ({
 134:       positionTitle: experience.position?.title || "",
 135: 
 136:       organizationName: experience.organization?.name || "",
 137: 
 138:       organizationIndustry: experience.organization?.industry || "",
 139: 
 140:       experienceCategory: experience.category || "",
 141: 
 142:       employmentType: experience.position?.employmentType || "",
 143: 
 144:       workArrangement: experience.position?.workArrangement || "",
 145: 
 146:       overview: experience.overview || "",
 147: 
 148:       responsibilities: (experience.responsibilities || [])
 149:         .map(getResponsibilityText)
 150:         .filter(Boolean),
 151: 
 152:       achievements: (experience.achievements || [])
 153:         .map(normalizeAchievement)
 154:         .filter((achievement) => achievement.text),
 155: 
 156:       leadership: {
 157:         hasLeadershipResponsibilities:
 158:           experience.leadership?.hasLeadershipResponsibilities === true,
 159: 
 160:         peopleManaged:
 161:           experience.leadership?.peopleManaged === "" ||
 162:           experience.leadership?.peopleManaged === undefined
 163:             ? null
 164:             : Number(experience.leadership?.peopleManaged),
 165: 
 166:         description: experience.leadership?.description || "",
 167:       },
 168: 
 169:       existingSkills: existingSkillNames,
 170: 
 171:       technologies: (experience.technologies || [])
 172:         .map(
 173:           (technology) => technology?.name || technology?.value || technology,
 174:         )
 175:         .filter(Boolean),
 176: 
 177:       generationRules: {
 178:         maximumSuggestions: 8,
 179:         excludeExistingSkills: true,
 180:         useOnlySupportedEvidence: true,
 181:       },
 182:     }),
 183:     [experience, existingSkillNames],
 184:   );
 185: 
 186:   const canGenerate = Boolean(
 187:     generationContext.positionTitle ||
 188:     generationContext.overview ||
 189:     generationContext.responsibilities.length > 0 ||
 190:     generationContext.achievements.length > 0 ||
 191:     generationContext.technologies.length > 0 ||
 192:     generationContext.leadership.description,
 193:   );
 194: 
 195:   const isGenerating = status === "generating";
 196: 
 197:   const isAccepting = status === "accepting";
 198: 
 199:   const isWorking = disabled || isGenerating || isAccepting;
 200: 
 201:   /*
 202:    * =========================================
 203:    * Cleanup Pending Request
 204:    * =========================================
 205:    */
 206: 
 207:   useEffect(() => {
 208:     return () => {
 209:       abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
 210:     };
 211:   }, []);
 212: 
 213:   /*
 214:    * =========================================
 215:    * Generate Suggestions
 216:    * =========================================
 217:    */
 218: 
 219:   const handleGenerate = async () => {
 220:     if (!canGenerate || isWorking) {
 221:       return;
 222:     }
 223: 
 224:     abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
 225: 
 226:     const controller = new AbortController();
 227: 
 228:     abortControllerRef.current = controller;
 229: 
 230:     try {
 231:       setStatus("generating");
 232:       setError("");
 233:       setWarnings([]);
 234:       setSelectedNames(new Set());
 235: 
 236:       const response = await fetch(API_ENDPOINTS.generateExperienceSkills, {
 237:         method: "POST",
 238: 
 239:         headers: {
 240:           "Content-Type": "application/json",
 241:         },
 242: 
 243:         body: JSON.stringify(generationContext),
 244: 
 245:         signal: controller.signal,
 246:       });
 247: 
 248:       const responseData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
 249: 
 250:       if (!response.ok) {
 251:         throw new Error(
 252:           responseData.message || "The skill-suggestion request failed.",
 253:         );
 254:       }
 255: 
 256:       const responseSuggestions = Array.isArray(responseData.suggestions)
 257:         ? responseData.suggestions
 258:         : [];
 259: 
 260:       const seenNames = new Set();
 261: 
 262:       const usableSuggestions = responseSuggestions.filter((suggestion) => {
 263:         const normalizedName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(
 264:           suggestion?.name,
 265:         );
 266: 
 267:         if (
 268:           !normalizedName ||
 269:           attachedSkillNames.has(normalizedName) ||
 270:           seenNames.has(normalizedName)
 271:         ) {
 272:           return false;
 273:         }
 274: 
 275:         seenNames.add(normalizedName);
 276: 
 277:         return Boolean(suggestion.reason);
 278:       });
 279: 
 280:       if (usableSuggestions.length === 0) {
 281:         throw new Error(
 282:           "The AI did not return any new supported skill suggestions.",
 283:         );
 284:       }
 285: 
 286:       setSuggestions(usableSuggestions);
 287: 
 288:       setWarnings(
 289:         Array.isArray(responseData.warnings) ? responseData.warnings : [],
 290:       );
 291: 
 292:       setStatus("success");
 293:     } catch (generationError) {
 294:       if (generationError.name === "AbortError") {
 295:         return;
 296:       }
 297: 
 298:       console.error("Unable to generate experience skills:", generationError);
 299: 
 300:       setSuggestions([]);
 301:       setSelectedNames(new Set());
 302:       setStatus("error");
 303: 
 304:       setError(
 305:         generationError.message || "Skill suggestions could not be generated.",
 306:       );
 307:     } finally {
 308:       if (abortControllerRef.current === controller) {
 309:         abortControllerRef.current = null;
 310:       }
 311:     }
 312:   };
 313: 
 314:   /*
 315:    * =========================================
 316:    * Selection
 317:    * =========================================
 318:    */
 319: 
 320:   const handleSelectionChange = (skillName, checked) => {
 321:     const normalizedName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(skillName);
 322: 
 323:     setSelectedNames((currentNames) => {
 324:       const nextNames = new Set(currentNames);
 325: 
 326:       if (checked) {
 327:         nextNames.add(normalizedName);
 328:       } else {
 329:         nextNames.delete(normalizedName);
 330:       }
 331: 
 332:       return nextNames;
 333:     });
 334:   };
 335: 
 336:   const selectableSuggestions = useMemo(
 337:     () =>
 338:       suggestions.filter(
 339:         (suggestion) =>
 340:           !attachedSkillNames.has(
 341:             normalizeSkillNameForComparison(suggestion.name),
 342:           ),
 343:       ),
 344:     [suggestions, attachedSkillNames],
 345:   );
 346: 
 347:   const allSuggestionsSelected =
 348:     selectableSuggestions.length > 0 &&
 349:     selectableSuggestions.every((suggestion) =>
 350:       selectedNames.has(normalizeSkillNameForComparison(suggestion.name)),
 351:     );
 352: 
 353:   const handleSelectAll = () => {
 354:     if (allSuggestionsSelected) {
 355:       setSelectedNames(new Set());
 356: 
 357:       return;
 358:     }
 359: 
 360:     setSelectedNames(
 361:       new Set(
 362:         selectableSuggestions.map((suggestion) =>
 363:           normalizeSkillNameForComparison(suggestion.name),
 364:         ),
 365:       ),
 366:     );
 367:   };
 368: 
 369:   /*
 370:    * =========================================
 371:    * Accept Suggestions
 372:    * =========================================
 373:    */
 374: 
 375:   const acceptSuggestions = async (suggestionsToAccept) => {
 376:     if (suggestionsToAccept.length === 0 || isWorking) {
 377:       return;
 378:     }
 379: 
 380:     const availableSlots = maximumExperienceSkills - experienceSkills.length;
 381: 
 382:     if (availableSlots <= 0) {
 383:       setStatus("error");
 384:       setError(
 385:         `This experience already contains the maximum of ${maximumExperienceSkills} skills.`,
 386:       );
 387: 
 388:       return;
 389:     }
 390: 
 391:     try {
 392:       setStatus("accepting");
 393:       setError("");
 394: 
 395:       const acceptedRelationships = [];
 396: 
 397:       /*
 398:        * Process sequentially so multiple new skills
 399:        * cannot overwrite one another in temporary
 400:        * browser storage.
 401:        */
 402: 
 403:       for (const suggestion of suggestionsToAccept.slice(0, availableSlots)) {
 404:         const normalizedName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(suggestion.name);
 405: 
 406:         if (attachedSkillNames.has(normalizedName)) {
 407:           continue;
 408:         }
 409: 
 410:         const result = await findOrCreateSkill({
 411:           name: suggestion.name,
 412:           category: suggestion.category || "other",
 413:           type: suggestion.type || "professional",
 414:           source: "ai-suggested",
 415:           sourceContext: "ai-experience-suggestion",
 416:         });
 417: 
 418:         const skill = result.skill;
 419: 
 420:         if (
 421:           attachedSkillIds.has(skill.id) ||
 422:           acceptedRelationships.some(
 423:             (relationship) => relationship.skillId === skill.id,
 424:           )
 425:         ) {
 426:           continue;
 427:         }
 428: 
 429:         acceptedRelationships.push(
 430:           createExperienceSkill({
 431:             skillId: skill.id,
 432:             nameSnapshot: skill.name,
 433:             level: "",
 434:             order: experienceSkills.length + acceptedRelationships.length,
 435:           }),
 436:         );
 437:       }
 438: 
 439:       if (acceptedRelationships.length === 0) {
 440:         throw new Error(
 441:           "The selected skills were already attached to this experience.",
 442:         );
 443:       }
 444: 
 445:       onChange?.(
 446:         normalizeRelationshipOrder([
 447:           ...experienceSkills,
 448:           ...acceptedRelationships,
 449:         ]),
 450:       );
 451: 
 452:       const acceptedNames = new Set(
 453:         suggestionsToAccept.map((suggestion) =>
 454:           normalizeSkillNameForComparison(suggestion.name),
 455:         ),
 456:       );
 457: 
 458:       setSuggestions((currentSuggestions) =>
 459:         currentSuggestions.filter(
 460:           (suggestion) =>
 461:             !acceptedNames.has(
 462:               normalizeSkillNameForComparison(suggestion.name),
 463:             ),
 464:         ),
 465:       );
 466: 
 467:       setSelectedNames(new Set());
 468:       setStatus("accepted");
 469:     } catch (acceptanceError) {
 470:       console.error("Unable to accept skill suggestions:", acceptanceError);
 471: 
 472:       setStatus("error");
 473: 
 474:       setError(
 475:         acceptanceError.publicMessage ||
 476:           acceptanceError.message ||
 477:           "The selected skills could not be added.",
 478:       );
 479:     }
 480:   };
 481: 
 482:   const handleAcceptOne = (suggestion) => {
 483:     acceptSuggestions([suggestion]);
 484:   };
 485: 
 486:   const handleAcceptSelected = () => {
 487:     const suggestionsToAccept = selectableSuggestions.filter((suggestion) =>
 488:       selectedNames.has(normalizeSkillNameForComparison(suggestion.name)),
 489:     );
 490: 
 491:     acceptSuggestions(suggestionsToAccept);
 492:   };
 493: 
 494:   /*
 495:    * =========================================
 496:    * Dismiss
 497:    * =========================================
 498:    */
 499: 
 500:   const handleDismiss = () => {
 501:     abortControllerRef.current?.abort();
 502: 
 503:     setSuggestions([]);
 504:     setSelectedNames(new Set());
 505:     setWarnings([]);
 506:     setError("");
 507:     setStatus("idle");
 508:   };
 509: 
 510:   return (
 511:     <section className="experience-skill-suggestions">
 512:       <header className="experience-skill-suggestions-header">
 513:         <div>
 514:           <span>AI Assistance</span>
 515: 
 516:           <h4>Skill Suggestions</h4>
 517: 
 518:           <p>
 519:             Generate skills supported by the overview, responsibilities,
 520:             achievements, technologies, and leadership information entered
 521:             above.
 522:           </p>
 523:         </div>
 524: 
 525:         <button
 526:           type="button"
 527:           className="experience-skill-generate-button"
 528:           onClick={handleGenerate}
 529:           disabled={!canGenerate || isWorking}
 530:         >
 531:           {isGenerating
 532:             ? "Generating..."
 533:             : suggestions.length > 0
 534:               ? "Regenerate"
 535:               : "Generate Skills"}
 536:         </button>
 537:       </header>
 538: 
 539:       {!canGenerate && (
 540:         <p className="experience-skill-generation-help">
 541:           Add a position title, overview, responsibility, achievement,
 542:           technology, or leadership description before generating suggestions.
 543:         </p>
 544:       )}
 545: 
 546:       {isGenerating && (
 547:         <div
 548:           className="experience-skill-loading"
 549:           role="status"
 550:           aria-live="polite"
 551:         >
 552:           <span aria-hidden="true" />
 553: 
 554:           <div>
 555:             <strong>Identifying supported skills</strong>
 556: 
 557:             <p>
 558:               The AI is reviewing this experience for evidence of professional
 559:               capabilities.
 560:             </p>
 561:           </div>
 562:         </div>
 563:       )}
 564: 
 565:       {error && (
 566:         <div className="experience-skill-error" role="alert">
 567:           <div>
 568:             <strong>Unable to process suggestions</strong>
 569: 
 570:             <p>{error}</p>
 571:           </div>
 572: 
 573:           <button type="button" onClick={() => setError("")}>
 574:             Dismiss
 575:           </button>
 576:         </div>
 577:       )}
 578: 
 579:       {status === "accepted" && (
 580:         <div className="experience-skill-accepted" role="status">
 581:           Accepted skills were added to this experience and your central Skill
 582:           Library.
 583:         </div>
 584:       )}
 585: 
 586:       {warnings.length > 0 && (
 587:         <div className="experience-skill-warnings">
 588:           <strong>Generation notes</strong>
 589: 
 590:           <ul>
 591:             {warnings.map((warning, index) => (
 592:               <li key={`${warning}-${index}`}>{warning}</li>
 593:             ))}
 594:           </ul>
 595:         </div>
 596:       )}
 597: 
 598:       {selectableSuggestions.length > 0 && (
 599:         <>
 600:           <div className="experience-skill-suggestion-toolbar">
 601:             <label>
 602:               <input
 603:                 type="checkbox"
 604:                 checked={allSuggestionsSelected}
 605:                 onChange={handleSelectAll}
 606:                 disabled={isWorking}
 607:               />
 608: 
 609:               <span>Select all suggestions</span>
 610:             </label>
 611: 
 612:             <div>
 613:               <button
 614:                 type="button"
 615:                 onClick={handleAcceptSelected}
 616:                 disabled={isWorking || selectedNames.size === 0}
 617:               >
 618:                 {isAccepting
 619:                   ? "Adding..."
 620:                   : `Add Selected (${selectedNames.size})`}
 621:               </button>
 622: 
 623:               <button
 624:                 type="button"
 625:                 onClick={handleDismiss}
 626:                 disabled={isAccepting}
 627:               >
 628:                 Dismiss
 629:               </button>
 630:             </div>
 631:           </div>
 632: 
 633:           <div className="experience-skill-suggestion-list">
 634:             {selectableSuggestions.map((suggestion) => {
 635:               const normalizedName = normalizeSkillNameForComparison(
 636:                 suggestion.name,
 637:               );
 638: 
 639:               const isSelected = selectedNames.has(normalizedName);
 640: 
 641:               return (
 642:                 <article
 643:                   key={normalizedName}
 644:                   className={`experience-skill-suggestion-card ${
 645:                     isSelected
 646:                       ? "experience-skill-suggestion-card--selected"
 647:                       : ""
 648:                   }`}
 649:                 >
 650:                   <label className="experience-skill-suggestion-select">
 651:                     <input
 652:                       type="checkbox"
 653:                       checked={isSelected}
 654:                       onChange={(event) =>
 655:                         handleSelectionChange(
 656:                           suggestion.name,
 657:                           event.target.checked,
 658:                         )
 659:                       }
 660:                       disabled={isWorking}
 661:                     />
 662: 
 663:                     <span className="sr-only">Select {suggestion.name}</span>
 664:                   </label>
 665: 
 666:                   <div className="experience-skill-suggestion-content">
 667:                     <div className="experience-skill-suggestion-tags">
 668:                       <span>{getSkillCategoryLabel(suggestion.category)}</span>
 669: 
 670:                       <span>{getSkillTypeLabel(suggestion.type)}</span>
 671:                     </div>
 672: 
 673:                     <h5>{suggestion.name}</h5>
 674: 
 675:                     <div className="experience-skill-evidence">
 676:                       <strong>Supporting evidence</strong>
 677: 
 678:                       <p>{suggestion.reason}</p>
 679:                     </div>
 680:                   </div>
 681: 
 682:                   <button
 683:                     type="button"
 684:                     className="experience-skill-accept-button"
 685:                     onClick={() => handleAcceptOne(suggestion)}
 686:                     disabled={isWorking}
 687:                   >
 688:                     Add Skill
 689:                   </button>
 690:                 </article>
 691:               );
 692:             })}
 693:           </div>
 694:         </>
 695:       )}
 696:     </section>
 697:   );
 698: }
 699:

   1: import { useId, useMemo } from "react";
   2:

  20:  * Relationship Helpers

  22:  */
  23: 
  24: function normalizeTechnologyOrder(technologies) {
  25:   return technologies.map((technology, index) => ({
  26:     ...technology,
  27:     order: index,
  28:   }));
  29: }
  30: 
  31: function getTechnologySkillId(technology) {
  32:   return technology?.skillId || technology?.profileSkillId || "";
  33: }
  34: 
  35: function getTechnologyName(technology, librarySkill) {
  36:   return (
  37:     librarySkill?.name ||
  38:     technology?.nameSnapshot ||
  39:     technology?.name ||
  40:     "Unavailable Technology"
  41:   );
  42: }
  43: 
  44: /*
  45:  * =========================================
  46:  * Experience Technology Editor
  47:  * =========================================
  48:  */
  49: 
  50: function ExperienceTechnologyEditor({
  51:   technologies = [],
  52:   fieldErrors = {},
  53:   disabled = false,
  54:   onChange,
  55: }) {
  56:   const sectionTitleId = useId();
  57: 
  58:   const {
  59:     activeSkills,
  60:     skillsById,
  61:     isLoading: areSkillsLoading,
  62:     findOrCreateSkill,
  63:   } = useSkillData();
  64: 
  65:   const maximumItems = EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumTechnologies;
  66: 
  67:   const isWorking = disabled || areSkillsLoading;
  68: 
  69:   /*
  70:    * =========================================
  71:    * Ordered Relationships
  72:    * =========================================
  73:    */
  74: 
  75:   const orderedTechnologies = useMemo(
  76:     () =>
  77:       [...technologies].sort(
  78:         (first, second) => (first.order ?? 0) - (second.order ?? 0),
  79:       ),
  80:     [technologies],
  81:   );
  82: 
  83:   const selectedSkillIds = useMemo(
  84:     () => orderedTechnologies.map(getTechnologySkillId).filter(Boolean),
  85:     [orderedTechnologies],
  86:   );
  87: 
  88:   const hasReachedMaximum = orderedTechnologies.length >= maximumItems;
  89: 
  90:   /*
  91:    * =========================================
  92:    * Add Existing Library Skill
  93:    * =========================================
  94:    */
  95: 
  96:   const handleSelectSkill = (skill) => {
  97:     if (!skill?.id || hasReachedMaximum) {
  98:       return;
  99:     }
 100: 
 101:     const alreadySelected = technologies.some(
 102:       (technology) => getTechnologySkillId(technology) === skill.id,
 103:     );
 104: 
 105:     if (alreadySelected) {
 106:       return;
 107:     }
 108: 
 109:     const relationship = createExperienceTechnology({
 110:       skillId: skill.id,
 111: 
 112:       nameSnapshot: skill.name,
 113:       name: skill.name,
 114: 
 115:       category: skill.category,
 116: 
 117:       proficiency: "",
 118: 
 119:       usageDescription: "",
 120: 
 121:       order: technologies.length,
 122:     });
 123: 
 124:     onChange?.(normalizeTechnologyOrder([...technologies, relationship]));
 125:   };
 126: 
 127:   /*
 128:    * =========================================
 129:    * Create Central Skill
 130:    * =========================================
 131:    */
 132: 
 133:   const handleCreateSkill = async (skillData) => {
 134:     const result = await findOrCreateSkill(
 135:       {
 136:         ...skillData,
 137: 
 138:         category: skillData.category || "tools-platforms",
 139: 
 140:         type: skillData.type || "tool",
 141: 
 142:         source: skillData.source || "manual",
 143: 
 144:         sourceContext:
 145:           skillData.sourceContext || "experience-technology-editor",
 146:       },
 147:       {
 148:         restoreArchived: true,
 149:       },
 150:     );
 151: 
 152:     if (result?.skill) {
 153:       handleSelectSkill(result.skill);
 154:     }
 155: 
 156:     return result;
 157:   };
 158: 
 159:   /*
 160:    * =========================================
 161:    * Relationship Updates
 162:    * =========================================
 163:    */
 164: 
 165:   const handleRelationshipChange = (relationshipId, updates) => {
 166:     const updatedTechnologies = technologies.map((technology) =>
 167:       technology.id === relationshipId
 168:         ? {
 169:             ...technology,
 170:             ...updates,
 171:           }
 172:         : technology,
 173:     );
 174: 
 175:     onChange?.(normalizeTechnologyOrder(updatedTechnologies));
 176:   };
 177: 
 178:   const handleRemove = (relationshipId) => {
 179:     const remainingTechnologies = technologies.filter(
 180:       (technology) => technology.id !== relationshipId,
 181:     );
 182: 
 183:     onChange?.(normalizeTechnologyOrder(remainingTechnologies));
 184:   };
 185: 
 186:   /*
 187:    * =========================================
 188:    * Reordering
 189:    * =========================================
 190:    */
 191: 
 192:   const handleMove = (relationshipId, direction) => {
 193:     const currentIndex = orderedTechnologies.findIndex(
 194:       (technology) => technology.id === relationshipId,
 195:     );
 196: 
 197:     if (currentIndex === -1) {
 198:       return;
 199:     }
 200: 
 201:     const targetIndex =
 202:       direction === "up" ? currentIndex - 1 : currentIndex + 1;
 203: 
 204:     if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= orderedTechnologies.length) {
 205:       return;
 206:     }
 207: 
 208:     const reorderedTechnologies = [...orderedTechnologies];
 209: 
 210:     const [movedTechnology] = reorderedTechnologies.splice(currentIndex, 1);
 211: 
 212:     reorderedTechnologies.splice(targetIndex, 0, movedTechnology);
 213: 
 214:     onChange?.(normalizeTechnologyOrder(reorderedTechnologies));
 215:   };
 216: 
 217:   return (
 218:     <section
 219:       className="experience-form-section experience-technology-editor"
 220:       aria-labelledby={sectionTitleId}
 221:     >
 222:       <header className="experience-form-section-header">
 223:         <span aria-hidden="true" />
 224: 
 225:         <div>
 226:           <small>Tools and Technologies</small>
 227: 
 228:           <h3 id={sectionTitleId}>Technologies Used in This Experience</h3>
 229: 
 230:           <p>
 231:             Select tools, platforms, systems, and technologies from your central
 232:             Skill Library. You can also create a new library skill without
 233:             leaving the experience form.
 234:           </p>
 235:         </div>
 236:       </header>
 237: 
 238:       <SkillSelector
 239:         skills={activeSkills}
 240:         selectedSkillIds={selectedSkillIds}
 241:         maximumSelections={maximumItems}
 242:         disabled={isWorking || hasReachedMaximum}
 243:         title="Select Technologies and Tools"
 244:         description="Search the central Skill Library or create a tool that is not saved yet."
 245:         emptyMessage="No matching tools or technologies were found."
 246:         defaultNewSkillCategory="tools-platforms"
 247:         defaultNewSkillType="tool"
 248:         onSelectSkill={handleSelectSkill}
 249:         onCreateSkill={handleCreateSkill}
 250:       />
 251: 
 252:       {fieldErrors.technologies && (
 253:         <p className="experience-technology-editor-error" role="alert">
 254:           {fieldErrors.technologies}
 255:         </p>
 256:       )}
 257: 
 258:       <div className="experience-technology-editor-summary">
 259:         <span>Experience Technology Set</span>
 260: 
 261:         <strong>
 262:           {orderedTechnologies.length}/{maximumItems}
 263:         </strong>
 264:       </div>
 265: 
 266:       {orderedTechnologies.length > 0 ? (
 267:         <div className="experience-technology-editor-list">
 268:           {orderedTechnologies.map((relationship, index) => {
 269:             const skillId = getTechnologySkillId(relationship);
 270: 
 271:             const librarySkill = skillId ? skillsById.get(skillId) : null;
 272: 
 273:             const technologyName = getTechnologyName(
 274:               relationship,
 275:               librarySkill,
 276:             );
 277: 
 278:             const category =
 279:               librarySkill?.category ||
 280:               relationship.category ||
 281:               "tools-platforms";
 282: 
 283:             const isArchived = librarySkill?.status === "archived";
 284: 
 285:             const isUnavailable = Boolean(skillId) && !librarySkill;
 286: 
 287:             const relationshipError =
 288:               fieldErrors[`technologies.${index}.skillId`] ||
 289:               fieldErrors[`technologies.${index}.nameSnapshot`] ||
 290:               fieldErrors[`technologies.${index}.name`] ||
 291:               fieldErrors[`technologies.${index}.category`];
 292: 
 293:             const proficiencyError =
 294:               fieldErrors[`technologies.${index}.proficiency`];
 295: 
 296:             const usageDescriptionError =
 297:               fieldErrors[`technologies.${index}.usageDescription`];
 298: 
 299:             return (
 300:               <article
 301:                 key={relationship.id}
 302:                 className={`experience-technology-editor-item ${
 303:                   relationshipError || proficiencyError || usageDescriptionError
 304:                     ? "experience-technology-editor-item--error"
 305:                     : ""
 306:                 }`}
 307:               >
 308:                 <div className="experience-technology-editor-order">
 309:                   <span>{String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
 310: 
 311:                   <div>
 312:                     <button
 313:                       type="button"
 314:                       onClick={() => handleMove(relationship.id, "up")}
 315:                       disabled={isWorking || index === 0}
 316:                       aria-label={`Move ${technologyName} up`}
 317:                     >
 318:                       â†‘
 319:                     </button>
 320: 
 321:                     <button
 322:                       type="button"
 323:                       onClick={() => handleMove(relationship.id, "down")}
 324:                       disabled={
 325:                         isWorking || index === orderedTechnologies.length - 1
 326:                       }
 327:                       aria-label={`Move ${technologyName} down`}
 328:                     >
 329:                       â†“
 330:                     </button>
 331:                   </div>
 332:                 </div>
 333: 
 334:                 <div className="experience-technology-editor-content">
 335:                   <span>{getSkillCategoryLabel(category)}</span>
 336: 
 337:                   <strong>{technologyName}</strong>
 338: 
 339:                   <div className="experience-technology-editor-badges">
 340:                     {librarySkill && <small>Skill Library</small>}
 341: 
 342:                     {isArchived && (
 343:                       <small className="experience-technology-editor-badge--warning">
 344:                         Archived
 345:                       </small>
 346:                     )}
 347: 
 348:                     {isUnavailable && (
 349:                       <small className="experience-technology-editor-badge--warning">
 350:                         Library record unavailable
 351:                       </small>
 352:                     )}
 353: 
 354:                     {!skillId && (
 355:                       <small className="experience-technology-editor-badge--legacy">
 356:                         Legacy record
 357:                       </small>
 358:                     )}
 359:                   </div>
 360: 
 361:                   {relationshipError && (
 362:                     <small
 363:                       className="experience-technology-editor-field-error"
 364:                       role="alert"
 365:                     >
 366:                       {relationshipError}
 367:                     </small>
 368:                   )}
 369:                 </div>
 370: 
 371:                 <div className="experience-technology-editor-fields">
 372:                   <div>
 373:                     <label htmlFor={`${relationship.id}-proficiency`}>
 374:                       Proficiency in this experience
 375:                     </label>
 376: 
 377:                     <select
 378:                       id={`${relationship.id}-proficiency`}
 379:                       value={relationship.proficiency || ""}
 380:                       onChange={(event) =>
 381:                         handleRelationshipChange(relationship.id, {
 382:                           proficiency: event.target.value,
 383:                         })
 384:                       }
 385:                       disabled={isWorking}
 386:                     >
 387:                       {SKILL_PROFICIENCY_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
 388:                         <option
 389:                           key={option.value || "unspecified"}
 390:                           value={option.value}
 391:                         >
 392:                           {option.label}
 393:                         </option>
 394:                       ))}
 395:                     </select>
 396: 
 397:                     {proficiencyError && (
 398:                       <small role="alert">{proficiencyError}</small>
 399:                     )}
 400:                   </div>
 401: 
 402:                   <div>
 403:                     <label htmlFor={`${relationship.id}-usage`}>
 404:                       How it was used
 405:                       <small> Optional</small>
 406:                     </label>
 407: 
 408:                     <textarea
 409:                       id={`${relationship.id}-usage`}
 410:                       rows={3}
 411:                       value={relationship.usageDescription || ""}
 412:                       onChange={(event) =>
 413:                         handleRelationshipChange(relationship.id, {
 414:                           usageDescription: event.target.value,
 415:                         })
 416:                       }
 417:                       maxLength={
 418:                         EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyUsageDescription
 419:                       }
 420:                       disabled={isWorking}
 421:                       placeholder={`Example: Used ${technologyName} to automate deployment and monitor application health.`}
 422:                     />
 423: 
 424:                     <div className="experience-technology-editor-character-count">
 425:                       <span>{usageDescriptionError || ""}</span>
 426: 
 427:                       <small>
 428:                         {(relationship.usageDescription || "").length}/
 429:                         {EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyUsageDescription}
 430:                       </small>
 431:                     </div>
 432:                   </div>
 433:                 </div>
 434: 
 435:                 <button
 436:                   type="button"
 437:                   className="experience-technology-editor-remove-button"
 438:                   onClick={() => handleRemove(relationship.id)}
 439:                   disabled={isWorking}
 440:                   aria-label={`Remove ${technologyName} from this experience`}
 441:                 >
 442:                   Remove
 443:                 </button>
 444:               </article>
 445:             );
 446:           })}
 447:         </div>
 448:       ) : (
 449:         <div className="experience-technology-editor-empty">
 450:           <span aria-hidden="true">â—‡</span>
 451: 
 452:           <h4>No technologies selected</h4>
 453: 
 454:           <p>
 455:             Search the Skill Library and add the tools, systems, or technologies
 456:             used in this experience.
 457:           </p>
 458:         </div>
 459:       )}
 460:     </section>
 461:   );
 462: }
 463: 
 464: export default ExperienceTechnologyEditor;

   1: import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
   2:

  18: function Experience() {
  19:   const {
  20:     experiences,
  21:     activeExperiences,
  22:     archivedExperiences,
  23:     isLoading,
  24:     loadStatus,
  25:     saveStatus,
  26:     operation,
  27:     error,
  28:     refreshExperiences,
  29:     createExperience,
  30:     updateExperience,
  31:     archiveExperience,
  32:     restoreExperience,
  33:     deleteExperience,
  34:     clearError,
  35:     resetSaveStatus,
  36:   } = useExperienceData();
  37: 
  38:   const [isFormOpen, setIsFormOpen] = useState(false);
  39:   const [editingExperienceId, setEditingExperienceId] = useState(null);
  40:   const [showArchived, setShowArchived] = useState(false);
  41: 
  42:   /*
  43:    * =========================================
  44:    * Derived Information
  45:    * =========================================
  46:    */
  47: 
  48:   const statistics = useMemo(
  49:     () => getExperienceStatistics(experiences),
  50:     [experiences],
  51:   );
  52: 
  53:   /*
  54:    * ExperienceList is responsible for search,
  55:    * filtering, and sorting.
  56:    */
  57: 
  58:   const selectedExperiences = showArchived
  59:     ? archivedExperiences
  60:     : activeExperiences;
  61: 
  62:   const editingExperience = useMemo(
  63:     () =>
  64:       editingExperienceId
  65:         ? experiences.find(
  66:             (experience) => experience.id === editingExperienceId,
  67:           ) || null
  68:         : null,
  69:     [experiences, editingExperienceId],
  70:   );
  71: 
  72:   /*
  73:    * =========================================
  74:    * Form Actions
  75:    * =========================================
  76:    */
  77: 
  78:   const handleAddExperience = () => {
  79:     setEditingExperienceId(null);
  80:     setIsFormOpen(true);
  81: 
  82:     clearError();
  83:     resetSaveStatus();
  84: 
  85:     window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  86:       document.querySelector(".experience-page-form")?.scrollIntoView({
  87:         behavior: "smooth",
  88:         block: "start",
  89:       });
  90:     });
  91:   };
  92: 
  93:   const handleCloseForm = () => {
  94:     setEditingExperienceId(null);
  95:     setIsFormOpen(false);
  96: 
  97:     clearError();
  98:     resetSaveStatus();
  99:   };
 100: 
 101:   /*
 102:    * =========================================
 103:    * Save Experience
 104:    * =========================================
 105:    */
 106: 
 107:   const handleSaveExperience = async (experienceData) => {
 108:     if (editingExperienceId) {
 109:       await updateExperience(editingExperienceId, experienceData);
 110:     } else {
 111:       await createExperience(experienceData);
 112:     }
 113: 
 114:     setEditingExperienceId(null);
 115:     setIsFormOpen(false);
 116:   };
 117: 
 118:   /*
 119:    * =========================================
 120:    * Edit Experience
 121:    * =========================================
 122:    */
 123: 
 124:   const handleEditExperience = (experience) => {
 125:     setEditingExperienceId(experience.id);
 126:     setIsFormOpen(true);
 127: 
 128:     clearError();
 129:     resetSaveStatus();
 130: 
 131:     window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
 132:       document.querySelector(".experience-page-form")?.scrollIntoView({
 133:         behavior: "smooth",
 134:         block: "start",
 135:       });
 136:     });
 137:   };
 138: 
 139:   /*
 140:    * =========================================
 141:    * Archive Experience
 142:    * =========================================
 143:    */
 144: 
 145:   const handleArchiveExperience = async (experience) => {
 146:     try {
 147:       await archiveExperience(experience.id);
 148: 
 149:       if (editingExperienceId === experience.id) {
 150:         handleCloseForm();
 151:       }
 152:     } catch {
 153:       /*
 154:        * ExperienceDataContext displays the error.
 155:        */
 156:     }
 157:   };
 158: 
 159:   /*
 160:    * =========================================
 161:    * Restore Experience
 162:    * =========================================
 163:    */
 164: 
 165:   const handleRestoreExperience = async (experience) => {
 166:     try {
 167:       await restoreExperience(experience.id);
 168:     } catch {
 169:       /*
 170:        * ExperienceDataContext displays the error.
 171:        */
 172:     }
 173:   };
 174: 
 175:   /*
 176:    * =========================================
 177:    * Delete Experience
 178:    * =========================================
 179:    */
 180: 
 181:   const handleDeleteExperience = async (experience) => {
 182:     const positionTitle = getExperiencePositionTitle(experience);
 183: 
 184:     const organizationName = getExperienceOrganizationName(experience);
 185: 
 186:     const shouldDelete = window.confirm(
 187:       `Permanently delete "${positionTitle}" at "${organizationName}"?\n\n` +
 188:         "This action cannot be undone.",
 189:     );
 190: 
 191:     if (!shouldDelete) {
 192:       return;
 193:     }
 194: 
 195:     try {
 196:       await deleteExperience(experience.id);
 197: 
 198:       if (editingExperienceId === experience.id) {
 199:         handleCloseForm();
 200:       }
 201:     } catch {
 202:       /*
 203:        * ExperienceDataContext displays the error.
 204:        */
 205:     }
 206:   };
 207: 
 208:   /*
 209:    * =========================================
 210:    * Archive View

 212:    */
 213:

 215:     setShowArchived((currentValue) => !currentValue);
 216: 
 217:     setEditingExperienceId(null);
 218:     setIsFormOpen(false);
 219: 
 220:     clearError();

 222:   };

 233:     }).catch(() => {
 234:       /*
 235:        * The context already records and displays
 236:        * the loading error.
 237:        */
 238:     });
 239:   };

 250:       />

 256:           className="experience-page-message experience-page-message--error"
 257:           role="alert"
 258:         >
 259:           <div>
 260:             <strong>Unable to complete the experience operation</strong>
 261: 
 262:             <p>{error.message}</p>
 263:           </div>
 264: 
 265:           <button type="button" onClick={clearError}>
 266:             Dismiss
 267:           </button>
 268:         </section>
 269:       )}
 270: 
 271:       {saveStatus === "success" && (
 272:         <section
 273:           className="experience-page-message experience-page-message--success"
 274:           role="status"
 275:         >
 276:           <div>
 277:             <strong>Experience changes saved</strong>
 278: 
 279:             <p>
 280:               The professional experience library has been updated successfully.
 281:             </p>
 282:           </div>

 285:             Dismiss
 286:           </button>

 357:                 ? "Archived records are preserved but unavailable to normal rÃ©sumÃ© and portfolio selection."
 358:                 : "Manage the professional records available to your rÃ©sumÃ©s and portfolios."}

 360:           </div>
 361:

 417:                 <button type="button" onClick={handleAddExperience}>
 418:                   Add Your First Experience

 422:           )}
 423: 
 424:         {!isLoading &&
 425:           loadStatus !== "error" &&
 426:           selectedExperiences.length > 0 && (
 427:             <ExperienceList
 428:               key={showArchived ? "archived" : "active"}
 429:               experiences={selectedExperiences}
 430:               operation={operation}
 431:               onEdit={handleEditExperience}
 432:               onArchive={handleArchiveExperience}
 433:               onRestore={handleRestoreExperience}
 434:               onDelete={handleDeleteExperience}
 435:             />
 436:           )}
 437:       </section>
 438:     </main>
 439:   );
 440: }
 441: 
 442: export default Experience;

   1: import {
   2:   createExperience as createExperienceModel,
   3:   normalizeExperience,
   4:   updateExperienceModel,
   5: } from "../../models/experienceModel.js";

   8:   assertValidExperience,
   9:   validateExperience,
  10: } from "./experienceValidation.js";

  13:   readStoredExperiences,
  14:   replaceStoredExperiences,

  17: /*
  18:  * =========================================
  19:  * Service Errors
  20:  * =========================================
  21:  */
  22: 
  23: function createServiceError({ message, publicMessage, code, status = 500 }) {
  24:   const error = new Error(message);
  25: 
  26:   error.name = "ExperienceServiceError";
  27:   error.code = code;
  28:   error.status = status;
  29:   error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
  30: 
  31:   return error;
  32: }
  33: 
  34: function createNotFoundError(experienceId) {
  35:   return createServiceError({
  36:     message: `Experience "${experienceId}" was not found.`,
  37:     publicMessage: "The requested experience could not be found.",
  38:     code: "EXPERIENCE_NOT_FOUND",
  39:     status: 404,
  40:   });
  41: }
  42: 
  43: /*
  44:  * =========================================
  45:  * Simulated Async Operation
  46:  * =========================================
  47:  *
  48:  * The service methods are asynchronous now so
  49:  * components will not need to change when these
  50:  * operations become real API requests.
  51:  */
  52: 
  53: function resolveAsync(value) {
  54:   return Promise.resolve(value);
  55: }
  56: 
  57: /*
  58:  * =========================================
  59:  * Get All Experiences
  60:  * =========================================
  61:  */
  62: 
  63: export async function getExperiences({ includeArchived = false } = {}) {
  64:   const experiences = readStoredExperiences();
  65: 
  66:   const filteredExperiences = includeArchived
  67:     ? experiences
  68:     : experiences.filter((experience) => experience.status !== "archived");
  69: 
  70:   return resolveAsync(filteredExperiences);
  71: }
  72: 
  73: /*
  74:  * =========================================
  75:  * Get Experience By ID
  76:  * =========================================
  77:  */
  78: 
  79: export async function getExperienceById(experienceId) {
  80:   if (!experienceId) {
  81:     throw createServiceError({
  82:       message: "An experience ID is required to retrieve an experience.",
  83:       publicMessage: "The requested experience could not be identified.",
  84:       code: "EXPERIENCE_ID_REQUIRED",
  85:       status: 400,
  86:     });
  87:   }
  88: 
  89:   const experiences = readStoredExperiences();
  90: 
  91:   const experience = experiences.find((item) => item.id === experienceId);
  92: 
  93:   if (!experience) {
  94:     throw createNotFoundError(experienceId);
  95:   }
  96: 
  97:   return resolveAsync(experience);
  98: }
  99: 
 100: /*
 101:  * =========================================
 102:  * Create Experience
 103:  * =========================================
 104:  */
 105: 
 106: export async function createExperience(experienceData) {
 107:   /*
 108:    * Validate before normalization so invalid or
 109:    * oversized input is not silently truncated.
 110:    */
 111: 
 112:   assertValidExperience(experienceData);
 113: 
 114:   const experiences = readStoredExperiences();
 115: 
 116:   const experience = createExperienceModel(experienceData);
 117: 
 118:   const duplicateId = experiences.some((item) => item.id === experience.id);
 119: 
 120:   if (duplicateId) {
 121:     throw createServiceError({
 122:       message: `Experience ID "${experience.id}" already exists.`,
 123:       publicMessage:
 124:         "This experience could not be created because its identifier already exists.",
 125:       code: "EXPERIENCE_ID_CONFLICT",
 126:       status: 409,
 127:     });
 128:   }
 129: 
 130:   replaceStoredExperiences([experience, ...experiences]);
 131: 
 132:   return resolveAsync(experience);
 133: }
 134: 
 135: /*
 136:  * =========================================
 137:  * Update Experience
 138:  * =========================================
 139:  */
 140: 
 141: export async function updateExperience(experienceId, updates) {
 142:   if (!experienceId) {
 143:     throw createServiceError({
 144:       message: "An experience ID is required to update an experience.",
 145:       publicMessage: "The experience could not be identified.",
 146:       code: "EXPERIENCE_ID_REQUIRED",
 147:       status: 400,
 148:     });
 149:   }
 150: 
 151:   const experiences = readStoredExperiences();
 152: 
 153:   const experienceIndex = experiences.findIndex(
 154:     (experience) => experience.id === experienceId,
 155:   );
 156: 
 157:   if (experienceIndex === -1) {
 158:     throw createNotFoundError(experienceId);
 159:   }
 160: 
 161:   const currentExperience = experiences[experienceIndex];
 162: 
 163:   /*
 164:    * Merge the record before validating it because
 165:    * updates may include only part of the model.
 166:    */
 167: 
 168:   const mergedExperience = {
 169:     ...currentExperience,
 170:     ...updates,
 171: 
 172:     position: {
 173:       ...currentExperience.position,
 174:       ...updates?.position,
 175:     },
 176: 
 177:     organization: {
 178:       ...currentExperience.organization,
 179:       ...updates?.organization,
 180:     },
 181: 
 182:     location: {
 183:       ...currentExperience.location,
 184:       ...updates?.location,
 185:     },
 186: 
 187:     dates: {
 188:       ...currentExperience.dates,
 189:       ...updates?.dates,
 190:     },
 191: 
 192:     leadership: {
 193:       ...currentExperience.leadership,
 194:       ...updates?.leadership,
 195:     },
 196: 
 197:     privateInformation: {
 198:       ...currentExperience.privateInformation,
 199:       ...updates?.privateInformation,
 200:     },
 201: 
 202:     visibility: {
 203:       ...currentExperience.visibility,
 204:       ...updates?.visibility,
 205:     },
 206:   };
 207: 
 208:   assertValidExperience(mergedExperience);
 209: 
 210:   const updatedExperience = updateExperienceModel(currentExperience, updates);
 211: 
 212:   const nextExperiences = [...experiences];
 213: 
 214:   nextExperiences[experienceIndex] = updatedExperience;
 215: 
 216:   replaceStoredExperiences(nextExperiences);
 217: 
 218:   return resolveAsync(updatedExperience);
 219: }
 220: 
 221: /*
 222:  * =========================================
 223:  * Archive Experience
 224:  * =========================================
 225:  */
 226: 
 227: export async function archiveExperience(experienceId) {
 228:   return updateExperience(experienceId, {
 229:     status: "archived",
 230:   });
 231: }
 232: 
 233: /*
 234:  * =========================================
 235:  * Restore Experience
 236:  * =========================================
 237:  */
 238: 
 239: export async function restoreExperience(experienceId) {
 240:   return updateExperience(experienceId, {
 241:     status: "active",
 242:   });
 243: }
 244: 
 245: /*
 246:  * =========================================
 247:  * Delete Experience
 248:  * =========================================
 249:  */
 250: 
 251: export async function deleteExperience(experienceId) {
 252:   if (!experienceId) {
 253:     throw createServiceError({
 254:       message: "An experience ID is required to delete an experience.",
 255:       publicMessage: "The experience could not be identified.",
 256:       code: "EXPERIENCE_ID_REQUIRED",
 257:       status: 400,
 258:     });
 259:   }
 260: 
 261:   const experiences = readStoredExperiences();
 262: 
 263:   const experienceToDelete = experiences.find(
 264:     (experience) => experience.id === experienceId,
 265:   );
 266: 
 267:   if (!experienceToDelete) {
 268:     throw createNotFoundError(experienceId);
 269:   }
 270: 
 271:   const remainingExperiences = experiences.filter(
 272:     (experience) => experience.id !== experienceId,
 273:   );
 274: 
 275:   replaceStoredExperiences(remainingExperiences);
 276: 
 277:   return resolveAsync({
 278:     id: experienceId,
 279:     deleted: true,
 280:   });
 281: }
 282: 
 283: /*
 284:  * =========================================
 285:  * Validate Without Saving
 286:  * =========================================
 287:  */
 288: 
 289: export async function validateExperienceDraft(experienceData) {
 290:   return resolveAsync(validateExperience(experienceData));
 291: }
 292: 
 293: /*
 294:  * =========================================
 295:  * Replace Collection
 296:  * =========================================
 297:  *
 298:  * This is useful for future imports and migration.
 299:  * It should not normally be called from the form.
 300:  */
 301: 
 302: export async function replaceExperiences(experienceValues) {
 303:   if (!Array.isArray(experienceValues)) {
 304:     throw createServiceError({
 305:       message: "The replacement experience collection must be an array.",
 306:       publicMessage: "The experience collection has an invalid format.",
 307:       code: "INVALID_EXPERIENCE_COLLECTION",
 308:       status: 400,
 309:     });
 310:   }
 311: 
 312:   const normalizedExperiences = experienceValues.map((experience) => {
 313:     assertValidExperience(experience);
 314: 
 315:     return normalizeExperience(experience);
 316:   });
 317: 
 318:   const savedExperiences = replaceStoredExperiences(normalizedExperiences);
 319: 
 320:   return resolveAsync(savedExperiences);
 321: }

FILE: src\services\Experience\experienceStorage.js
===========================================================================
   1: import {
   2:   EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION,
   3:   normalizeExperienceCollection,
   4: } from "../../models/experienceModel.js";
   5: 
   6: /*
   7:  * =========================================
   8:  * Storage Configuration
   9:  * =========================================
  10:  */
  11: 
  12: export const EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_KEY = "portfolio-platform:experiences";
  13: 
  14: const EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_VERSION = 2;
  15: 
  16: /*
  17:  * =========================================
  18:  * Storage Error
  19:  * =========================================
  20:  */
  21: 
  22: function createStorageError(message, publicMessage, cause) {
  23:   const error = new Error(message);
  24: 
  25:   error.name = "ExperienceStorageError";
  26:   error.code = "EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_ERROR";
  27:   error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
  28: 
  29:   if (cause) {
  30:     error.cause = cause;
  31:   }
  32: 
  33:   return error;
  34: }
  35: 
  36: /*
  37:  * =========================================
  38:  * Browser Storage
  39:  * =========================================
  40:  */
  41: 
  42: function getBrowserStorage() {
  43:   if (typeof window === "undefined") {
  44:     throw createStorageError(
  45:       "Experience storage is unavailable outside the browser.",
  46:       "Experience storage is currently unavailable.",
  47:     );
  48:   }
  49: 
  50:   if (!window.localStorage) {
  51:     throw createStorageError(
  52:       "The browser does not provide local storage.",
  53:       "Your browser does not support local experience storage.",
  54:     );
  55:   }
  56: 
  57:   return window.localStorage;
  58: }
  59: 
  60: /*
  61:  * =========================================
  62:  * Empty Storage Document
  63:  * =========================================
  64:  */
  65: 
  66: function createEmptyStorageDocument() {
  67:   return {
  68:     storageVersion: EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_VERSION,
  69: 
  70:     experienceModelVersion: EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION,
  71: 
  72:     experiences: [],
  73: 
  74:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  75:   };
  76: }
  77: 
  78: /*
  79:  * =========================================
  80:  * Version 1 Relationship Migration
  81:  * =========================================
  82:  */
  83: 
  84: function migrateVersionOneSkill(skill, index) {
  85:   if (typeof skill === "string") {
  86:     return {
  87:       id: "",
  88:       skillId: "",
  89:       nameSnapshot: skill,
  90:       categorySnapshot: "",
  91:       typeSnapshot: "",
  92:       level: "",
  93:       usageDescription: "",
  94:       order: index,
  95:     };
  96:   }
  97: 
  98:   const source = skill && typeof skill === "object" ? skill : {};
  99: 
 100:   return {
 101:     ...source,
 102: 
 103:     skillId: source.skillId || source.profileSkillId || "",
 104: 
 105:     nameSnapshot:
 106:       source.nameSnapshot || source.name || source.label || source.value || "",
 107: 
 108:     categorySnapshot: source.categorySnapshot || source.category || "",
 109: 
 110:     typeSnapshot: source.typeSnapshot || source.type || "",
 111: 
 112:     level: source.level || source.proficiency || "",
 113: 
 114:     usageDescription: source.usageDescription || source.description || "",
 115: 
 116:     order:
 117:       Number.isInteger(source.order) && source.order >= 0
 118:         ? source.order
 119:         : index,
 120:   };
 121: }
 122: 
 123: function migrateVersionOneTechnology(technology, index) {
 124:   if (typeof technology === "string") {
 125:     return {
 126:       id: "",
 127:       skillId: "",
 128:       nameSnapshot: technology,
 129:       name: technology,
 130:       category: "",
 131:       proficiency: "",
 132:       usageDescription: "",
 133:       order: index,
 134:     };
 135:   }
 136: 
 137:   const source = technology && typeof technology === "object" ? technology : {};
 138: 
 139:   const nameSnapshot =
 140:     source.nameSnapshot || source.name || source.label || source.value || "";
 141: 
 142:   return {
 143:     ...source,
 144: 
 145:     skillId: source.skillId || source.profileSkillId || "",
 146: 
 147:     nameSnapshot,
 148: 
 149:     /*
 150:      * Retain the compatibility field while the
 151:      * frontend still supports version 1 records.
 152:      */
 153: 
 154:     name: source.name || nameSnapshot,
 155: 
 156:     category: source.category || source.categorySnapshot || "",
 157: 
 158:     proficiency: source.proficiency || source.level || "",
 159: 
 160:     usageDescription: source.usageDescription || source.description || "",
 161: 
 162:     order:
 163:       Number.isInteger(source.order) && source.order >= 0
 164:         ? source.order
 165:         : index,
 166:   };
 167: }
 168: 
 169: function migrateExperienceModelVersionOne(experience) {
 170:   const source = experience && typeof experience === "object" ? experience : {};
 171: 
 172:   return {
 173:     ...source,
 174: 
 175:     modelVersion: 2,
 176: 
 177:     skills: Array.isArray(source.skills)
 178:       ? source.skills.map(migrateVersionOneSkill)
 179:       : [],
 180: 
 181:     technologies: Array.isArray(source.technologies)
 182:       ? source.technologies.map(migrateVersionOneTechnology)
 183:       : [],
 184:   };
 185: }
 186: 
 187: /*
 188:  * =========================================
 189:  * Model Migration Pipeline
 190:  * =========================================
 191:  */
 192: 
 193: function migrateExperienceModel(experience) {
 194:   const source = experience && typeof experience === "object" ? experience : {};
 195: 
 196:   let migratedExperience = source;
 197: 
 198:   const sourceVersion = Number(source.modelVersion) || 1;
 199: 
 200:   if (sourceVersion < 2) {
 201:     migratedExperience = migrateExperienceModelVersionOne(migratedExperience);
 202:   }
 203: 
 204:   return migratedExperience;
 205: }
 206: 
 207: function migrateExperienceCollection(experiences) {
 208:   if (!Array.isArray(experiences)) {
 209:     return [];
 210:   }
 211: 
 212:   return experiences.map(migrateExperienceModel);
 213: }
 214: 
 215: /*
 216:  * =========================================
 217:  * Storage Document Migration
 218:  * =========================================
 219:  */
 220: 
 221: function migrateStorageDocument(document) {
 222:   const source = document && typeof document === "object" ? document : {};
 223: 
 224:   const experiences = migrateExperienceCollection(source.experiences);
 225: 
 226:   return {
 227:     storageVersion: EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_VERSION,
 228: 
 229:     experienceModelVersion: EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION,
 230: 
 231:     experiences: normalizeExperienceCollection(experiences),
 232: 
 233:     updatedAt:
 234:       typeof source.updatedAt === "string"
 235:         ? source.updatedAt
 236:         : new Date().toISOString(),
 237:   };
 238: }
 239: 
 240: /*
 241:  * =========================================
 242:  * Parse Storage Document
 243:  * =========================================
 244:  */
 245: 
 246: function parseStorageDocument(value) {
 247:   if (!value) {
 248:     return {
 249:       document: createEmptyStorageDocument(),
 250:       migrated: false,
 251:     };
 252:   }
 253: 
 254:   let parsedValue;
 255: 
 256:   try {
 257:     parsedValue = JSON.parse(value);
 258:   } catch (cause) {
 259:     throw createStorageError(
 260:       "Unable to parse stored experience data.",
 261:       "The saved experience data could not be read.",
 262:       cause,
 263:     );
 264:   }
 265: 
 266:   /*
 267:    * Legacy storage may contain the experience
 268:    * collection directly as an array.
 269:    */
 270: 
 271:   if (Array.isArray(parsedValue)) {
 272:     return {
 273:       document: migrateStorageDocument({
 274:         storageVersion: 1,
 275:         experienceModelVersion: 1,
 276:         experiences: parsedValue,
 277:       }),
 278: 
 279:       migrated: true,
 280:     };
 281:   }
 282: 
 283:   if (!parsedValue || typeof parsedValue !== "object") {
 284:     throw createStorageError(
 285:       "The stored experience document has an invalid structure.",
 286:       "The saved experience data has an invalid format.",
 287:     );
 288:   }
 289: 
 290:   const storageVersion = Number(parsedValue.storageVersion) || 1;
 291: 
 292:   const modelVersion = Number(parsedValue.experienceModelVersion) || 1;
 293: 
 294:   const requiresMigration =
 295:     storageVersion < EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_VERSION ||
 296:     modelVersion < EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION ||
 297:     parsedValue.experiences?.some(
 298:       (experience) =>
 299:         Number(experience?.modelVersion || 1) < EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION,
 300:     );
 301: 
 302:   if (requiresMigration) {
 303:     return {
 304:       document: migrateStorageDocument(parsedValue),
 305: 
 306:       migrated: true,
 307:     };
 308:   }
 309: 
 310:   return {
 311:     document: {
 312:       storageVersion: EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_VERSION,
 313: 
 314:       experienceModelVersion: EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION,
 315: 
 316:       experiences: normalizeExperienceCollection(parsedValue.experiences),
 317: 
 318:       updatedAt:
 319:         typeof parsedValue.updatedAt === "string"
 320:           ? parsedValue.updatedAt
 321:           : new Date().toISOString(),
 322:     },
 323: 
 324:     migrated: false,
 325:   };
 326: }
 327: 
 328: /*
 329:  * =========================================
 330:  * Read Storage
 331:  * =========================================
 332:  */
 333: 
 334: export function readExperienceStorage() {
 335:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
 336: 
 337:   try {
 338:     const storedValue = storage.getItem(EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_KEY);
 339: 
 340:     const { document, migrated } = parseStorageDocument(storedValue);
 341: 
 342:     /*
 343:      * Persist the upgraded representation so the
 344:      * migration does not run during every read.
 345:      */
 346: 
 347:     if (migrated) {
 348:       const migratedDocument = {
 349:         ...document,
 350:         updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 351:       };
 352: 
 353:       storage.setItem(EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(migratedDocument));
 354: 
 355:       return migratedDocument;
 356:     }
 357: 
 358:     return document;
 359:   } catch (error) {
 360:     if (error?.name === "ExperienceStorageError") {
 361:       throw error;
 362:     }
 363: 
 364:     throw createStorageError(
 365:       "Unable to read experience storage.",
 366:       "Your saved experiences could not be loaded.",
 367:       error,
 368:     );
 369:   }
 370: }
 371: 
 372: /*
 373:  * =========================================
 374:  * Write Storage
 375:  * =========================================
 376:  */
 377: 
 378: export function writeExperienceStorage(experiences) {
 379:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
 380: 
 381:   const storageDocument = {
 382:     storageVersion: EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_VERSION,
 383: 
 384:     experienceModelVersion: EXPERIENCE_MODEL_VERSION,
 385: 
 386:     experiences: normalizeExperienceCollection(experiences),
 387: 
 388:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 389:   };
 390: 
 391:   try {
 392:     storage.setItem(EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(storageDocument));
 393: 
 394:     return storageDocument;
 395:   } catch (error) {
 396:     throw createStorageError(
 397:       "Unable to write experience storage.",
 398:       "Your experiences could not be saved in this browser.",
 399:       error,
 400:     );
 401:   }
 402: }

 411:   return readExperienceStorage().experiences;
 412: }
 413: 
 414: export function replaceStoredExperiences(experiences) {
 415:   return writeExperienceStorage(experiences).experiences;
 416: }

 421:  * =========================================
 422:  */

 425:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
 426: 
 427:   try {
 428:     storage.removeItem(EXPERIENCE_STORAGE_KEY);
 429:   } catch (error) {
 430:     throw createStorageError(
 431:       "Unable to clear experience storage.",
 432:       "The saved experience data could not be cleared.",
 433:       error,
 434:     );
 435:   }

   8: /*
   9:  * =========================================
  10:  * Basic Helpers
  11:  * =========================================
  12:  */
  13: 
  14: function getText(value) {
  15:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
  16: }
  17: 
  18: function getArray(value) {
  19:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  20: }
  21: 
  22: function getObject(value) {
  23:   return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
  24:     ? value
  25:     : {};
  26: }

  28: function getSearchableText(value) {
  29:   return getText(value).toLocaleLowerCase();

  32: /*
  33:  * =========================================
  34:  * Date Parsing
  35:  * =========================================
  36:  */
  37: 
  38: export function parseExperienceDate(value) {
  39:   const text = getText(value);
  40: 
  41:   if (!text) {
  42:     return null;
  43:   }
  44: 
  45:   const match = text.match(
  46:     /^(\d{4})-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))?$/,
  47:   );
  48: 
  49:   if (!match) {
  50:     return null;
  51:   }
  52: 
  53:   const year = Number(match[1]);
  54:   const month = Number(match[2]);
  55:   const day = match[3] ? Number(match[3]) : 1;
  56: 
  57:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  58: 
  59:   /*
  60:    * Reject impossible complete dates, such as
  61:    * February 31.
  62:    */
  63: 
  64:   if (
  65:     match[3] &&
  66:     (date.getUTCFullYear() !== year ||
  67:       date.getUTCMonth() !== month - 1 ||
  68:       date.getUTCDate() !== day)
  69:   ) {
  70:     return null;
  71:   }
  72: 
  73:   return {
  74:     value: text,
  75:     date,
  76:     year,
  77:     month,
  78:     day,
  79:     hasDay: Boolean(match[3]),
  80:   };
  81: }
  82: 
  83: /*
  84:  * =========================================
  85:  * Date Formatting
  86:  * =========================================
  87:  */
  88: 
  89: export function formatExperienceDate(
  90:   value,
  91:   { locale = "en-US", includeDay = false, fallback = "" } = {},
  92: ) {
  93:   const parsedDate = parseExperienceDate(value);
  94: 
  95:   if (!parsedDate) {
  96:     return fallback;
  97:   }
  98: 
  99:   const formatOptions =
 100:     includeDay && parsedDate.hasDay
 101:       ? {
 102:           month: "short",
 103:           day: "numeric",
 104:           year: "numeric",
 105:           timeZone: "UTC",
 106:         }
 107:       : {
 108:           month: "short",
 109:           year: "numeric",
 110:           timeZone: "UTC",
 111:         };
 112: 
 113:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, formatOptions).format(parsedDate.date);
 114: }

 118:  * Date Range
 119:  * =========================================

 122: export function formatExperienceDateRange(experience, options = {}) {
 123:   const dates = getObject(experience?.dates);
 124: 
 125:   const startDate = formatExperienceDate(dates.startDate, options);
 126: 
 127:   const endDate = dates.isCurrent
 128:     ? "Present"
 129:     : formatExperienceDate(dates.endDate, options);
 130: 
 131:   if (startDate && endDate) {
 132:     return `${startDate} â€“ ${endDate}`;
 133:   }
 134: 
 135:   if (startDate) {
 136:     return startDate;
 137:   }
 138: 
 139:   if (endDate) {
 140:     return endDate;
 141:   }
 142: 
 143:   return "Dates not provided";
 144: }

 146: /*
 147:  * =========================================
 148:  * Duration Calculation
 149:  * =========================================
 150:  */
 151: 
 152: function getCurrentUtcDateParts() {
 153:   const currentDate = new Date();
 154: 
 155:   return {
 156:     year: currentDate.getUTCFullYear(),
 157:     month: currentDate.getUTCMonth() + 1,
 158:     day: currentDate.getUTCDate(),
 159:   };
 160: }
 161: 
 162: export function calculateExperienceDuration(experience) {
 163:   const dates = getObject(experience?.dates);
 164: 
 165:   const start = parseExperienceDate(dates.startDate);
 166: 
 167:   if (!start) {
 168:     return null;
 169:   }
 170: 
 171:   let end;
 172: 
 173:   if (dates.isCurrent) {
 174:     const current = getCurrentUtcDateParts();
 175: 
 176:     end = {
 177:       year: current.year,
 178:       month: current.month,
 179:       day: current.day,
 180:       hasDay: true,
 181:     };
 182:   } else {
 183:     end = parseExperienceDate(dates.endDate);
 184:   }
 185: 
 186:   if (!end) {
 187:     return null;
 188:   }
 189: 
 190:   let totalMonths = (end.year - start.year) * 12 + (end.month - start.month);
 191: 
 192:   /*
 193:    * If exact days are supplied and the ending day
 194:    * is earlier than the starting day, the final
 195:    * month has not been completed.
 196:    */
 197: 
 198:   if (start.hasDay && end.hasDay && end.day < start.day) {
 199:     totalMonths -= 1;
 200:   }
 201: 
 202:   /*
 203:    * Month-only employment dates represent calendar
 204:    * months. Count the ending month as part of the
 205:    * experience.
 206:    */
 207: 
 208:   if (!start.hasDay && !end.hasDay) {
 209:     totalMonths += 1;
 210:   }
 211: 
 212:   totalMonths = Math.max(totalMonths, 0);
 213: 
 214:   return {
 215:     totalMonths,
 216:     years: Math.floor(totalMonths / 12),
 217:     months: totalMonths % 12,
 218:   };
 219: }
 220: 
 221: /*
 222:  * =========================================
 223:  * Duration Formatting
 224:  * =========================================
 225:  */
 226: 
 227: export function formatExperienceDuration(
 228:   experience,
 229:   { compact = false, fallback = "" } = {},
 230: ) {
 231:   const duration = calculateExperienceDuration(experience);
 232: 
 233:   if (!duration) {
 234:     return fallback;
 235:   }
 236: 
 237:   const { totalMonths, years, months } = duration;
 238: 
 239:   if (totalMonths === 0) {
 240:     return compact ? "< 1 mo" : "Less than one month";
 241:   }
 242: 
 243:   const durationParts = [];
 244: 
 245:   if (years > 0) {
 246:     durationParts.push(
 247:       compact
 248:         ? `${years} yr${years === 1 ? "" : "s"}`
 249:         : `${years} ${years === 1 ? "year" : "years"}`,
 250:     );
 251:   }
 252: 
 253:   if (months > 0) {
 254:     durationParts.push(
 255:       compact
 256:         ? `${months} mo${months === 1 ? "" : "s"}`
 257:         : `${months} ${months === 1 ? "month" : "months"}`,
 258:     );
 259:   }
 260: 
 261:   return durationParts.join(" ");
 262: }

 266:  * Location Formatting

 270: export function formatExperienceLocation(experience) {

 272: 
 273:   if (getText(location.displayValue)) {
 274:     return getText(location.displayValue);
 275:   }
 276: 
 277:   const locationParts = [
 278:     getText(location.city),
 279:     getText(location.stateRegion),
 280:     getText(location.country),
 281:   ].filter(Boolean);
 282: 
 283:   return locationParts.join(", ");
 284: }

 346: /*
 347:  * =========================================
 348:  * Record Completeness
 349:  * =========================================
 350:  */
 351: 
 352: export function getExperienceCompleteness(experience) {
 353:   const checks = [
 354:     {
 355:       id: "position",
 356:       label: "Position title",
 357:       complete: Boolean(getText(experience?.position?.title)),
 358:       weight: 2,
 359:     },
 360:     {
 361:       id: "organization",
 362:       label: "Organization",
 363:       complete: Boolean(getText(experience?.organization?.name)),
 364:       weight: 2,
 365:     },
 366:     {
 367:       id: "dates",
 368:       label: "Employment dates",
 369:       complete: Boolean(
 370:         getText(experience?.dates?.startDate) &&
 371:         (experience?.dates?.isCurrent || getText(experience?.dates?.endDate)),
 372:       ),
 373:       weight: 2,
 374:     },
 375:     {
 376:       id: "overview",
 377:       label: "Experience overview",
 378:       complete: Boolean(getText(experience?.overview)),
 379:       weight: 1,
 380:     },
 381:     {
 382:       id: "responsibilities",
 383:       label: "Responsibilities",
 384:       complete: getArray(experience?.responsibilities).length > 0,
 385:       weight: 1,
 386:     },
 387:     {
 388:       id: "achievements",
 389:       label: "Achievements",
 390:       complete: getArray(experience?.achievements).length > 0,
 391:       weight: 1,
 392:     },
 393:     {
 394:       id: "capabilities",
 395:       label: "Skills or technologies",
 396:       complete:
 397:         getArray(experience?.skills).length > 0 ||
 398:         getArray(experience?.technologies).length > 0,
 399:       weight: 1,
 400:     },
 401:   ];
 402: 
 403:   const maximumScore = checks.reduce((total, check) => total + check.weight, 0);
 404: 
 405:   const score = checks.reduce(
 406:     (total, check) => total + (check.complete ? check.weight : 0),
 407:     0,
 408:   );
 409: 
 410:   const percentage =
 411:     maximumScore > 0 ? Math.round((score / maximumScore) * 100) : 0;
 412: 
 413:   let level = "basic";
 414:   let label = "Basic";
 415: 
 416:   if (percentage >= 90) {
 417:     level = "excellent";
 418:     label = "Excellent";
 419:   } else if (percentage >= 70) {
 420:     level = "strong";
 421:     label = "Strong";
 422:   } else if (percentage >= 50) {
 423:     level = "developing";
 424:     label = "Developing";
 425:   }
 426: 
 427:   return {
 428:     score,
 429:     maximumScore,
 430:     percentage,
 431:     level,
 432:     label,
 433: 
 434:     checks,
 435: 
 436:     completedChecks: checks.filter((check) => check.complete),
 437: 
 438:     missingChecks: checks.filter((check) => !check.complete),
 439:   };
 440: }
