 442: /*
 443:  * =========================================
 444:  * Searchable Experience Text
 445:  * =========================================
 446:  */
 447: 
 448: export function createExperienceSearchText(experience) {
 449:   const responsibilities = getArray(experience?.responsibilities).map((item) =>
 450:     getText(item?.text || item),
 451:   );
 452: 
 453:   const achievements = getArray(experience?.achievements).flatMap((item) => [
 454:     getText(item?.text || item),
 455:     getText(item?.metric),
 456:   ]);
 457: 
 458:   const skills = getArray(experience?.skills).map((item) =>
 459:     getText(item?.name || item?.label || item?.value || item),
 460:   );
 461: 
 462:   const technologies = getArray(experience?.technologies).map((item) =>
 463:     getText(item?.name || item?.label || item?.value || item),
 464:   );
 465: 
 466:   const leadership = getObject(experience?.leadership);
 467: 
 468:   return [
 469:     getExperiencePositionTitle(experience),
 470: 
 471:     getExperienceOrganizationName(experience),
 472: 
 473:     getExperienceCategoryDisplay(experience),
 474: 
 475:     getExperienceEmploymentTypeDisplay(experience),
 476: 
 477:     getExperienceWorkArrangementDisplay(experience),
 478: 
 479:     formatExperienceLocation(experience),
 480: 
 481:     getText(experience?.organization?.industry),
 482: 
 483:     getText(experience?.overview),
 484: 
 485:     getText(leadership.description),
 486: 
 487:     ...responsibilities,
 488:     ...achievements,
 489:     ...skills,
 490:     ...technologies,
 491:   ]
 492:     .filter(Boolean)
 493:     .join(" ")
 494:     .toLocaleLowerCase();
 495: }
 496: 
 497: /*
 498:  * =========================================
 499:  * Search Experiences
 500:  * =========================================
 501:  */
 502: 
 503: export function searchExperiences(experiences, query) {
 504:   const normalizedQuery = getSearchableText(query);
 505: 
 506:   if (!normalizedQuery) {
 507:     return [...getArray(experiences)];
 508:   }
 509: 
 510:   const queryTerms = normalizedQuery.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
 511: 
 512:   return getArray(experiences).filter((experience) => {
 513:     const searchableText = createExperienceSearchText(experience);
 514: 
 515:     return queryTerms.every((term) => searchableText.includes(term));
 516:   });
 517: }
 518: 
 519: /*
 520:  * =========================================
 521:  * Filter Experiences
 522:  * =========================================
 523:  */
 524: 
 525: export function filterExperiences(experiences, filters = {}) {
 526:   const {
 527:     category = "all",
 528:     employmentType = "all",
 529:     workArrangement = "all",
 530:     status = "all",
 531:     timeline = "all",
 532:     hasAchievements = false,
 533:     hasSkillsOrTechnologies = false,
 534:   } = filters;
 535: 
 536:   return getArray(experiences).filter((experience) => {
 537:     if (category !== "all" && experience.category !== category) {
 538:       return false;
 539:     }
 540: 
 541:     if (
 542:       employmentType !== "all" &&
 543:       experience.position?.employmentType !== employmentType
 544:     ) {
 545:       return false;
 546:     }
 547: 
 548:     if (
 549:       workArrangement !== "all" &&
 550:       experience.position?.workArrangement !== workArrangement
 551:     ) {
 552:       return false;
 553:     }
 554: 
 555:     if (status !== "all" && experience.status !== status) {
 556:       return false;
 557:     }
 558: 
 559:     if (timeline === "current" && experience.dates?.isCurrent !== true) {
 560:       return false;
 561:     }
 562: 
 563:     if (timeline === "previous" && experience.dates?.isCurrent === true) {
 564:       return false;
 565:     }
 566: 
 567:     if (hasAchievements && getArray(experience.achievements).length === 0) {
 568:       return false;
 569:     }
 570: 
 571:     if (
 572:       hasSkillsOrTechnologies &&
 573:       getArray(experience.skills).length === 0 &&
 574:       getArray(experience.technologies).length === 0
 575:     ) {
 576:       return false;
 577:     }
 578: 
 579:     return true;
 580:   });
 581: }
 582: 
 583: /*
 584:  * =========================================
 585:  * Sort Date Helpers
 586:  * =========================================
 587:  */
 588: 
 589: function getDateSortValue(value) {
 590:   const parsedDate = parseExperienceDate(value);
 591: 
 592:   return parsedDate ? parsedDate.date.getTime() : 0;
 593: }
 594: 
 595: function compareTextValues(firstValue, secondValue) {
 596:   return getText(firstValue).localeCompare(getText(secondValue), undefined, {
 597:     sensitivity: "base",
 598:   });
 599: }
 600: 
 601: /*
 602:  * =========================================
 603:  * Sort Experiences
 604:  * =========================================
 605:  */
 606: 
 607: export function sortExperiences(experiences, sortOption = "newest") {
 608:   const sortedExperiences = [...getArray(experiences)];
 609: 
 610:   sortedExperiences.sort((firstExperience, secondExperience) => {
 611:     switch (sortOption) {
 612:       case "oldest":
 613:         return (
 614:           getDateSortValue(firstExperience.dates?.startDate) -
 615:           getDateSortValue(secondExperience.dates?.startDate)
 616:         );
 617: 
 618:       case "position-ascending":
 619:         return compareTextValues(
 620:           getExperiencePositionTitle(firstExperience),
 621:           getExperiencePositionTitle(secondExperience),
 622:         );
 623: 
 624:       case "position-descending":
 625:         return compareTextValues(
 626:           getExperiencePositionTitle(secondExperience),
 627:           getExperiencePositionTitle(firstExperience),
 628:         );
 629: 
 630:       case "organization-ascending":
 631:         return compareTextValues(
 632:           getExperienceOrganizationName(firstExperience),
 633:           getExperienceOrganizationName(secondExperience),
 634:         );
 635: 
 636:       case "organization-descending":
 637:         return compareTextValues(
 638:           getExperienceOrganizationName(secondExperience),
 639:           getExperienceOrganizationName(firstExperience),
 640:         );
 641: 
 642:       case "recently-updated":
 643:         return (
 644:           new Date(secondExperience.updatedAt || 0).getTime() -
 645:           new Date(firstExperience.updatedAt || 0).getTime()
 646:         );
 647: 
 648:       case "newest":
 649:       default: {
 650:         /*
 651:          * Current positions appear first.
 652:          */
 653: 
 654:         const firstIsCurrent = firstExperience.dates?.isCurrent === true;
 655: 
 656:         const secondIsCurrent = secondExperience.dates?.isCurrent === true;
 657: 
 658:         if (firstIsCurrent !== secondIsCurrent) {
 659:           return firstIsCurrent ? -1 : 1;
 660:         }
 661: 
 662:         const firstEndDate = firstIsCurrent
 663:           ? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
 664:           : getDateSortValue(firstExperience.dates?.endDate);
 665: 
 666:         const secondEndDate = secondIsCurrent
 667:           ? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
 668:           : getDateSortValue(secondExperience.dates?.endDate);
 669: 
 670:         if (firstEndDate !== secondEndDate) {
 671:           return secondEndDate - firstEndDate;
 672:         }
 673: 
 674:         return (
 675:           getDateSortValue(secondExperience.dates?.startDate) -
 676:           getDateSortValue(firstExperience.dates?.startDate)
 677:         );
 678:       }
 679:     }
 680:   });
 681: 
 682:   return sortedExperiences;
 683: }
 684: 
 685: /*
 686:  * =========================================
 687:  * Search, Filter and Sort
 688:  * =========================================
 689:  */
 690: 
 691: export function prepareExperienceCollection({
 692:   experiences = [],
 693:   query = "",
 694:   filters = {},
 695:   sortOption = "newest",
 696: } = {}) {
 697:   const searchedExperiences = searchExperiences(experiences, query);
 698: 
 699:   const filteredExperiences = filterExperiences(searchedExperiences, filters);
 700: 
 701:   return sortExperiences(filteredExperiences, sortOption);
 702: }
 703: 
 704: /*
 705:  * =========================================
 706:  * Experience Statistics
 707:  * =========================================
 708:  */
 709: 
 710: export function getExperienceStatistics(experiences) {
 711:   const collection = getArray(experiences);
 712: 
 713:   const active = collection.filter(
 714:     (experience) => experience.status !== "archived",
 715:   );
 716: 
 717:   const archived = collection.filter(
 718:     (experience) => experience.status === "archived",
 719:   );
 720: 
 721:   const current = active.filter(
 722:     (experience) => experience.dates?.isCurrent === true,
 723:   );
 724: 
 725:   const previous = active.filter(
 726:     (experience) => experience.dates?.isCurrent !== true,
 727:   );
 728: 
 729:   const withAchievements = active.filter(
 730:     (experience) => getArray(experience.achievements).length > 0,
 731:   );
 732: 
 733:   return {
 734:     total: collection.length,
 735:     active: active.length,
 736:     archived: archived.length,
 737:     current: current.length,
 738:     previous: previous.length,
 739:     withAchievements: withAchievements.length,
 740:   };
 741: }

  15:  * Basic Helpers
  16:  * =========================================
  17:  */
  18: 
  19: function getText(value) {
  20:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
  21: }
  22: 
  23: function getArray(value) {
  24:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  25: }
  26: 
  27: function getObject(value) {
  28:   return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
  29:     ? value
  30:     : {};
  31: }
  32: 
  33: function getItemText(value) {
  34:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  35:     return value.trim();
  36:   }
  37: 
  38:   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") {
  39:     return "";
  40:   }
  41: 
  42:   return getText(
  43:     value.text || value.name || value.description || value.value || value.label,
  44:   );
  45: }
  46: 
  47: function hasText(value) {
  48:   return getText(value).length > 0;
  49: }
  50: 
  51: function createIssue(field, message, code = "invalid") {
  52:   return {
  53:     field,
  54:     message,
  55:     code,
  56:   };
  57: }
  58: 
  59: /*
  60:  * =========================================
  61:  * Date Helpers
  62:  * =========================================
  63:  */
  64: 
  65: const EXPERIENCE_DATE_PATTERN =
  66:   /^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))?$/;
  67: 
  68: function isValidExperienceDate(value) {
  69:   const text = getText(value);
  70: 
  71:   if (!text || !EXPERIENCE_DATE_PATTERN.test(text)) {
  72:     return false;
  73:   }
  74: 
  75:   /*
  76:    * YYYY-MM dates are valid after passing the
  77:    * regular expression.
  78:    */
  79: 
  80:   if (text.length === 7) {
  81:     return true;
  82:   }
  83: 
  84:   /*
  85:    * Validate complete YYYY-MM-DD dates so values
  86:    * such as February 31 are rejected.
  87:    */
  88: 
  89:   const [year, month, day] = text.split("-").map(Number);
  90: 
  91:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  92: 
  93:   return (
  94:     date.getUTCFullYear() === year &&
  95:     date.getUTCMonth() === month - 1 &&
  96:     date.getUTCDate() === day
  97:   );
  98: }
  99: 
 100: function getComparableDate(value, useEndOfMonth = false) {
 101:   const text = getText(value);
 102: 
 103:   if (!isValidExperienceDate(text)) {
 104:     return null;
 105:   }
 106: 
 107:   const [year, month, suppliedDay] = text.split("-").map(Number);
 108: 
 109:   let day = suppliedDay;
 110: 
 111:   if (!day) {
 112:     day = useEndOfMonth ? new Date(Date.UTC(year, month, 0)).getUTCDate() : 1;
 113:   }
 114: 
 115:   return Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day);
 116: }
 117: 
 118: /*
 119:  * =========================================
 120:  * URL Validation
 121:  * =========================================
 122:  */
 123: 
 124: function isValidWebsite(value) {
 125:   const text = getText(value);
 126: 
 127:   if (!text) {
 128:     return true;
 129:   }
 130: 
 131:   try {
 132:     const normalizedUrl = /^https?:\/\//i.test(text) ? text : `https://${text}`;
 133: 
 134:     const url = new URL(normalizedUrl);
 135: 
 136:     return ["http:", "https:"].includes(url.protocol) && Boolean(url.hostname);
 137:   } catch {
 138:     return false;
 139:   }
 140: }
 141: 
 142: /*
 143:  * =========================================
 144:  * Validation Collector
 145:  * =========================================
 146:  */
 147: 
 148: function createValidationCollector() {
 149:   const errors = [];
 150:   const warnings = [];
 151: 
 152:   return {
 153:     errors,
 154:     warnings,
 155: 
 156:     addError(field, message, code) {
 157:       errors.push(createIssue(field, message, code));
 158:     },
 159: 
 160:     addWarning(field, message, code = "warning") {
 161:       warnings.push(createIssue(field, message, code));
 162:     },
 163:   };
 164: }
 165: 
 166: /*
 167:  * =========================================
 168:  * Text Length Validation
 169:  * =========================================
 170:  */
 171: 
 172: function validateTextLength(collector, field, value, maximumLength, label) {
 173:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
 174:     return;
 175:   }
 176: 
 177:   if (value.trim().length > maximumLength) {
 178:     collector.addError(
 179:       field,
 180:       `${label} cannot exceed ${maximumLength} characters.`,
 181:       "too_long",
 182:     );
 183:   }
 184: }
 185: 
 186: /*
 187:  * =========================================
 188:  * Duplicate Validation
 189:  * =========================================
 190:  */
 191: 
 192: function findDuplicateIndexes(values, getIdentity) {
 193:   const seen = new Map();
 194:   const duplicates = [];
 195: 
 196:   values.forEach((value, index) => {
 197:     const identity = getIdentity(value).trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
 198: 
 199:     if (!identity) {
 200:       return;
 201:     }
 202: 
 203:     if (seen.has(identity)) {
 204:       duplicates.push({
 205:         firstIndex: seen.get(identity),
 206:         duplicateIndex: index,
 207:       });
 208: 
 209:       return;
 210:     }
 211: 
 212:     seen.set(identity, index);
 213:   });
 214: 
 215:   return duplicates;
 216: }
 217: 
 218: /*
 219:  * =========================================
 220:  * Required Information
 221:  * =========================================
 222:  */
 223: 
 224: function validateRequiredInformation(collector, experience) {
 225:   const position = getObject(experience.position);
 226:   const organization = getObject(experience.organization);
 227:   const dates = getObject(experience.dates);
 228: 
 229:   if (!hasText(position.title)) {
 230:     collector.addError(
 231:       "position.title",
 232:       "Enter the position or role title.",
 233:       "required",
 234:     );
 235:   }
 236: 
 237:   if (!hasText(organization.name)) {
 238:     collector.addError(
 239:       "organization.name",
 240:       "Enter the organization, employer, or client name.",
 241:       "required",
 242:     );
 243:   }
 244: 
 245:   if (!hasText(dates.startDate)) {
 246:     collector.addError(
 247:       "dates.startDate",
 248:       "Enter the experience start date.",
 249:       "required",
 250:     );
 251:   }
 252: }
 253: 
 254: /*
 255:  * =========================================
 256:  * Category and Option Validation
 257:  * =========================================
 258:  */
 259: 
 260: function validateConfiguredOptions(collector, experience) {
 261:   const position = getObject(experience.position);
 262: 
 263:   if (!isValidExperienceCategory(experience.category)) {
 264:     collector.addError(
 265:       "category",
 266:       "Select a valid experience category.",
 267:       "invalid_option",
 268:     );
 269:   }
 270: 
 271:   if (!isValidEmploymentType(position.employmentType)) {
 272:     collector.addError(
 273:       "position.employmentType",
 274:       "Select a valid employment type.",
 275:       "invalid_option",
 276:     );
 277:   }
 278: 
 279:   if (!isValidWorkArrangement(position.workArrangement)) {
 280:     collector.addError(
 281:       "position.workArrangement",
 282:       "Select a valid work arrangement.",
 283:       "invalid_option",
 284:     );
 285:   }
 286: 
 287:   if (!isValidExperienceStatus(experience.status)) {
 288:     collector.addError(
 289:       "status",
 290:       "Select a valid experience status.",
 291:       "invalid_option",
 292:     );
 293:   }
 294: }
 295: 
 296: /*
 297:  * =========================================
 298:  * Date Validation
 299:  * =========================================
 300:  */
 301: 
 302: function validateDates(collector, experience) {
 303:   const dates = getObject(experience.dates);
 304: 
 305:   const startDate = getText(dates.startDate);
 306:   const endDate = getText(dates.endDate);
 307:   const isCurrent = dates.isCurrent === true;
 308: 
 309:   if (startDate && !isValidExperienceDate(startDate)) {
 310:     collector.addError(
 311:       "dates.startDate",
 312:       "Enter a valid start date.",
 313:       "invalid_date",
 314:     );
 315:   }
 316: 
 317:   if (isCurrent && endDate) {
 318:     collector.addError(
 319:       "dates.endDate",
 320:       "A current position cannot have an end date.",
 321:       "current_position_has_end_date",
 322:     );
 323:   }
 324: 
 325:   if (!isCurrent && !endDate) {
 326:     collector.addError(
 327:       "dates.endDate",
 328:       "Enter an end date or mark this position as current.",
 329:       "required",
 330:     );
 331:   }
 332: 
 333:   if (!isCurrent && endDate && !isValidExperienceDate(endDate)) {
 334:     collector.addError(
 335:       "dates.endDate",
 336:       "Enter a valid end date.",
 337:       "invalid_date",
 338:     );
 339:   }
 340: 
 341:   if (
 342:     startDate &&
 343:     endDate &&
 344:     isValidExperienceDate(startDate) &&
 345:     isValidExperienceDate(endDate)
 346:   ) {
 347:     const comparableStartDate = getComparableDate(startDate);
 348: 
 349:     const comparableEndDate = getComparableDate(endDate, true);
 350: 
 351:     if (comparableEndDate < comparableStartDate) {
 352:       collector.addError(
 353:         "dates.endDate",
 354:         "The end date cannot be earlier than the start date.",
 355:         "invalid_date_order",
 356:       );
 357:     }
 358:   }
 359: }
 360: 
 361: /*
 362:  * =========================================
 363:  * Position and Organization
 364:  * =========================================
 365:  */
 366: 
 367: function validatePositionAndOrganization(collector, experience) {
 368:   const position = getObject(experience.position);
 369:   const organization = getObject(experience.organization);
 370: 
 371:   validateTextLength(
 372:     collector,
 373:     "position.title",
 374:     position.title,
 375:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.positionTitle,
 376:     "Position title",
 377:   );
 378: 
 379:   validateTextLength(
 380:     collector,
 381:     "organization.name",
 382:     organization.name,
 383:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.organizationName,
 384:     "Organization name",
 385:   );
 386: 
 387:   validateTextLength(
 388:     collector,
 389:     "organization.website",
 390:     organization.website,
 391:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.organizationWebsite,
 392:     "Organization website",
 393:   );
 394: 
 395:   validateTextLength(
 396:     collector,
 397:     "organization.industry",
 398:     organization.industry,
 399:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.industry,
 400:     "Industry",
 401:   );
 402: 
 403:   if (organization.website && !isValidWebsite(organization.website)) {
 404:     collector.addError(
 405:       "organization.website",
 406:       "Enter a valid organization website.",
 407:       "invalid_url",
 408:     );
 409:   }
 410: }
 411: 
 412: /*
 413:  * =========================================
 414:  * Location
 415:  * =========================================
 416:  */
 417: 
 418: function validateLocation(collector, experience) {
 419:   const location = getObject(experience.location);
 420: 
 421:   validateTextLength(
 422:     collector,
 423:     "location.city",
 424:     location.city,
 425:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.city,
 426:     "City",
 427:   );
 428: 
 429:   validateTextLength(
 430:     collector,
 431:     "location.stateRegion",
 432:     location.stateRegion,
 433:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.stateRegion,
 434:     "State or region",
 435:   );
 436: 
 437:   validateTextLength(
 438:     collector,
 439:     "location.country",
 440:     location.country,
 441:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.country,
 442:     "Country",
 443:   );
 444: 
 445:   validateTextLength(
 446:     collector,
 447:     "location.displayValue",
 448:     location.displayValue,
 449:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.locationDisplayValue,
 450:     "Display location",
 451:   );
 452: }
 453: 
 454: /*
 455:  * =========================================
 456:  * Overview
 457:  * =========================================
 458:  */
 459: 
 460: function validateOverview(collector, experience) {
 461:   validateTextLength(
 462:     collector,
 463:     "overview",
 464:     experience.overview,
 465:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.overview,
 466:     "Experience overview",
 467:   );
 468: }
 469: 
 470: /*
 471:  * =========================================
 472:  * Responsibilities
 473:  * =========================================
 474:  */
 475: 
 476: function validateResponsibilities(collector, experience) {
 477:   const responsibilities = getArray(experience.responsibilities);
 478: 
 479:   if (
 480:     responsibilities.length > EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumResponsibilities
 481:   ) {
 482:     collector.addError(
 483:       "responsibilities",
 484:       `Add no more than ${EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumResponsibilities} responsibilities.`,
 485:       "too_many_items",
 486:     );
 487:   }
 488: 
 489:   responsibilities.forEach((responsibility, index) => {
 490:     const text = getItemText(responsibility);
 491: 
 492:     if (!text) {
 493:       collector.addError(
 494:         `responsibilities.${index}.text`,
 495:         "Responsibility text cannot be empty.",
 496:         "required",
 497:       );
 498: 
 499:       return;
 500:     }
 501: 
 502:     validateTextLength(
 503:       collector,
 504:       `responsibilities.${index}.text`,
 505:       text,
 506:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.responsibilityText,
 507:       "Responsibility",
 508:     );
 509:   });
 510: 
 511:   const duplicates = findDuplicateIndexes(responsibilities, getItemText);
 512: 
 513:   duplicates.forEach(({ duplicateIndex }) => {
 514:     collector.addError(
 515:       `responsibilities.${duplicateIndex}.text`,
 516:       "This responsibility has already been added.",
 517:       "duplicate",
 518:     );
 519:   });
 520: }
 521: 
 522: /*
 523:  * =========================================
 524:  * Achievements
 525:  * =========================================
 526:  */
 527: 
 528: function validateAchievements(collector, experience) {
 529:   const achievements = getArray(experience.achievements);
 530: 
 531:   if (achievements.length > EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumAchievements) {
 532:     collector.addError(
 533:       "achievements",
 534:       `Add no more than ${EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumAchievements} achievements.`,
 535:       "too_many_items",
 536:     );
 537:   }
 538: 
 539:   achievements.forEach((achievement, index) => {
 540:     const item = getObject(achievement);
 541:     const text = getItemText(achievement);
 542: 
 543:     if (!text) {
 544:       collector.addError(
 545:         `achievements.${index}.text`,
 546:         "Achievement text cannot be empty.",
 547:         "required",
 548:       );
 549: 
 550:       return;
 551:     }
 552: 
 553:     validateTextLength(
 554:       collector,
 555:       `achievements.${index}.text`,
 556:       text,
 557:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.achievementText,
 558:       "Achievement",
 559:     );
 560: 
 561:     validateTextLength(
 562:       collector,
 563:       `achievements.${index}.metric`,
 564:       item.metric,
 565:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.achievementMetric,
 566:       "Achievement metric",
 567:     );
 568:   });
 569: 
 570:   const duplicates = findDuplicateIndexes(achievements, getItemText);
 571: 
 572:   duplicates.forEach(({ duplicateIndex }) => {
 573:     collector.addError(
 574:       `achievements.${duplicateIndex}.text`,
 575:       "This achievement has already been added.",
 576:       "duplicate",
 577:     );
 578:   });
 579: }
 580: 
 581: /*
 582:  * =========================================
 583:  * Central Skill Relationship Helpers
 584:  * =========================================
 585:  */
 586: 
 587: const EXPERIENCE_SKILL_LEVEL_VALUES = [
 588:   "",
 589:   "foundational",
 590:   "intermediate",
 591:   "advanced",
 592:   "expert",
 593: ];
 594: 
 595: function getRelationshipSkillId(value) {
 596:   const relationship = getObject(value);
 597: 
 598:   return getText(relationship.skillId || relationship.profileSkillId);
 599: }
 600: 
 601: function getSkillSnapshotName(value) {
 602:   const relationship = getObject(value);
 603: 
 604:   return getText(
 605:     relationship.nameSnapshot ||
 606:       relationship.name ||
 607:       relationship.label ||
 608:       relationship.value,
 609:   );
 610: }
 611: 
 612: function validateRelationshipIdentifier(collector, field, value, label) {
 613:   if (!value) {
 614:     return;
 615:   }
 616: 
 617:   if (typeof value !== "string") {
 618:     collector.addError(
 619:       field,
 620:       `${label} has an invalid identifier.`,
 621:       "invalid_identifier",
 622:     );
 623: 
 624:     return;
 625:   }
 626: 
 627:   if (value.trim().length > 200) {
 628:     collector.addError(
 629:       field,
 630:       `${label} identifier cannot exceed 200 characters.`,
 631:       "too_long",
 632:     );
 633:   }
 634: }
 635: 
 636: function validateRelationshipOrder(collector, field, value) {
 637:   if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") {
 638:     return;
 639:   }
 640: 
 641:   if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0) {
 642:     collector.addError(
 643:       field,
 644:       "Relationship order must be a whole number of zero or greater.",
 645:       "invalid_order",
 646:     );
 647:   }
 648: }
 649: 
 650: function validateRelationshipProficiency(collector, field, value, label) {
 651:   const proficiency = getText(value);
 652: 
 653:   if (!EXPERIENCE_SKILL_LEVEL_VALUES.includes(proficiency)) {
 654:     collector.addError(
 655:       field,
 656:       `Select a valid ${label.toLowerCase()} proficiency level.`,
 657:       "invalid_option",
 658:     );
 659:   }
 660: }
 661: 
 662: /*
 663:  * =========================================
 664:  * Skills
 665:  * =========================================
 666:  */
 667: 
 668: function validateSkills(collector, experience) {
 669:   const skills = getArray(experience.skills);
 670: 
 671:   if (skills.length > EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumSkills) {
 672:     collector.addError(
 673:       "skills",
 674:       `Add no more than ${EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumSkills} skills.`,
 675:       "too_many_items",
 676:     );
 677:   }
 678: 
 679:   skills.forEach((skill, index) => {
 680:     const relationship = getObject(skill);
 681: 
 682:     const skillId = getRelationshipSkillId(relationship);
 683: 
 684:     const nameSnapshot = getSkillSnapshotName(relationship);
 685: 
 686:     const categorySnapshot = getText(relationship.categorySnapshot);
 687: 
 688:     const typeSnapshot = getText(relationship.typeSnapshot);
 689: 
 690:     const level = relationship.level ?? "";
 691: 
 692:     const usageDescription = relationship.usageDescription;
 693: 
 694:     validateRelationshipIdentifier(
 695:       collector,
 696:       `skills.${index}.id`,
 697:       relationship.id,
 698:       "Skill relationship",
 699:     );
 700: 
 701:     validateRelationshipIdentifier(
 702:       collector,
 703:       `skills.${index}.skillId`,
 704:       skillId,
 705:       "Central skill",
 706:     );
 707: 
 708:     if (!skillId && !nameSnapshot) {
 709:       collector.addError(
 710:         `skills.${index}.skillId`,
 711:         "Select a saved skill or provide a skill name.",
 712:         "required",
 713:       );
 714: 
 715:       return;
 716:     }
 717: 
 718:     /*
 719:      * A snapshot is required even when the central
 720:      * Skill Library relationship exists. This keeps
 721:      * the experience readable if the central record
 722:      * is archived, deleted, or temporarily unavailable.
 723:      */
 724: 
 725:     if (skillId && !nameSnapshot) {
 726:       collector.addError(
 727:         `skills.${index}.nameSnapshot`,
 728:         "Store the skill name snapshot with this relationship.",
 729:         "required_snapshot",
 730:       );
 731:     }
 732: 
 733:     if (!skillId && nameSnapshot) {
 734:       collector.addWarning(
 735:         `skills.${index}.skillId`,
 736:         `"${nameSnapshot}" is a legacy skill without a central Skill Library relationship.`,
 737:         "missing_skill_relationship",
 738:       );
 739:     }
 740: 
 741:     validateTextLength(
 742:       collector,
 743:       `skills.${index}.nameSnapshot`,
 744:       nameSnapshot,
 745:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillName,
 746:       "Skill name",
 747:     );
 748: 
 749:     validateTextLength(
 750:       collector,
 751:       `skills.${index}.categorySnapshot`,
 752:       categorySnapshot,
 753:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillCategory,
 754:       "Skill category snapshot",
 755:     );
 756: 
 757:     validateTextLength(
 758:       collector,
 759:       `skills.${index}.typeSnapshot`,
 760:       typeSnapshot,
 761:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillType,
 762:       "Skill type snapshot",
 763:     );
 764: 
 765:     validateTextLength(
 766:       collector,
 767:       `skills.${index}.level`,
 768:       level,
 769:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillLevel,
 770:       "Experience-specific proficiency",
 771:     );
 772: 
 773:     validateRelationshipProficiency(
 774:       collector,
 775:       `skills.${index}.level`,
 776:       level,
 777:       "Skill",
 778:     );
 779: 
 780:     validateTextLength(
 781:       collector,
 782:       `skills.${index}.usageDescription`,
 783:       usageDescription,
 784:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.skillUsageDescription,
 785:       "Skill usage description",
 786:     );
 787: 
 788:     validateRelationshipOrder(
 789:       collector,
 790:       `skills.${index}.order`,
 791:       relationship.order,
 792:     );
 793:   });
 794: 
 795:   /*
 796:    * Prefer the permanent central skill ID for
 797:    * duplicate detection. Fall back to the snapshot
 798:    * name for migrated or legacy records.
 799:    */
 800: 
 801:   const duplicates = findDuplicateIndexes(skills, (skill) => {
 802:     const skillId = getRelationshipSkillId(skill);
 803: 
 804:     if (skillId) {
 805:       return `id:${skillId}`;
 806:     }
 807: 
 808:     return `name:${getSkillSnapshotName(skill)}`;
 809:   });
 810: 
 811:   duplicates.forEach(({ duplicateIndex }) => {
 812:     collector.addError(
 813:       `skills.${duplicateIndex}.skillId`,
 814:       "This skill has already been added to the experience.",
 815:       "duplicate",
 816:     );
 817:   });
 818: }
 819: 
 820: /*
 821:  * =========================================
 822:  * Technologies
 823:  * =========================================
 824:  */
 825: 
 826: function validateTechnologies(collector, experience) {
 827:   const technologies = getArray(experience.technologies);
 828: 
 829:   if (technologies.length > EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumTechnologies) {
 830:     collector.addError(
 831:       "technologies",
 832:       `Add no more than ${EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumTechnologies} technologies.`,
 833:       "too_many_items",
 834:     );
 835:   }
 836: 
 837:   technologies.forEach((technology, index) => {
 838:     const relationship = getObject(technology);
 839: 
 840:     const skillId = getRelationshipSkillId(relationship);
 841: 
 842:     const nameSnapshot = getSkillSnapshotName(relationship);
 843: 
 844:     const category = getText(relationship.category);
 845: 
 846:     const proficiency = relationship.proficiency ?? "";
 847: 
 848:     const usageDescription = relationship.usageDescription;
 849: 
 850:     validateRelationshipIdentifier(
 851:       collector,
 852:       `technologies.${index}.id`,
 853:       relationship.id,
 854:       "Technology relationship",
 855:     );
 856: 
 857:     validateRelationshipIdentifier(
 858:       collector,
 859:       `technologies.${index}.skillId`,
 860:       skillId,
 861:       "Central skill",
 862:     );
 863: 
 864:     if (!skillId && !nameSnapshot) {
 865:       collector.addError(
 866:         `technologies.${index}.skillId`,
 867:         "Select a saved technology or provide its name.",
 868:         "required",
 869:       );
 870: 
 871:       return;
 872:     }
 873: 
 874:     if (skillId && !nameSnapshot) {
 875:       collector.addError(
 876:         `technologies.${index}.nameSnapshot`,
 877:         "Store the technology name snapshot with this relationship.",
 878:         "required_snapshot",
 879:       );
 880:     }
 881: 
 882:     if (!skillId && nameSnapshot) {
 883:       collector.addWarning(
 884:         `technologies.${index}.skillId`,
 885:         `"${nameSnapshot}" is a legacy technology without a central Skill Library relationship.`,
 886:         "missing_skill_relationship",
 887:       );
 888:     }
 889: 
 890:     validateTextLength(
 891:       collector,
 892:       `technologies.${index}.nameSnapshot`,
 893:       nameSnapshot,
 894:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyName,
 895:       "Technology name",
 896:     );
 897: 
 898:     validateTextLength(
 899:       collector,
 900:       `technologies.${index}.name`,
 901:       relationship.name,
 902:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyName,
 903:       "Technology compatibility name",
 904:     );
 905: 
 906:     validateTextLength(
 907:       collector,
 908:       `technologies.${index}.category`,
 909:       category,
 910:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyCategory,
 911:       "Technology category",
 912:     );
 913: 
 914:     validateTextLength(
 915:       collector,
 916:       `technologies.${index}.proficiency`,
 917:       proficiency,
 918:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyProficiency,
 919:       "Technology proficiency",
 920:     );
 921: 
 922:     validateRelationshipProficiency(
 923:       collector,
 924:       `technologies.${index}.proficiency`,
 925:       proficiency,
 926:       "Technology",
 927:     );
 928: 
 929:     validateTextLength(
 930:       collector,
 931:       `technologies.${index}.usageDescription`,
 932:       usageDescription,
 933:       EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.technologyUsageDescription,
 934:       "Technology usage description",
 935:     );
 936: 
 937:     validateRelationshipOrder(
 938:       collector,
 939:       `technologies.${index}.order`,
 940:       relationship.order,
 941:     );
 942:   });
 943: 
 944:   const duplicates = findDuplicateIndexes(technologies, (technology) => {
 945:     const skillId = getRelationshipSkillId(technology);
 946: 
 947:     if (skillId) {
 948:       return `id:${skillId}`;
 949:     }
 950: 
 951:     return `name:${getSkillSnapshotName(technology)}`;
 952:   });
 953: 
 954:   duplicates.forEach(({ duplicateIndex }) => {
 955:     collector.addError(
 956:       `technologies.${duplicateIndex}.skillId`,
 957:       "This technology has already been added to the experience.",
 958:       "duplicate",
 959:     );
 960:   });
 961: }
 962: 
 963: /*
 964:  * =========================================
 965:  * Related Projects
 966:  * =========================================
 967:  */
 968: 
 969: function validateRelatedProjects(collector, experience) {
 970:   const projectIds = getArray(experience.relatedProjectIds);
 971: 
 972:   if (projectIds.length > EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedProjects) {
 973:     collector.addError(
 974:       "relatedProjectIds",
 975:       `Select no more than ${EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumRelatedProjects} related projects.`,
 976:       "too_many_items",
 977:     );
 978:   }
 979: 
 980:   const normalizedIds = projectIds
 981:     .map((projectId) => getText(projectId))
 982:     .filter(Boolean);
 983: 
 984:   const duplicates = findDuplicateIndexes(
 985:     normalizedIds,
 986:     (projectId) => projectId,
 987:   );
 988: 
 989:   duplicates.forEach(({ duplicateIndex }) => {
 990:     collector.addError(
 991:       `relatedProjectIds.${duplicateIndex}`,
 992:       "This related project has already been selected.",
 993:       "duplicate",
 994:     );
 995:   });
 996: }
 997: 
 998: /*
 999:  * =========================================
1000:  * Leadership
1001:  * =========================================
1002:  */
1003: 
1004: function validateLeadership(collector, experience) {
1005:   const leadership = getObject(experience.leadership);
1006: 
1007:   const hasLeadershipResponsibilities =
1008:     leadership.hasLeadershipResponsibilities === true;
1009: 
1010:   const peopleManaged = leadership.peopleManaged;
1011: 
1012:   if (
1013:     peopleManaged !== "" &&
1014:     peopleManaged !== null &&
1015:     peopleManaged !== undefined
1016:   ) {
1017:     const numberValue = Number(peopleManaged);
1018: 
1019:     if (!Number.isInteger(numberValue) || numberValue < 0) {
1020:       collector.addError(
1021:         "leadership.peopleManaged",
1022:         "People managed must be a whole number of zero or greater.",
1023:         "invalid_number",
1024:       );
1025:     }
1026: 
1027:     if (numberValue > 100000) {
1028:       collector.addError(
1029:         "leadership.peopleManaged",
1030:         "Enter a realistic number of people managed.",
1031:         "invalid_number",
1032:       );
1033:     }
1034: 
1035:     if (!hasLeadershipResponsibilities && numberValue > 0) {
1036:       collector.addWarning(
1037:         "leadership.hasLeadershipResponsibilities",
1038:         "People managed was entered, but leadership responsibilities are not enabled.",
1039:         "leadership_mismatch",
1040:       );
1041:     }
1042:   }
1043: 
1044:   validateTextLength(
1045:     collector,
1046:     "leadership.description",
1047:     leadership.description,
1048:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.leadershipDescription,
1049:     "Leadership description",
1050:   );
1051: }
1052: 
1053: /*
1054:  * =========================================
1055:  * Private Information
1056:  * =========================================
1057:  */
1058: 
1059: function validatePrivateInformation(collector, experience) {
1060:   const privateInformation = getObject(experience.privateInformation);
1061: 
1062:   validateTextLength(
1063:     collector,
1064:     "privateInformation.reasonForLeaving",
1065:     privateInformation.reasonForLeaving,
1066:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.reasonForLeaving,
1067:     "Reason for leaving",
1068:   );
1069: 
1070:   validateTextLength(
1071:     collector,
1072:     "privateInformation.notes",
1073:     privateInformation.notes,
1074:     EXPERIENCE_FIELD_LIMITS.privateNotes,
1075:     "Private notes",
1076:   );
1077: }
1078: 
1079: /*
1080:  * =========================================
1081:  * Meaningful Content
1082:  * =========================================
1083:  */
1084: 
1085: function validateMeaningfulContent(collector, experience) {
1086:   const hasOverview = hasText(experience.overview);
1087: 
1088:   const hasResponsibility = getArray(experience.responsibilities).some(
1089:     (responsibility) => hasText(getItemText(responsibility)),
1090:   );
1091: 
1092:   const hasAchievement = getArray(experience.achievements).some((achievement) =>
1093:     hasText(getItemText(achievement)),
1094:   );
1095: 
1096:   if (!hasOverview && !hasResponsibility && !hasAchievement) {
1097:     collector.addError(
1098:       "overview",
1099:       "Add an overview, responsibility, or achievement that explains what you did.",
1100:       "insufficient_content",
1101:     );
1102:   }
1103: 
1104:   if (!hasAchievement) {
1105:     collector.addWarning(
1106:       "achievements",
1107:       "Consider adding at least one achievement or outcome to strengthen this experience.",
1108:       "missing_achievement",
1109:     );
1110:   }
1111: 
1112:   const hasCapability =
1113:     getArray(experience.skills).some((skill) => {
1114:       return Boolean(
1115:         getRelationshipSkillId(skill) || getSkillSnapshotName(skill),
1116:       );
1117:     }) ||
1118:     getArray(experience.technologies).some((technology) => {
1119:       return Boolean(
1120:         getRelationshipSkillId(technology) || getSkillSnapshotName(technology),
1121:       );
1122:     });
1123: 
1124:   if (!hasCapability) {
1125:     collector.addWarning(
1126:       "skills",
1127:       "Consider adding relevant skills or technologies used in this role.",
1128:       "missing_capability",
1129:     );
1130:   }
1131: }
1132: 
1133: /*
1134:  * =========================================
1135:  * Build Field Error Map
1136:  * =========================================
1137:  */
1138: 
1139: export function createExperienceFieldErrorMap(errors = []) {
1140:   return errors.reduce((fieldErrors, error) => {
1141:     if (!fieldErrors[error.field]) {
1142:       fieldErrors[error.field] = error.message;
1143:     }
1144: 
1145:     return fieldErrors;
1146:   }, {});
1147: }
1148: 
1149: /*
1150:  * =========================================
1151:  * Main Validation
1152:  * =========================================
1153:  */
1154: 
1155: export function validateExperience(experienceValue) {
1156:   const experience = getObject(experienceValue);
1157:   const collector = createValidationCollector();
1158: 
1159:   validateRequiredInformation(collector, experience);
1160: 
1161:   validateConfiguredOptions(collector, experience);
1162: 
1163:   validateDates(collector, experience);
1164: 
1165:   validatePositionAndOrganization(collector, experience);
1166: 
1167:   validateLocation(collector, experience);
1168:   validateOverview(collector, experience);
1169: 
1170:   validateResponsibilities(collector, experience);
1171: 
1172:   validateAchievements(collector, experience);
1173:   validateSkills(collector, experience);
1174: 
1175:   validateTechnologies(collector, experience);
1176: 
1177:   validateRelatedProjects(collector, experience);
1178: 
1179:   validateLeadership(collector, experience);
1180: 
1181:   validatePrivateInformation(collector, experience);
1182: 
1183:   validateMeaningfulContent(collector, experience);
1184: 
1185:   return {
1186:     isValid: collector.errors.length === 0,
1187:     errors: collector.errors,
1188:     warnings: collector.warnings,
1189: 
1190:     fieldErrors: createExperienceFieldErrorMap(collector.errors),
1191:   };
1192: }
1193: 
1194: /*
1195:  * =========================================
1196:  * Assertion for Save Operations
1197:  * =========================================
1198:  */
1199: 
1200: export function assertValidExperience(experience) {
1201:   const validationResult = validateExperience(experience);
1202: 
1203:   if (validationResult.isValid) {
1204:     return validationResult;
1205:   }
1206: 
1207:   const error = new Error(
1208:     "The experience contains invalid or incomplete information.",
1209:   );
1210: 
1211:   error.name = "ExperienceValidationError";
1212:   error.code = "EXPERIENCE_VALIDATION_FAILED";
1213:   error.status = 400;
1214: 
1215:   error.validation = validationResult;
1216:   error.errors = validationResult.errors;
1217:   error.fieldErrors = validationResult.fieldErrors;
1218: 
1219:   throw error;
1220: }
1221: 
1222: /*
1223:  * =========================================
1224:  * Human-Readable Summary
1225:  * =========================================
1226:  */
1227: 
1228: export function getExperienceValidationSummary(experience) {
1229:   const validationResult = validateExperience(experience);
1230: 
1231:   if (!validationResult.isValid) {
1232:     return `${validationResult.errors.length} ${
1233:       validationResult.errors.length === 1 ? "problem must" : "problems must"
1234:     } be corrected before saving.`;
1235:   }
1236: 
1237:   if (validationResult.warnings.length > 0) {
1238:     return `Ready to save with ${validationResult.warnings.length} ${
1239:       validationResult.warnings.length === 1 ? "suggestion" : "suggestions"
1240:     } for improvement.`;
1241:   }
1242: 
1243:   return "This experience is complete and ready to save.";
1244: }
1245: 
1246: /*
1247:  * =========================================
1248:  * Development Description
1249:  * =========================================
1250:  */
1251: 
1252: export function getExperienceDescription(experience) {
1253:   const position = getObject(experience?.position);
1254: 
1255:   const organization = getObject(experience?.organization);
1256: 
1257:   const parts = [
1258:     getText(position.title),
1259:     getText(organization.name),
1260:     getEmploymentTypeLabel(position.employmentType),
1261:     getWorkArrangementLabel(position.workArrangement),
1262:     getExperienceCategoryLabel(experience?.category),
1263:     getExperienceStatusLabel(experience?.status),
1264:   ].filter(Boolean);
1265: 
1266:   return parts.join(" Â· ");
1267: }

FILE: src\services\Project\projectApiAdapter.js

   1: 
   2: const SERVER_OWNED_PROJECT_FIELDS = [
   3:   "userId",
   4:   "user_id",
   5:   "createdAt",
   6:   "updatedAt",
   7:   "rowVersion",
   8: ];
   9: 
  10: export function prepareProjectApiPayload(project) {
  11:   if (
  12:     !project ||
  13:     typeof project !== "object" ||
  14:     Array.isArray(project)
  15:   ) {
  16:     throw new TypeError("A valid Project object is required.");
  17:   }
  18: 
  19:   const payload = structuredClone(project);
  20: 
  21:   for (const field of SERVER_OWNED_PROJECT_FIELDS) {
  22:     delete payload[field];
  23:   }
  24: 
  25:   return payload;
  26: }
  27: 
  28: export function getProjectRowVersion(project) {
  29:   const rowVersion = project?.rowVersion;
  30: 
  31:   if (
  32:     typeof rowVersion !== "string" ||
  33:     !/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]{11}=$/.test(rowVersion)
  34:   ) {
  35:     throw new TypeError(
  36:       "A valid Project rowVersion is required.",
  37:     );
  38:   }
  39: 
  40:   return rowVersion;
  41: }

FILE: src\services\Project\projectApiService.js
===========================================================================
   1: 
   2: import {
   3:   apiGet,
   4:   apiPost,
   5:   apiPut,
   6:   apiPatch,
   7:   apiDelete,
   8: } from "../apiClient.js";
   9: 
  10: import { prepareProjectApiPayload } from "./projectApiAdapter.js";
  11: 
  12: const PROJECT_ENDPOINT = "/projects";
  13: 
  14: function projectPath(projectId) {
  15:   if (typeof projectId !== "string" || !projectId.trim()) {
  16:     throw new TypeError("A Project ID is required.");
  17:   }
  18: 
  19:   return `${PROJECT_ENDPOINT}/${encodeURIComponent(projectId.trim())}`;
  20: }
  21: 
  22: function requireRowVersion(rowVersion) {
  23:   if (typeof rowVersion !== "string" || !rowVersion.trim()) {
  24:     throw new TypeError("A Project rowVersion is required.");
  25:   }
  26: 
  27:   return rowVersion.trim();
  28: }
  29: 
  30: export async function fetchProjects(options = {}) {
  31:   const response = await apiGet(PROJECT_ENDPOINT, options);
  32:   return response.data.projects;
  33: }
  34: 
  35: export async function fetchProjectById(projectId, options = {}) {
  36:   const response = await apiGet(projectPath(projectId), options);
  37:   return response.data.project;
  38: }
  39: 
  40: export async function createProjectApi(project, options = {}) {
  41:   const response = await apiPost(PROJECT_ENDPOINT, prepareProjectApiPayload(project), options);
  42:   return response.data.project;
  43: }
  44: 
  45: export async function updateProjectApi(
  46:   projectId,
  47:   project,
  48:   rowVersion,
  49:   options = {},
  50: ) {
  51:   const response = await apiPut(
  52:     projectPath(projectId),
  53:     {
  54:       project: prepareProjectApiPayload(project),
  55:       rowVersion: requireRowVersion(rowVersion),
  56:     },
  57:     options,
  58:   );
  59: 
  60:   return response.data.project;
  61: }
  62: 
  63: export async function setProjectArchivedApi(
  64:   projectId,
  65:   archived,
  66:   rowVersion,
  67:   options = {},
  68: ) {
  69:   if (typeof archived !== "boolean") {
  70:     throw new TypeError("archived must be a boolean.");
  71:   }
  72: 
  73:   const response = await apiPatch(
  74:     `${projectPath(projectId)}/archive`,
  75:     {
  76:       archived,
  77:       rowVersion: requireRowVersion(rowVersion),
  78:     },
  79:     options,
  80:   );
  81: 
  82:   return response.data.project;
  83: }
  84: 
  85: export async function deleteProjectApi(
  86:   projectId,
  87:   rowVersion,
  88:   options = {},
  89: ) {
  90:   const response = await apiDelete(projectPath(projectId), {
  91:     ...options,
  92:     body: {
  93:       rowVersion: requireRowVersion(rowVersion),
  94:     },
  95:   });
  96: 
  97:   return response.data;
  98: }
  99:

FILE: src\services\Project\projectAssetResolver.js

   1: import {
   2:   createProjectAssetUrl,
   3:   revokeProjectAssetUrl,
   4: } from "./projectAssetStorage.js";
   5: 
   6: import {
   7:   createProjectMediaObjectUrl,
   8: } from "./projectMediaApiService.js";
   9: 
  10: import {
  11:   createProjectDocumentObjectUrl,
  12: } from "./projectDocumentApiService.js";
  13: 
  14: export function isBackendProjectAsset(asset) {
  15:   return ["backend", "azure-sql"].includes(
  16:     String(asset?.storageProvider || "").trim().toLowerCase(),
  17:   );
  18: }
  19: 
  20: export async function resolveProjectAssetUrl({
  21:   projectId,
  22:   asset,
  23:   kind,
  24:   signal,
  25: }) {
  26:   if (!asset) return "";
  27: 
  28:   if (isBackendProjectAsset(asset)) {
  29:     if (!projectId || !asset.backendAssetId) {
  30:       throw new Error("Project and asset IDs are required for backend files.");
  31:     }
  32: 
  33:     if (kind === "media") {
  34:       return createProjectMediaObjectUrl(projectId, asset.backendAssetId, { signal });
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     if (kind === "document") {
  38:       return createProjectDocumentObjectUrl(projectId, asset.backendAssetId, { signal });
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     throw new Error("Unsupported Project asset kind.");
  42:   }
  43: 
  44:   if (asset.storageKey) {
  45:     return createProjectAssetUrl(asset.storageKey);
  46:   }
  47: 
  48:   return asset.externalUrl || "";
  49: }
  50: 
  51: export function releaseProjectAssetUrl(url) {
  52:   if (typeof url === "string" && url.startsWith("blob:")) {
  53:     revokeProjectAssetUrl(url);
  54:   }
  55: }

  41:  * =========================================
  42:  * Storage Error
  43:  * =========================================
  44:  */
  45: 
  46: function createProjectAssetStorageError({
  47:   code = "PROJECT_ASSET_STORAGE_ERROR",
  48:   message,
  49:   publicMessage,
  50:   cause = null,
  51:   details = {},
  52: }) {
  53:   const error = new Error(message);
  54: 
  55:   error.name = "ProjectAssetStorageError";
  56:   error.code = code;
  57:   error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
  58: 
  59:   Object.assign(error, details);
  60: 
  61:   if (cause) {
  62:     error.cause = cause;
  63:   }
  64: 
  65:   return error;
  66: }

  71:  * =========================================
  72:  */
  73: 
  74: function isStorageQuotaError(error) {
  75:   return (
  76:     error?.name === "QuotaExceededError" ||
  77:     error?.name === "NS_ERROR_DOM_QUOTA_REACHED" ||
  78:     error?.code === 22 ||
  79:     error?.code === 1014
  80:   );
  81: }

  88: 
  89: function getIndexedDb() {

  91:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
  92:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE",
  93: 
  94:       message: "IndexedDB is unavailable.",
  95: 
  96:       publicMessage:
  97:         "Project media and document storage is not supported in this browser.",
  98:     });
  99:   }
 100: 
 101:   return window.indexedDB;
 102: }

 104: /*
 105:  * =========================================
 106:  * Promise Helpers
 107:  * =========================================
 108:  */
 109: 
 110: function requestToPromise(request) {
 111:   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 112:     request.onsuccess = () => {
 113:       resolve(request.result);
 114:     };
 115: 
 116:     request.onerror = () => {
 117:       reject(request.error);
 118:     };
 119:   });
 120: }
 121: 
 122: function transactionToPromise(transaction) {
 123:   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 124:     transaction.oncomplete = () => {
 125:       resolve();
 126:     };
 127: 
 128:     transaction.onerror = () => {
 129:       reject(transaction.error);
 130:     };
 131: 
 132:     transaction.onabort = () => {
 133:       reject(
 134:         transaction.error ||
 135:           new Error("The IndexedDB transaction was aborted."),
 136:       );
 137:     };
 138:   });
 139: }
 140: 
 141: /*
 142:  * =========================================
 143:  * Open Database
 144:  * =========================================
 145:  */
 146: 
 147: function openProjectAssetDatabase() {
 148:   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 149:     const request = getIndexedDb().open(
 150:       PROJECT_ASSET_DATABASE_NAME,
 151:       PROJECT_ASSET_DATABASE_VERSION,
 152:     );
 153: 
 154:     request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
 155:       const database = request.result;
 156: 
 157:       let store;
 158: 
 159:       if (!database.objectStoreNames.contains(PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME)) {
 160:         store = database.createObjectStore(PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME, {
 161:           keyPath: "storageKey",
 162:         });
 163:       } else {
 164:         store = request.transaction.objectStore(PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME);
 165:       }
 166: 
 167:       if (!store.indexNames.contains(PROJECT_ID_INDEX_NAME)) {
 168:         store.createIndex(PROJECT_ID_INDEX_NAME, "projectId", {
 169:           unique: false,
 170:         });
 171:       }
 172: 
 173:       if (!store.indexNames.contains(ASSET_KIND_INDEX_NAME)) {
 174:         store.createIndex(ASSET_KIND_INDEX_NAME, "kind", {
 175:           unique: false,
 176:         });
 177:       }
 178:     };
 179: 
 180:     request.onsuccess = () => {
 181:       const database = request.result;
 182: 
 183:       /*
 184:        * A tab holding an old database connection
 185:        * must close it when another tab upgrades
 186:        * the database.
 187:        */
 188: 
 189:       database.onversionchange = () => {
 190:         database.close();
 191:       };
 192: 
 193:       resolve(database);
 194:     };
 195: 
 196:     request.onerror = () => {
 197:       reject(
 198:         createProjectAssetStorageError({
 199:           code: "PROJECT_ASSET_DATABASE_OPEN_FAILED",
 200: 
 201:           message: "Unable to open Project asset storage.",
 202: 
 203:           publicMessage: "Project file storage could not be opened.",
 204: 
 205:           cause: request.error,
 206:         }),
 207:       );
 208:     };
 209: 
 210:     request.onblocked = () => {
 211:       reject(
 212:         createProjectAssetStorageError({
 213:           code: "PROJECT_ASSET_DATABASE_BLOCKED",
 214: 
 215:           message: "The Project asset database operation was blocked.",
 216: 
 217:           publicMessage: "Close other tabs using this portfolio and try again.",
 218:         }),
 219:       );
 220:     };
 221:   });
 222: }
 223: 
 224: /*
 225:  * =========================================
 226:  * Asset Kind
 227:  * =========================================
 228:  */
 229: 
 230: function normalizeAssetKind(kind) {
 231:   return kind === "document" ? "document" : "media";
 232: }
 233: 
 234: /*
 235:  * =========================================
 236:  * File Validation
 237:  * =========================================
 238:  */
 239: 
 240: function validateProjectAssetFile(file, kind) {
 241:   if (!file || typeof file !== "object" || typeof file.slice !== "function") {
 242:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 243:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_FILE_REQUIRED",
 244: 
 245:       message: "A valid File or Blob-like object is required.",
 246: 
 247:       publicMessage: "Select a valid Project file before uploading.",
 248:     });
 249:   }
 250: 
 251:   const isSupported =
 252:     kind === "media"
 253:       ? isSupportedProjectMedia(file)
 254:       : isSupportedProjectDocument(file);
 255: 
 256:   if (!isSupported) {
 257:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 258:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_FILE_TYPE_UNSUPPORTED",
 259: 
 260:       message: "The selected Project asset type is unsupported.",
 261: 
 262:       publicMessage:
 263:         kind === "media"
 264:           ? "Upload a supported JPG, PNG, WebP, GIF, MP4, or WebM file."
 265:           : "Upload a supported Project document type.",
 266:     });
 267:   }
 268: 
 269:   const isWithinSizeLimit =
 270:     kind === "media"
 271:       ? isProjectMediaWithinSizeLimit(file)
 272:       : isProjectDocumentWithinSizeLimit(file);
 273: 
 274:   if (!isWithinSizeLimit) {
 275:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 276:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_FILE_SIZE_INVALID",
 277: 
 278:       message: "The selected Project asset exceeds its size limit or is empty.",
 279: 
 280:       publicMessage:
 281:         kind === "media"
 282:           ? "The selected image or video is empty or exceeds the permitted size."
 283:           : "The selected document is empty or exceeds the permitted size.",
 284:     });
 285:   }
 286: }
 287: 
 288: /*
 289:  * =========================================
 290:  * Public Asset Metadata
 291:  * =========================================
 292:  */
 293: 
 294: function createAssetMetadata(record) {
 295:   if (!record) {
 296:     return null;
 297:   }
 298: 
 299:   return {
 300:     storageKey: record.storageKey,
 301: 
 302:     projectId: record.projectId,
 303: 
 304:     assetId: record.assetId,
 305: 
 306:     kind: record.kind,
 307: 
 308:     fileName: record.fileName,
 309: 
 310:     mimeType: record.mimeType,
 311: 
 312:     fileSize: record.fileSize,
 313: 
 314:     createdAt: record.createdAt,
 315: 
 316:     updatedAt: record.updatedAt,
 317:   };
 318: }
 319: 
 320: /*
 321:  * =========================================
 322:  * Write Asset
 323:  * =========================================
 324:  */
 325: 
 326: export async function writeProjectAsset({
 327:   file,
 328:   projectId,
 329:   assetId = "",
 330:   storageKey = "",
 331:   kind = "media",
 332: } = {}) {
 333:   const normalizedKind = normalizeAssetKind(kind);
 334: 
 335:   const normalizedProjectId = getText(projectId);
 336: 
 337:   if (!normalizedProjectId) {
 338:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 339:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_PROJECT_ID_REQUIRED",
 340: 
 341:       message: "A Project identifier is required before storing an asset.",
 342: 
 343:       publicMessage:
 344:         "The Project file cannot be saved because the Project could not be identified.",
 345:     });
 346:   }
 347: 
 348:   validateProjectAssetFile(file, normalizedKind);
 349: 
 350:   const resolvedAssetId = getText(assetId) || createProjectId("project-asset");
 351: 
 352:   const resolvedStorageKey =
 353:     getText(storageKey) ||
 354:     `project:${normalizedProjectId}:${normalizedKind}:${resolvedAssetId}`;
 355: 
 356:   const database = await openProjectAssetDatabase();
 357: 
 358:   const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
 359: 
 360:   try {
 361:     const transaction = database.transaction(
 362:       PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME,
 363:       "readwrite",
 364:     );
 365: 
 366:     const store = transaction.objectStore(PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME);
 367: 
 368:     /*
 369:      * The read and write are handled within the
 370:      * same request callback so the transaction
 371:      * cannot become inactive between operations.
 372:      */
 373: 
 374:     const existingRequest = store.get(resolvedStorageKey);
 375: 
 376:     const preparedRecordPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 377:       existingRequest.onerror = () => {
 378:         reject(existingRequest.error);
 379:       };
 380: 
 381:       existingRequest.onsuccess = () => {
 382:         const existingRecord = existingRequest.result;
 383: 
 384:         /*
 385:          * Never allow a storage key belonging
 386:          * to another Project to be overwritten.
 387:          */
 388: 
 389:         if (
 390:           existingRecord &&
 391:           existingRecord.projectId !== normalizedProjectId
 392:         ) {
 393:           transaction.abort();
 394: 
 395:           reject(
 396:             createProjectAssetStorageError({
 397:               code: "PROJECT_ASSET_STORAGE_KEY_CONFLICT",
 398: 
 399:               message:
 400:                 "The requested asset storage key belongs to another Project.",
 401: 
 402:               publicMessage:
 403:                 "The Project file could not be saved because its storage identifier conflicts with another file.",
 404:             }),
 405:           );
 406: 
 407:           return;
 408:         }
 409: 
 410:         const blob = file.slice(
 411:           0,
 412:           file.size,
 413:           file.type || "application/octet-stream",
 414:         );
 415: 
 416:         const record = {
 417:           storageKey: resolvedStorageKey,
 418: 
 419:           projectId: normalizedProjectId,
 420: 
 421:           assetId: resolvedAssetId,
 422: 
 423:           kind: normalizedKind,
 424: 
 425:           fileName: getText(file.name),
 426: 
 427:           mimeType: getText(file.type) || "application/octet-stream",
 428: 
 429:           fileSize: Number(file.size) || 0,
 430: 
 431:           blob,
 432: 
 433:           createdAt: existingRecord?.createdAt || timestamp,
 434: 
 435:           updatedAt: timestamp,
 436:         };
 437: 
 438:         const putRequest = store.put(record);
 439: 
 440:         putRequest.onerror = () => {
 441:           reject(putRequest.error);
 442:         };
 443: 
 444:         putRequest.onsuccess = () => {
 445:           resolve(record);
 446:         };
 447:       };
 448:     });
 449: 
 450:     const [record] = await Promise.all([
 451:       preparedRecordPromise,
 452:       transactionToPromise(transaction),
 453:     ]);
 454: 
 455:     return createAssetMetadata(record);
 456:   } catch (error) {
 457:     if (error?.name === "ProjectAssetStorageError") {
 458:       throw error;
 459:     }
 460: 
 461:     const quotaExceeded = isStorageQuotaError(error);
 462: 
 463:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 464:       code: quotaExceeded
 465:         ? "PROJECT_ASSET_QUOTA_EXCEEDED"
 466:         : "PROJECT_ASSET_WRITE_FAILED",
 467: 
 468:       message: quotaExceeded
 469:         ? "IndexedDB storage quota was exceeded."
 470:         : "Unable to save a Project asset.",
 471: 
 472:       publicMessage: quotaExceeded
 473:         ? "The browser does not have enough storage space for this Project file. Remove unused files or choose a smaller file."
 474:         : "The selected Project file could not be saved.",
 475: 
 476:       cause: error,
 477:     });
 478:   } finally {
 479:     database.close();
 480:   }
 481: }
 482: 
 483: /*
 484:  * =========================================
 485:  * Read Asset
 486:  * =========================================
 487:  */
 488: 
 489: export async function readProjectAsset(storageKey) {
 490:   const normalizedStorageKey = getText(storageKey);
 491: 
 492:   if (!normalizedStorageKey) {
 493:     return null;
 494:   }
 495: 
 496:   const database = await openProjectAssetDatabase();
 497: 
 498:   try {
 499:     const transaction = database.transaction(
 500:       PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME,
 501:       "readonly",
 502:     );
 503: 
 504:     const store = transaction.objectStore(PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME);
 505: 
 506:     const record = await requestToPromise(store.get(normalizedStorageKey));
 507: 
 508:     await transactionToPromise(transaction);
 509: 
 510:     return record || null;
 511:   } catch (error) {
 512:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 513:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_READ_FAILED",
 514: 
 515:       message: "Unable to read a Project asset.",
 516: 
 517:       publicMessage: "The Project file could not be loaded.",
 518: 
 519:       cause: error,
 520:     });
 521:   } finally {
 522:     database.close();
 523:   }
 524: }
 525: 
 526: /*
 527:  * =========================================
 528:  * Asset Existence
 529:  * =========================================
 530:  */
 531: 
 532: export async function hasProjectAsset(storageKey) {
 533:   const record = await readProjectAsset(storageKey);
 534: 
 535:   return Boolean(record?.blob);
 536: }
 537: 
 538: /*
 539:  * =========================================
 540:  * Create Object URL
 541:  * =========================================
 542:  */
 543: 
 544: export async function createProjectAssetUrl(storageKey) {
 545:   const normalizedStorageKey = getText(storageKey);
 546: 
 547:   if (!normalizedStorageKey) {
 548:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 549:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_STORAGE_KEY_REQUIRED",
 550: 
 551:       message: "An asset storage key is required.",
 552: 
 553:       publicMessage: "The Project file could not be identified.",
 554:     });
 555:   }
 556: 
 557:   const record = await readProjectAsset(normalizedStorageKey);
 558: 
 559:   if (!record?.blob) {
 560:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 561:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_NOT_FOUND",
 562: 
 563:       message: `Project asset not found: ${normalizedStorageKey}`,
 564: 
 565:       publicMessage:
 566:         "The Project file is no longer available in browser storage.",
 567:     });
 568:   }
 569: 
 570:   if (typeof URL === "undefined" || typeof URL.createObjectURL !== "function") {
 571:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 572:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_URL_UNAVAILABLE",
 573: 
 574:       message: "Object URLs are unavailable.",
 575: 
 576:       publicMessage:
 577:         "The Project file cannot be previewed in this environment.",
 578:     });
 579:   }
 580: 
 581:   return URL.createObjectURL(record.blob);
 582: }
 583: 
 584: /*
 585:  * =========================================
 586:  * Revoke Object URL
 587:  * =========================================
 588:  */
 589: 
 590: export function revokeProjectAssetUrl(objectUrl) {
 591:   if (
 592:     typeof objectUrl === "string" &&
 593:     objectUrl.startsWith("blob:") &&
 594:     typeof URL !== "undefined" &&
 595:     typeof URL.revokeObjectURL === "function"
 596:   ) {
 597:     URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl);
 598:   }
 599: }
 600: 
 601: /*
 602:  * =========================================
 603:  * List Project Asset Metadata
 604:  * =========================================
 605:  *
 606:  * Blob data is removed from the returned
 607:  * records to avoid unnecessarily retaining
 608:  * large file data in application state.
 609:  */
 610: 
 611: export async function listProjectAssets(projectId, { kind = "all" } = {}) {
 612:   const normalizedProjectId = getText(projectId);
 613: 
 614:   if (!normalizedProjectId) {
 615:     return [];
 616:   }
 617: 
 618:   const normalizedKind = kind === "media" || kind === "document" ? kind : "all";
 619: 
 620:   const database = await openProjectAssetDatabase();
 621: 
 622:   try {
 623:     const transaction = database.transaction(
 624:       PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME,
 625:       "readonly",
 626:     );
 627: 
 628:     const store = transaction.objectStore(PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME);
 629: 
 630:     const index = store.index(PROJECT_ID_INDEX_NAME);
 631: 
 632:     const records = await requestToPromise(index.getAll(normalizedProjectId));
 633: 
 634:     await transactionToPromise(transaction);
 635: 
 636:     return getArray(records)
 637:       .filter(
 638:         (record) => normalizedKind === "all" || record.kind === normalizedKind,
 639:       )
 640:       .map(createAssetMetadata);
 641:   } catch (error) {
 642:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 643:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_LIST_FAILED",
 644: 
 645:       message: "Unable to list Project assets.",
 646: 
 647:       publicMessage: "The Project file list could not be loaded.",
 648: 
 649:       cause: error,
 650:     });
 651:   } finally {
 652:     database.close();
 653:   }
 654: }
 655: 
 656: /*
 657:  * =========================================
 658:  * Delete Assets by Storage Key
 659:  * =========================================
 660:  */
 661: 
 662: export async function deleteProjectAssets(storageKeys = []) {
 663:   const normalizedStorageKeys = [
 664:     ...new Set(getArray(storageKeys).map(getText).filter(Boolean)),
 665:   ];
 666: 
 667:   if (normalizedStorageKeys.length === 0) {
 668:     return 0;
 669:   }
 670: 
 671:   const database = await openProjectAssetDatabase();
 672: 
 673:   try {
 674:     const transaction = database.transaction(
 675:       PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME,
 676:       "readwrite",
 677:     );
 678: 
 679:     const store = transaction.objectStore(PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME);
 680: 
 681:     normalizedStorageKeys.forEach((normalizedStorageKey) => {
 682:       store.delete(normalizedStorageKey);
 683:     });
 684: 
 685:     await transactionToPromise(transaction);
 686: 
 687:     return normalizedStorageKeys.length;
 688:   } catch (error) {
 689:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 690:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_DELETE_FAILED",
 691: 
 692:       message: "Unable to delete Project assets.",
 693: 
 694:       publicMessage: "One or more Project files could not be removed.",
 695: 
 696:       cause: error,
 697:     });
 698:   } finally {
 699:     database.close();
 700:   }
 701: }
 702: 
 703: /*
 704:  * =========================================
 705:  * Delete All Assets for Project
 706:  * =========================================
 707:  *
 708:  * A cursor is used instead of getAll(). This
 709:  * prevents every large Blob from being loaded
 710:  * into application memory before deletion.
 711:  */
 712: 
 713: export async function deleteProjectAssetsForProject(projectId) {
 714:   const normalizedProjectId = getText(projectId);
 715: 
 716:   if (!normalizedProjectId) {
 717:     return 0;
 718:   }
 719: 
 720:   const database = await openProjectAssetDatabase();
 721: 
 722:   try {
 723:     const transaction = database.transaction(
 724:       PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME,
 725:       "readwrite",
 726:     );
 727: 
 728:     const store = transaction.objectStore(PROJECT_ASSET_STORE_NAME);
 729: 
 730:     const index = store.index(PROJECT_ID_INDEX_NAME);
 731: 
 732:     if (typeof IDBKeyRange === "undefined") {
 733:       throw new Error("IDBKeyRange is unavailable.");
 734:     }
 735: 
 736:     const keyRange = IDBKeyRange.only(normalizedProjectId);
 737: 
 738:     /*
 739:      * This variable is updated while the cursor
 740:      * deletes each matching Project asset.
 741:      */
 742: 
 743:     let cursorDeletedCount = 0;
 744: 
 745:     const cursorPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 746:       const cursorRequest = index.openCursor(keyRange);
 747: 
 748:       cursorRequest.onerror = () => {
 749:         reject(
 750:           cursorRequest.error || new Error("The Project asset cursor failed."),
 751:         );
 752:       };
 753: 
 754:       cursorRequest.onsuccess = () => {
 755:         const cursor = cursorRequest.result;
 756: 
 757:         /*
 758:          * No cursor means every matching asset
 759:          * has been processed.
 760:          */
 761: 
 762:         if (!cursor) {
 763:           resolve(cursorDeletedCount);
 764: 
 765:           return;
 766:         }
 767: 
 768:         const deleteRequest = cursor.delete();
 769: 
 770:         deleteRequest.onerror = () => {
 771:           reject(
 772:             deleteRequest.error ||
 773:               new Error("A Project asset could not be deleted."),
 774:           );
 775:         };
 776: 
 777:         deleteRequest.onsuccess = () => {
 778:           cursorDeletedCount += 1;
 779: 
 780:           cursor.continue();
 781:         };
 782:       };
 783:     });
 784: 
 785:     /*
 786:      * Use a different variable name for the
 787:      * resolved count to avoid redeclaration.
 788:      */
 789: 
 790:     const [completedDeletedCount] = await Promise.all([
 791:       cursorPromise,
 792:       transactionToPromise(transaction),
 793:     ]);
 794: 
 795:     return completedDeletedCount;
 796:   } catch (error) {
 797:     throw createProjectAssetStorageError({
 798:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_PROJECT_DELETE_FAILED",
 799: 
 800:       message: "Unable to delete Project assets.",
 801: 
 802:       publicMessage: "One or more Project files could not be removed.",
 803: 
 804:       cause: error,
 805:     });
 806:   } finally {
 807:     database.close();
 808:   }
 809: }

FILE: src\services\Project\projectBinaryApi.js
===========================================================================
   1: import { API_BASE_URL, ApiError } from "../apiClient.js";
   2: 
   3: export async function fetchProjectBinary(endpoint, options = {}) {
   4:   if (
   5:     typeof endpoint !== "string" ||
   6:     !endpoint.startsWith("/projects/") ||
   7:     endpoint.startsWith("//")
   8:   ) {
   9:     throw new TypeError("A valid Project asset endpoint is required.");
  10:   }
  11: 
  12:   const controller = new AbortController();
  13:   const timeoutMs = options.timeoutMs ?? 180000;
  14:   let timedOut = false;
  15: 
  16:   const onAbort = () => controller.abort();
  17: 
  18:   if (options.signal?.aborted) {
  19:     controller.abort();
  20:   } else {
  21:     options.signal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
  22:   }
  23: 
  24:   const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
  25:     timedOut = true;
  26:     controller.abort();
  27:   }, timeoutMs);
  28: 
  29:   try {
  30:     const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
  31:       method: "GET",
  32:       credentials: "include",
  33:       headers: { Accept: "*/*" },
  34:       cache: "no-store",
  35:       signal: controller.signal,
  36:     });
  37: 
  38:     if (!response.ok) {
  39:       let errorData = null;
  40: 
  41:       try {
  42:         errorData = await response.json();
  43:       } catch {
  44:         // Non-JSON error responses are handled below.
  45:       }
  46: 
  47:       throw new ApiError({
  48:         message:
  49:           errorData?.message ||
  50:           `Project asset request failed (HTTP ${response.status}).`,
  51:         status: response.status,
  52:         code: errorData?.code || "PROJECT_ASSET_REQUEST_FAILED",
  53:         url: `${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`,
  54:         method: "GET",
  55:       });
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     return response.blob();
  59:   } catch (error) {
  60:     if (error instanceof ApiError) throw error;
  61: 
  62:     if (error?.name === "AbortError") {
  63:       throw new ApiError({
  64:         message: timedOut
  65:           ? "The Project asset download timed out."
  66:           : "The Project asset download was canceled.",
  67:         code: timedOut
  68:           ? "PROJECT_ASSET_TIMEOUT"
  69:           : "PROJECT_ASSET_ABORTED",
  70:         cause: error,
  71:       });
  72:     }
  73: 
  74:     throw new ApiError({
  75:       message: "Unable to download the Project asset.",
  76:       code: "PROJECT_ASSET_NETWORK_ERROR",
  77:       cause: error,
  78:     });
  79:   } finally {
  80:     clearTimeout(timeout);
  81:     options.signal?.removeEventListener?.("abort", onAbort);
  82:   }
  83: }
  84: 
  85: export async function createProjectBinaryObjectUrl(endpoint, options = {}) {
  86:   const blob = await fetchProjectBinary(endpoint, options);
  87: 
  88:   return URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  89: }

FILE: src\services\Project\projectDocumentApiService.js
