   1: import {
   2:   apiGet,
   3:   apiPost,
   4:   apiDelete,
   5: } from "../apiClient.js";
   6: 
   7: import {
   8:   fetchProjectBinary,
   9:   createProjectBinaryObjectUrl,
  10: } from "./projectBinaryApi.js";
  11: 
  12: function documentBasePath(projectId) {
  13:   if (typeof projectId !== "string" || !projectId.trim()) {
  14:     throw new TypeError("A Project ID is required.");
  15:   }
  16: 
  17:   return `/projects/${encodeURIComponent(projectId.trim())}/documents`;
  18: }
  19: 
  20: function documentPath(projectId, documentId) {
  21:   if (typeof documentId !== "string" || !documentId.trim()) {
  22:     throw new TypeError("A document ID is required.");
  23:   }
  24: 
  25:   return `${documentBasePath(projectId)}/${encodeURIComponent(documentId.trim())}`;
  26: }
  27: 
  28: export async function fetchProjectDocuments(projectId, options = {}) {
  29:   return apiGet(documentBasePath(projectId), options);
  30: }
  31: 
  32: export async function uploadProjectDocument(projectId, file, options = {}) {
  33:   if (!(file instanceof Blob)) {
  34:     throw new TypeError("A document File or Blob is required.");
  35:   }
  36: 
  37:   const formData = new FormData();
  38:   formData.append("document", file, file.name || "document");
  39: 
  40:   return apiPost(documentBasePath(projectId), formData, {
  41:     timeoutMs: 180000,
  42:     ...options,
  43:   });
  44: }
  45: 
  46: export async function fetchProjectDocumentBlob(
  47:   projectId,
  48:   documentId,
  49:   options = {},
  50: ) {
  51:   return fetchProjectBinary(
  52:     documentPath(projectId, documentId),
  53:     options,
  54:   );
  55: }
  56: 
  57: export async function createProjectDocumentObjectUrl(
  58:   projectId,
  59:   documentId,
  60:   options = {},
  61: ) {
  62:   return createProjectBinaryObjectUrl(
  63:     documentPath(projectId, documentId),
  64:     options,
  65:   );
  66: }
  67: 
  68: export async function deleteProjectDocument(
  69:   projectId,
  70:   documentId,
  71:   options = {},
  72: ) {
  73:   return apiDelete(documentPath(projectId, documentId), options);
  74: }

===========================================================================
FILE: src\services\Project\projectFeaturedMedia.js
===========================================================================
   1: function isEligibleFeaturedMedia(mediaItem) {
   2:   return Boolean(
   3:     mediaItem &&
   4:       mediaItem.status !== "archived" &&
   5:       mediaItem.visibility !== "private" &&
   6:       (mediaItem.storageKey || mediaItem.externalUrl),
   7:   );
   8: }
   9: 
  10: export function prepareFeaturedProjectMedia(
  11:   existingMedia = [],
  12:   uploadedMedia = [],
  13:   requestedFeaturedMediaId = "",
  14: ) {
  15:   const uploadedFeaturedMedia = uploadedMedia.find(
  16:     (item) => item.isFeatured && isEligibleFeaturedMedia(item),
  17:   );
  18: 
  19:   const previousMedia = existingMedia.map((item) => ({
  20:     ...item,
  21:     isFeatured: uploadedFeaturedMedia ? false : item.isFeatured,
  22:   }));
  23: 
  24:   const completeMedia = [...previousMedia, ...uploadedMedia];
  25: 
  26:   const featuredMedia =
  27:     uploadedFeaturedMedia ||
  28:     completeMedia.find(
  29:       (item) =>
  30:         item.id === requestedFeaturedMediaId &&
  31:         isEligibleFeaturedMedia(item),
  32:     ) ||
  33:     completeMedia.find(
  34:       (item) => item.isFeatured && isEligibleFeaturedMedia(item),
  35:     ) ||
  36:     completeMedia.find(isEligibleFeaturedMedia) ||
  37:     null;
  38: 
  39:   return {
  40:     media: completeMedia.map((item) => ({
  41:       ...item,
  42:       isFeatured: item.id === featuredMedia?.id,
  43:     })),
  44:     featuredMediaId: featuredMedia?.id || "",
  45:   };
  46: }

   1: import {
   2:   apiGet,
   3:   apiPost,
   4:   apiDelete,
   5: } from "../apiClient.js";
   6: 
   7: import {
   8:   fetchProjectBinary,
   9:   createProjectBinaryObjectUrl,
  10: } from "./projectBinaryApi.js";
  11: 
  12: function mediaBasePath(projectId) {
  13:   if (typeof projectId !== "string" || !projectId.trim()) {
  14:     throw new TypeError("A Project ID is required.");
  15:   }
  16: 
  17:   return `/projects/${encodeURIComponent(projectId.trim())}/media`;
  18: }
  19: 
  20: function mediaPath(projectId, mediaId) {
  21:   if (typeof mediaId !== "string" || !mediaId.trim()) {
  22:     throw new TypeError("A media ID is required.");
  23:   }
  24: 
  25:   return `${mediaBasePath(projectId)}/${encodeURIComponent(mediaId.trim())}`;
  26: }
  27: 
  28: export async function fetchProjectMedia(projectId, options = {}) {
  29:   return apiGet(mediaBasePath(projectId), options);
  30: }
  31: 
  32: export async function uploadProjectMedia(projectId, file, options = {}) {
  33:   if (!(file instanceof Blob)) {
  34:     throw new TypeError("A media File or Blob is required.");
  35:   }
  36: 
  37:   const formData = new FormData();
  38:   formData.append("media", file, file.name || "media");
  39: 
  40:   return apiPost(mediaBasePath(projectId), formData, {
  41:     timeoutMs: 180000,
  42:     ...options,
  43:   });
  44: }
  45: 
  46: export async function fetchProjectMediaBlob(
  47:   projectId,
  48:   mediaId,
  49:   options = {},
  50: ) {
  51:   return fetchProjectBinary(mediaPath(projectId, mediaId), options);
  52: }
  53: 
  54: export async function createProjectMediaObjectUrl(
  55:   projectId,
  56:   mediaId,
  57:   options = {},
  58: ) {
  59:   return createProjectBinaryObjectUrl(
  60:     mediaPath(projectId, mediaId),
  61:     options,
  62:   );
  63: }
  64: 
  65: export async function deleteProjectMedia(
  66:   projectId,
  67:   mediaId,
  68:   options = {},
  69: ) {
  70:   return apiDelete(mediaPath(projectId, mediaId), options);
  71: }

   1: import {
   2:   createProject as createProjectModel,
   3:   createProjectDocument,
   4:   createProjectMedia,
   5:   createPublicProject,
   6:   normalizeProject,
   7:   updateProjectModel,
   8: } from "../../models/projectModel.js";
   9: 
  10: import {
  11:   deleteProjectAssets,
  12:   deleteProjectAssetsForProject,
  13:   writeProjectAsset,
  14: } from "./projectAssetStorage.js";
  15: 
  16: import { readStoredProjects, replaceStoredProjects } from "./projectStorage.js";
  17: 
  18: import { validateProject } from "./projectValidation.js";
  19: import { prepareFeaturedProjectMedia } from "./projectFeaturedMedia.js";
  20: 
  21: /*
  22:  * =========================================
  23:  * Primitive Helpers
  24:  * =========================================
  25:  */
  26: 
  27: function getText(value) {
  28:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
  29: }
  30: 
  31: function getArray(value) {
  32:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  33: }
  34: 
  35: /*
  36:  * =========================================
  37:  * Service Error
  38:  * =========================================
  39:  */
  40: 
  41: function createProjectServiceError(code, message, publicMessage, details = {}) {
  42:   const error = new Error(message);
  43: 
  44:   error.name = "ProjectServiceError";
  45:   error.code = code;
  46:   error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
  47: 
  48:   Object.assign(error, details);
  49: 
  50:   return error;
  51: }
  52: 
  53: /*
  54:  * =========================================
  55:  * Validate Project for Storage
  56:  * =========================================
  57:  */
  58: 
  59: function validateForSave(project, collections) {
  60:   const validation = validateProject(project, collections);
  61: 
  62:   if (!validation.isValid) {
  63:     throw createProjectServiceError(
  64:       "PROJECT_VALIDATION_ERROR",
  65:       "Project validation failed.",
  66:       "Correct the highlighted Project fields before saving.",
  67:       {
  68:         validation,
  69:         errors: validation.errors,
  70:         warnings: validation.warnings,
  71:         fieldErrors: validation.fieldErrors,
  72:       },
  73:     );
  74:   }
  75: 
  76:   return validation;
  77: }
  78: 
  79: /*
  80:  * =========================================
  81:  * Project Identifier Validation
  82:  * =========================================
  83:  */
  84: 
  85: function requireProjectId(projectId) {
  86:   const normalizedProjectId = getText(projectId);
  87: 
  88:   if (!normalizedProjectId) {
  89:     throw createProjectServiceError(
  90:       "PROJECT_ID_REQUIRED",
  91:       "A Project identifier is required.",
  92:       "The selected Project could not be identified.",
  93:     );
  94:   }
  95: 
  96:   return normalizedProjectId;
  97: }
  98: 
  99: /*
 100:  * =========================================
 101:  * Find Project
 102:  * =========================================
 103:  */
 104: 
 105: function findProjectIndex(records, projectId) {
 106:   return records.findIndex((project) => project.id === projectId);
 107: }
 108: 
 109: /*
 110:  * =========================================
 111:  * Duplicate Identity
 112:  * =========================================
 113:  *
 114:  * Projects may share a title when they belong
 115:  * to different organizations or clients.
 116:  */
 117: 
 118: function createProjectIdentity(project) {
 119:   const title = getText(project?.title).normalize("NFKC").toLocaleLowerCase();
 120: 
 121:   const organization = getText(
 122:     project?.organization?.name || project?.organization?.clientName,
 123:   )
 124:     .normalize("NFKC")
 125:     .toLocaleLowerCase();
 126: 
 127:   return {
 128:     title,
 129:     organization,
 130:   };
 131: }
 132: 
 133: function ensureUniqueProject(records, candidate, excludedProjectId = "") {
 134:   const candidateIdentity = createProjectIdentity(candidate);
 135: 
 136:   if (!candidateIdentity.title) {
 137:     return;
 138:   }
 139: 
 140:   const duplicate = records.find((project) => {
 141:     if (project.id === excludedProjectId) {
 142:       return false;
 143:     }
 144: 
 145:     const existingIdentity = createProjectIdentity(project);
 146: 
 147:     /*
 148:      * When neither record identifies an
 149:      * organization, the normalized title alone
 150:      * is used to prevent accidental duplicates.
 151:      */
 152: 
 153:     return (
 154:       existingIdentity.title === candidateIdentity.title &&
 155:       existingIdentity.organization === candidateIdentity.organization
 156:     );
 157:   });
 158: 
 159:   if (!duplicate) {
 160:     return;
 161:   }
 162: 
 163:   throw createProjectServiceError(
 164:     "PROJECT_DUPLICATE",
 165:     `A matching Project already exists: ${duplicate.id}`,
 166:     candidateIdentity.organization
 167:       ? "A Project with this title and organization already exists."
 168:       : "A Project with this title already exists.",
 169:     {
 170:       project: duplicate,
 171: 
 172:       fieldErrors: {
 173:         title: "A matching Project record already exists.",
 174:       },
 175:     },
 176:   );
 177: }
 178: 
 179: /*
 180:  * =========================================
 181:  * Store Upload Collection
 182:  * =========================================
 183:  */
 184: 
 185: async function storeProjectUploads(projectId, uploads, kind) {
 186:   const uploadCollection = getArray(uploads);
 187: 
 188:   const metadata = [];
 189: 
 190:   const createdStorageKeys = [];
 191: 
 192:   try {
 193:     for (let index = 0; index < uploadCollection.length; index += 1) {
 194:       const upload = uploadCollection[index];
 195: 
 196:       if (!(upload?.file instanceof Blob)) {
 197:         throw createProjectServiceError(
 198:           "PROJECT_UPLOAD_FILE_INVALID",
 199:           `Project ${kind} upload does not contain a valid Blob.`,
 200:           `One of the selected Project ${
 201:             kind === "media" ? "media files" : "documents"
 202:           } is invalid.`,
 203:         );
 204:       }
 205: 
 206:       const storedAsset = await writeProjectAsset({
 207:         file: upload.file,
 208: 
 209:         projectId,
 210: 
 211:         assetId: upload.assetId || upload.id,
 212: 
 213:         storageKey: upload.storageKey,
 214: 
 215:         kind,
 216:       });
 217: 
 218:       createdStorageKeys.push(storedAsset.storageKey);
 219: 
 220:       const metadataValues = {
 221:         ...upload,
 222: 
 223:         /*
 224:          * File objects must never be included
 225:          * in localStorage metadata.
 226:          */
 227: 
 228:         file: undefined,
 229: 
 230:         id: storedAsset.assetId,
 231: 
 232:         fileName: storedAsset.fileName,
 233: 
 234:         mimeType: storedAsset.mimeType,
 235: 
 236:         fileSize: storedAsset.fileSize,
 237: 
 238:         storageProvider: "indexed-db",
 239: 
 240:         storageKey: storedAsset.storageKey,
 241: 
 242:         uploadedAt: storedAsset.updatedAt,
 243:       };
 244: 
 245:       metadata.push(
 246:         kind === "media"
 247:           ? createProjectMedia(metadataValues, index)
 248:           : createProjectDocument(metadataValues, index),
 249:       );
 250:     }
 251: 
 252:     return {
 253:       metadata,
 254:       createdStorageKeys,
 255:     };
 256:   } catch (error) {
 257:     /*
 258:      * If one upload fails, remove every asset
 259:      * already created during this collection.
 260:      */
 261: 
 262:     await deleteProjectAssets(createdStorageKeys).catch(() => {});
 263: 
 264:     throw error;
 265:   }
 266: }
 267: 
 268: /*
 269:  * =========================================
 270:  * Prepare Project Assets
 271:  * =========================================
 272:  */
 273: 
 274: async function prepareProjectAssets(
 275:   project,
 276:   { mediaUploads = [], documentUploads = [] } = {},
 277: ) {
 278:   const storedMedia = await storeProjectUploads(
 279:     project.id,
 280:     mediaUploads,
 281:     "media",
 282:   );
 283: 
 284:   try {
 285:     const storedDocuments = await storeProjectUploads(
 286:       project.id,
 287:       documentUploads,
 288:       "document",
 289:     );
 290: 
 291:     /*
 292:      * A staged upload selected in the Media
 293:      * Editor must take priority. Before upload,
 294:      * its ID cannot be resolved by normalizeProject
 295:      * because it is not yet part of project.media.
 296:      */
 297: 
 298:     const featuredMediaResult = prepareFeaturedProjectMedia(
 299:       getArray(project.media),
 300:       storedMedia.metadata,
 301:       getText(project.presentation?.featuredMediaId),
 302:     );
 303:     const preparedProject = normalizeProject({
 304:       ...project,
 305: 
 306:       media: featuredMediaResult.media,
 307: 
 308:       supportingDocuments: [
 309:         ...getArray(project.supportingDocuments),
 310: 
 311:         ...storedDocuments.metadata,
 312:       ],
 313: 
 314:       presentation: {
 315:         ...project.presentation,
 316: 
 317:         featuredMediaId: featuredMediaResult.featuredMediaId,
 318:       },
 319:     });
 320: 
 321:     return {
 322:       project: preparedProject,
 323: 
 324:       createdStorageKeys: [
 325:         ...storedMedia.createdStorageKeys,
 326:         ...storedDocuments.createdStorageKeys,
 327:       ],
 328:     };
 329:   } catch (error) {
 330:     /*
 331:      * Document storage failed after media was
 332:      * saved, so the new media must be rolled
 333:      * back as well.
 334:      */
 335: 
 336:     await deleteProjectAssets(storedMedia.createdStorageKeys).catch(() => {});
 337: 
 338:     throw error;
 339:   }
 340: }
 341: 
 342: /*
 343:  * =========================================
 344:  * Safe Removed-Asset Cleanup
 345:  * =========================================
 346:  */
 347: 
 348: async function removeUnreferencedAssets(project, requestedStorageKeys) {
 349:   const referencedStorageKeys = new Set([
 350:     ...getArray(project.media)
 351:       .map((mediaItem) => getText(mediaItem.storageKey))
 352:       .filter(Boolean),
 353: 
 354:     ...getArray(project.supportingDocuments)
 355:       .map((documentRecord) => getText(documentRecord.storageKey))
 356:       .filter(Boolean),
 357:   ]);
 358: 
 359:   const removableStorageKeys = [
 360:     ...new Set(getArray(requestedStorageKeys).map(getText).filter(Boolean)),
 361:   ].filter((storageKey) => !referencedStorageKeys.has(storageKey));
 362: 
 363:   if (removableStorageKeys.length === 0) {
 364:     return 0;
 365:   }
 366: 
 367:   return deleteProjectAssets(removableStorageKeys);
 368: }
 369: 
 370: /*
 371:  * =========================================
 372:  * Get Projects
 373:  * =========================================
 374:  */
 375: 
 376: export function getProjects({ includeArchived = false } = {}) {
 377:   const projects = readStoredProjects();
 378: 
 379:   return includeArchived
 380:     ? projects
 381:     : projects.filter((project) => project.recordStatus !== "archived");
 382: }
 383: 
 384: /*
 385:  * =========================================
 386:  * Get Project by ID
 387:  * =========================================
 388:  */
 389: 
 390: export function getProjectById(projectId, { includeArchived = true } = {}) {
 391:   const normalizedProjectId = requireProjectId(projectId);
 392: 
 393:   const project =
 394:     readStoredProjects().find((record) => record.id === normalizedProjectId) ||
 395:     null;
 396: 
 397:   if (project?.recordStatus === "archived" && !includeArchived) {
 398:     return null;
 399:   }
 400: 
 401:   return project;
 402: }
 403: 
 404: /*
 405:  * =========================================
 406:  * Public Projects
 407:  * =========================================
 408:  */
 409: 
 410: export function getPublicProjects() {
 411:   return getProjects({
 412:     includeArchived: false,
 413:   }).map(createPublicProject);
 414: }
 415: 
 416: /*
 417:  * =========================================
 418:  * Public Project by ID
 419:  * =========================================
 420:  */
 421: 
 422: export function getPublicProjectById(projectId) {
 423:   const project = getProjectById(projectId, {
 424:     includeArchived: false,
 425:   });
 426: 
 427:   return project ? createPublicProject(project) : null;
 428: }
 429: 
 430: /*
 431:  * =========================================
 432:  * Create Project
 433:  * =========================================
 434:  */
 435: 
 436: export async function createProject(
 437:   projectData,
 438:   {
 439:     collections = {},
 440:     mediaUploads = [],
 441:     documentUploads = [],
 442:     removedAssetStorageKeys = [],
 443:   } = {},
 444: ) {
 445:   const records = readStoredProjects();
 446: 
 447:   let project = createProjectModel(projectData);
 448: 
 449:   ensureUniqueProject(records, project);
 450: 
 451:   const preparedAssets = await prepareProjectAssets(project, {
 452:     mediaUploads,
 453:     documentUploads,
 454:   });
 455: 
 456:   project = preparedAssets.project;
 457: 
 458:   try {
 459:     const validation = validateForSave(project, collections);
 460: 
 461:     /*
 462:      * Store metadata only after every staged
 463:      * asset was written successfully.
 464:      */
 465: 
 466:     replaceStoredProjects([...records, project]);
 467: 
 468:     /*
 469:      * This collection is normally empty while
 470:      * creating, but accepting it keeps the
 471:      * service contract consistent.
 472:      */
 473: 
 474:     await removeUnreferencedAssets(project, removedAssetStorageKeys).catch(
 475:       () => {},
 476:     );
 477: 
 478:     return {
 479:       project,
 480:       warnings: validation.warnings,
 481:     };
 482:   } catch (error) {
 483:     /*
 484:      * Metadata was not saved, so every new
 485:      * IndexedDB asset must be removed.
 486:      */
 487: 
 488:     await deleteProjectAssets(preparedAssets.createdStorageKeys).catch(
 489:       () => {},
 490:     );
 491: 
 492:     throw error;
 493:   }
 494: }
 495: 
 496: /*
 497:  * =========================================
 498:  * Update Project
 499:  * =========================================
 500:  */
 501: 
 502: export async function updateProject(
 503:   projectId,
 504:   updates,
 505:   {
 506:     collections = {},
 507:     mediaUploads = [],
 508:     documentUploads = [],
 509:     removedAssetStorageKeys = [],
 510:   } = {},
 511: ) {
 512:   const normalizedProjectId = requireProjectId(projectId);
 513: 
 514:   const records = readStoredProjects();
 515: 
 516:   const projectIndex = findProjectIndex(records, normalizedProjectId);
 517: 
 518:   if (projectIndex < 0) {
 519:     throw createProjectServiceError(
 520:       "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND",
 521:       `Project not found: ${normalizedProjectId}`,
 522:       "The selected Project could not be found.",
 523:       {
 524:         projectId: normalizedProjectId,
 525:       },
 526:     );
 527:   }
 528: 
 529:   let project = updateProjectModel(records[projectIndex], updates);
 530: 
 531:   ensureUniqueProject(records, project, project.id);
 532: 
 533:   const preparedAssets = await prepareProjectAssets(project, {
 534:     mediaUploads,
 535:     documentUploads,
 536:   });
 537: 
 538:   project = preparedAssets.project;
 539: 
 540:   try {
 541:     const validation = validateForSave(project, collections);
 542: 
 543:     const nextRecords = [...records];
 544: 
 545:     nextRecords[projectIndex] = project;
 546: 
 547:     /*
 548:      * Save updated metadata before deleting
 549:      * old assets. If this write fails, existing
 550:      * files remain recoverable.
 551:      */
 552: 
 553:     replaceStoredProjects(nextRecords);
 554: 
 555:     /*
 556:      * Asset deletion occurs only after the new
 557:      * Project record has been saved.
 558:      */
 559: 
 560:     await removeUnreferencedAssets(project, removedAssetStorageKeys).catch(
 561:       () => {},
 562:     );
 563: 
 564:     return {
 565:       project,
 566:       warnings: validation.warnings,
 567:     };
 568:   } catch (error) {
 569:     /*
 570:      * Preserve all pre-existing files and remove
 571:      * only assets created during this attempt.
 572:      */
 573: 
 574:     await deleteProjectAssets(preparedAssets.createdStorageKeys).catch(
 575:       () => {},
 576:     );
 577: 
 578:     throw error;
 579:   }
 580: }
 581: 
 582: /*
 583:  * =========================================
 584:  * Archive Project
 585:  * =========================================
 586:  */
 587: 
 588: export async function archiveProject(projectId) {
 589:   const normalizedProjectId = requireProjectId(projectId);
 590: 
 591:   const records = readStoredProjects();
 592: 
 593:   const projectIndex = findProjectIndex(records, normalizedProjectId);
 594: 
 595:   if (projectIndex < 0) {
 596:     throw createProjectServiceError(
 597:       "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND",
 598:       `Project not found: ${normalizedProjectId}`,
 599:       "The selected Project could not be found.",
 600:       {
 601:         projectId: normalizedProjectId,
 602:       },
 603:     );
 604:   }
 605: 
 606:   const project = records[projectIndex];
 607: 
 608:   if (project.recordStatus === "archived") {
 609:     return project;
 610:   }
 611: 
 612:   const archivedProject = normalizeProject({
 613:     ...project,
 614: 
 615:     recordStatus: "archived",
 616: 
 617:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 618:   });
 619: 
 620:   const nextRecords = [...records];
 621: 
 622:   nextRecords[projectIndex] = archivedProject;
 623: 
 624:   replaceStoredProjects(nextRecords);
 625: 
 626:   return archivedProject;
 627: }
 628: 
 629: /*
 630:  * =========================================
 631:  * Restore Project
 632:  * =========================================
 633:  */
 634: 
 635: export async function restoreProject(projectId) {
 636:   const normalizedProjectId = requireProjectId(projectId);
 637: 
 638:   const records = readStoredProjects();
 639: 
 640:   const projectIndex = findProjectIndex(records, normalizedProjectId);
 641: 
 642:   if (projectIndex < 0) {
 643:     throw createProjectServiceError(
 644:       "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND",
 645:       `Project not found: ${normalizedProjectId}`,
 646:       "The selected Project could not be found.",
 647:       {
 648:         projectId: normalizedProjectId,
 649:       },
 650:     );
 651:   }
 652: 
 653:   const project = records[projectIndex];
 654: 
 655:   if (project.recordStatus === "active") {
 656:     return project;
 657:   }
 658: 
 659:   const restoredProject = normalizeProject({
 660:     ...project,
 661: 
 662:     recordStatus: "active",
 663: 
 664:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 665:   });
 666: 
 667:   const nextRecords = [...records];
 668: 
 669:   nextRecords[projectIndex] = restoredProject;
 670: 
 671:   replaceStoredProjects(nextRecords);
 672: 
 673:   return restoredProject;
 674: }
 675: 
 676: /*
 677:  * =========================================
 678:  * Delete Project Permanently
 679:  * =========================================
 680:  */
 681: 
 682: export async function deleteProject(projectId) {
 683:   const normalizedProjectId = requireProjectId(projectId);
 684: 
 685:   const records = readStoredProjects();
 686: 
 687:   const projectIndex = findProjectIndex(records, normalizedProjectId);
 688: 
 689:   if (projectIndex < 0) {
 690:     throw createProjectServiceError(
 691:       "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND",
 692:       `Project not found: ${normalizedProjectId}`,
 693:       "The selected Project could not be found.",
 694:       {
 695:         projectId: normalizedProjectId,
 696:       },
 697:     );
 698:   }
 699: 
 700:   const deletedProject = records[projectIndex];
 701: 
 702:   const nextRecords = records.filter(
 703:     (project) => project.id !== normalizedProjectId,
 704:   );
 705: 
 706:   /*
 707:    * Delete metadata first. If localStorage
 708:    * fails, the Project and all assets remain.
 709:    */
 710: 
 711:   replaceStoredProjects(nextRecords);
 712: 
 713:   /*
 714:    * Once metadata is removed, delete every
 715:    * IndexedDB asset owned by this Project.
 716:    *
 717:    * Cleanup is best effort because the Project
 718:    * metadata has already been deleted.
 719:    */
 720: 
 721:   let assetCleanupError = null;
 722: 
 723:   try {
 724:     await deleteProjectAssetsForProject(normalizedProjectId);
 725:   } catch (error) {
 726:     assetCleanupError = error;
 727:   }
 728: 
 729:   return {
 730:     ...deletedProject,
 731: 
 732:     assetCleanupWarning: assetCleanupError
 733:       ? "The Project was deleted, but one or more locally stored files could not be cleaned up."
 734:       : "",
 735:   };
 736: }
 737: 
 738: 
 739:

FILE: src\services\Project\projectStorage.js
===========================================================================
   1: import {
   2:   PROJECT_MODEL_VERSION,
   3:   normalizeProjectCollection,
   4: } from "../../models/projectModel.js";
   5: 
   6: /*
   7:  * =========================================
   8:  * Storage Configuration
   9:  * =========================================
  10:  */
  11: 
  12: export const PROJECT_STORAGE_KEY =
  13:   "portfolio-platform:projects";
  14: 
  15: export const PROJECT_STORAGE_RECOVERY_KEY =
  16:   "portfolio-platform:projects:recovery";
  17: 
  18: export const PROJECT_STORAGE_VERSION = 2;
  19: 
  20: /*
  21:  * =========================================
  22:  * Storage Error
  23:  * =========================================
  24:  */
  25: 
  26: function createProjectStorageError(
  27:   code,
  28:   message,
  29:   publicMessage,
  30:   cause = null,
  31: ) {
  32:   const error = new Error(message);
  33: 
  34:   error.name = "ProjectStorageError";
  35:   error.code = code;
  36:   error.publicMessage = publicMessage;
  37: 
  38:   if (cause) {
  39:     error.cause = cause;
  40:   }
  41: 
  42:   return error;
  43: }
  44: 
  45: /*
  46:  * =========================================
  47:  * Browser Storage
  48:  * =========================================
  49:  */
  50: 
  51: function getBrowserStorage() {
  52:   if (typeof window === "undefined") {
  53:     throw createProjectStorageError(
  54:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_ENVIRONMENT_UNAVAILABLE",
  55:       "Window is unavailable.",
  56:       "Project storage is not supported in this environment.",
  57:     );
  58:   }
  59: 
  60:   try {
  61:     if (!window.localStorage) {
  62:       throw new Error("localStorage is unavailable.");
  63:     }
  64: 
  65:     return window.localStorage;
  66:   } catch (cause) {
  67:     throw createProjectStorageError(
  68:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_ACCESS_DENIED",
  69:       "Browser localStorage could not be accessed.",
  70:       "Project storage is unavailable. Check your browser privacy and storage settings.",
  71:       cause,
  72:     );
  73:   }
  74: }
  75: 
  76: /*
  77:  * =========================================
  78:  * Empty Storage Document
  79:  * =========================================
  80:  */
  81: 
  82: function createEmptyProjectStorageDocument() {
  83:   return {
  84:     storageVersion: PROJECT_STORAGE_VERSION,
  85: 
  86:     projectModelVersion: PROJECT_MODEL_VERSION,
  87: 
  88:     projectRecords: [],
  89: 
  90:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  91:   };
  92: }
  93: 
  94: /*
  95:  * =========================================
  96:  * Recovery Copy
  97:  * =========================================
  98:  *
  99:  * If stored JSON is corrupted, retain its raw
 100:  * value under a recovery key before reporting
 101:  * the error. This avoids silently discarding
 102:  * potentially recoverable Project metadata.
 103:  */
 104: 
 105: function preserveRecoveryCopy(
 106:   storage,
 107:   rawValue,
 108:   reason,
 109: ) {
 110:   if (!rawValue) {
 111:     return;
 112:   }
 113: 
 114:   try {
 115:     storage.setItem(
 116:       PROJECT_STORAGE_RECOVERY_KEY,
 117:       JSON.stringify({
 118:         originalStorageKey: PROJECT_STORAGE_KEY,
 119:         reason,
 120:         recoveredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 121:         rawValue,
 122:       }),
 123:     );
 124:   } catch {
 125:     /*
 126:      * Recovery storage is best effort. Do not
 127:      * replace the original error if the browser
 128:      * cannot save the recovery copy.
 129:      */
 130:   }
 131: }
 132: 
 133: /*
 134:  * =========================================
 135:  * Record Collection Resolution
 136:  * =========================================
 137:  */
 138: 
 139: function getStoredRecordCollection(document) {
 140:   if (Array.isArray(document.projectRecords)) {
 141:     return document.projectRecords;
 142:   }
 143: 
 144:   if (Array.isArray(document.projects)) {
 145:     return document.projects;
 146:   }
 147: 
 148:   if (Array.isArray(document.records)) {
 149:     return document.records;
 150:   }
 151: 
 152:   /*
 153:    * A completely absent collection represents
 154:    * an empty library. A present collection with
 155:    * the wrong type represents corruption.
 156:    */
 157: 
 158:   const hasCollectionProperty =
 159:     Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
 160:       document,
 161:       "projectRecords",
 162:     ) ||
 163:     Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
 164:       document,
 165:       "projects",
 166:     ) ||
 167:     Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
 168:       document,
 169:       "records",
 170:     );
 171: 
 172:   if (!hasCollectionProperty) {
 173:     return [];
 174:   }
 175: 
 176:   throw createProjectStorageError(
 177:     "PROJECT_STORAGE_RECORDS_INVALID",
 178:     "The stored Project collection is not an array.",
 179:     "The saved Project Library contains an invalid record collection.",
 180:   );
 181: }
 182: 
 183: /*
 184:  * =========================================
 185:  * Storage Migration
 186:  * =========================================
 187:  */
 188: 
 189: function migrateProjectStorageDocument(
 190:   storedDocument,
 191: ) {
 192:   /*
 193:    * Original storage sometimes contained only
 194:    * the Project array.
 195:    */
 196: 
 197:   if (Array.isArray(storedDocument)) {
 198:     return {
 199:       document: {
 200:         ...createEmptyProjectStorageDocument(),
 201: 
 202:         projectRecords:
 203:           normalizeProjectCollection(
 204:             storedDocument,
 205:           ),
 206:       },
 207: 
 208:       wasMigrated: true,
 209:     };
 210:   }
 211: 
 212:   if (
 213:     !storedDocument ||
 214:     typeof storedDocument !== "object"
 215:   ) {
 216:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 217:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_DOCUMENT_INVALID",
 218:       "Stored Project data has an invalid structure.",
 219:       "The saved Project Library has an invalid format.",
 220:     );
 221:   }
 222: 
 223:   const storedVersion =
 224:     Number(storedDocument.storageVersion) || 1;
 225: 
 226:   if (storedVersion > PROJECT_STORAGE_VERSION) {
 227:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 228:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED",
 229:       `Stored Project version ${storedVersion} is newer than supported version ${PROJECT_STORAGE_VERSION}.`,
 230:       "This Project Library was created by a newer version of the application and cannot be opened safely.",
 231:     );
 232:   }
 233: 
 234:   const storedRecords =
 235:     getStoredRecordCollection(storedDocument);
 236: 
 237:   /*
 238:    * normalizeProjectCollection performs the
 239:    * model migration. It upgrades version-1
 240:    * Projects to the current model structure.
 241:    */
 242: 
 243:   const normalizedRecords =
 244:     normalizeProjectCollection(storedRecords);
 245: 
 246:   const wasMigrated =
 247:     storedVersion !== PROJECT_STORAGE_VERSION ||
 248:     Number(storedDocument.projectModelVersion) !==
 249:       PROJECT_MODEL_VERSION ||
 250:     !Array.isArray(storedDocument.projectRecords);
 251: 
 252:   return {
 253:     document: {
 254:       storageVersion: PROJECT_STORAGE_VERSION,
 255: 
 256:       projectModelVersion: PROJECT_MODEL_VERSION,
 257: 
 258:       projectRecords: normalizedRecords,
 259: 
 260:       updatedAt:
 261:         typeof storedDocument.updatedAt ===
 262:         "string"
 263:           ? storedDocument.updatedAt
 264:           : new Date().toISOString(),
 265:     },
 266: 
 267:     wasMigrated,
 268:   };
 269: }
 270: 
 271: /*
 272:  * =========================================
 273:  * Parse Storage Document
 274:  * =========================================
 275:  */
 276: 
 277: function parseProjectStorageDocument(rawValue) {
 278:   if (!rawValue) {
 279:     return {
 280:       document:
 281:         createEmptyProjectStorageDocument(),
 282: 
 283:       wasMigrated: false,
 284:     };
 285:   }
 286: 
 287:   let parsedValue;
 288: 
 289:   try {
 290:     parsedValue = JSON.parse(rawValue);
 291:   } catch (cause) {
 292:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 293:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_JSON_INVALID",
 294:       "Unable to parse stored Project data.",
 295:       "The saved Project Library could not be read because its data is damaged.",
 296:       cause,
 297:     );
 298:   }
 299: 
 300:   return migrateProjectStorageDocument(
 301:     parsedValue,
 302:   );
 303: }
 304: 
 305: /*
 306:  * =========================================
 307:  * Internal Document Write
 308:  * =========================================
 309:  */
 310: 
 311: function writeStorageDocument(
 312:   storage,
 313:   document,
 314: ) {
 315:   let serializedDocument;
 316: 
 317:   try {
 318:     serializedDocument = JSON.stringify(document);
 319:   } catch (cause) {
 320:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 321:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_SERIALIZATION_FAILED",
 322:       "Project storage could not be serialized.",
 323:       "Your Project records could not be prepared for storage.",
 324:       cause,
 325:     );
 326:   }
 327: 
 328:   try {
 329:     storage.setItem(
 330:       PROJECT_STORAGE_KEY,
 331:       serializedDocument,
 332:     );
 333:   } catch (cause) {
 334:     const isQuotaError =
 335:       cause?.name === "QuotaExceededError" ||
 336:       cause?.name === "NS_ERROR_DOM_QUOTA_REACHED" ||
 337:       cause?.code === 22 ||
 338:       cause?.code === 1014;
 339: 
 340:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 341:       isQuotaError
 342:         ? "PROJECT_STORAGE_QUOTA_EXCEEDED"
 343:         : "PROJECT_STORAGE_WRITE_FAILED",
 344: 
 345:       isQuotaError
 346:         ? "Project localStorage quota was exceeded."
 347:         : "Unable to write Project Library storage.",
 348: 
 349:       isQuotaError
 350:         ? "The browser does not have enough storage space for the Project Library metadata."
 351:         : "Your Project records could not be saved.",
 352: 
 353:       cause,
 354:     );
 355:   }
 356: 
 357:   return document;
 358: }
 359: 
 360: /*
 361:  * =========================================
 362:  * Read Project Storage
 363:  * =========================================
 364:  */
 365: 
 366: export function readProjectStorage({
 367:   persistMigration = true,
 368: } = {}) {
 369:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
 370: 
 371:   let rawValue;
 372: 
 373:   try {
 374:     rawValue = storage.getItem(
 375:       PROJECT_STORAGE_KEY,
 376:     );
 377:   } catch (cause) {
 378:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 379:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_READ_FAILED",
 380:       "Unable to read Project Library storage.",
 381:       "Your saved Project records could not be loaded.",
 382:       cause,
 383:     );
 384:   }
 385: 
 386:   try {
 387:     const {
 388:       document,
 389:       wasMigrated,
 390:     } = parseProjectStorageDocument(rawValue);
 391: 
 392:     /*
 393:      * Persist successful migrations so every
 394:      * future read uses the current schema.
 395:      */
 396: 
 397:     if (
 398:       rawValue &&
 399:       wasMigrated &&
 400:       persistMigration
 401:     ) {
 402:       return writeStorageDocument(
 403:         storage,
 404:         {
 405:           ...document,
 406:           updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 407:         },
 408:       );
 409:     }
 410: 
 411:     return document;
 412:   } catch (error) {
 413:     preserveRecoveryCopy(
 414:       storage,
 415:       rawValue,
 416:       error?.code || "PROJECT_STORAGE_READ_FAILED",
 417:     );
 418: 
 419:     if (error?.name === "ProjectStorageError") {
 420:       throw error;
 421:     }
 422: 
 423:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 424:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_READ_FAILED",
 425:       "Unable to read Project Library storage.",
 426:       "Your saved Project records could not be loaded.",
 427:       error,
 428:     );
 429:   }
 430: }
 431: 
 432: /*
 433:  * =========================================
 434:  * Write Project Storage
 435:  * =========================================
 436:  */
 437: 
 438: export function writeProjectStorage(
 439:   projectRecords,
 440: ) {
 441:   if (!Array.isArray(projectRecords)) {
 442:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 443:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_WRITE_INPUT_INVALID",
 444:       "Project records must be supplied as an array.",
 445:       "The Project Library could not be saved because the record collection is invalid.",
 446:     );
 447:   }
 448: 
 449:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
 450: 
 451:   const document = {
 452:     storageVersion: PROJECT_STORAGE_VERSION,
 453: 
 454:     projectModelVersion: PROJECT_MODEL_VERSION,
 455: 
 456:     projectRecords:
 457:       normalizeProjectCollection(
 458:         projectRecords,
 459:       ),
 460: 
 461:     updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 462:   };
 463: 
 464:   return writeStorageDocument(
 465:     storage,
 466:     document,
 467:   );
 468: }
 469: 
 470: /*
 471:  * =========================================
 472:  * Read Stored Projects
 473:  * =========================================
 474:  */
 475: 
 476: export function readStoredProjects() {
 477:   return readProjectStorage().projectRecords;
 478: }
 479: 
 480: /*
 481:  * =========================================
 482:  * Replace Stored Projects
 483:  * =========================================
 484:  */
 485: 
 486: export function replaceStoredProjects(
 487:   projectRecords,
 488: ) {
 489:   return writeProjectStorage(
 490:     projectRecords,
 491:   ).projectRecords;
 492: }
 493: 
 494: /*
 495:  * =========================================
 496:  * Update Stored Projects
 497:  * =========================================
 498:  */
 499: 
 500: export function updateStoredProjects(updater) {
 501:   if (typeof updater !== "function") {
 502:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 503:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_UPDATER_INVALID",
 504:       "The Project updater must be a function.",
 505:       "The Project Library could not be updated.",
 506:     );
 507:   }
 508: 
 509:   const currentRecords = readStoredProjects();
 510: 
 511:   /*
 512:    * Supply a new array so the updater cannot
 513:    * mutate the collection currently returned
 514:    * by the storage reader.
 515:    */
 516: 
 517:   const nextRecords = updater([
 518:     ...currentRecords,
 519:   ]);
 520: 
 521:   if (!Array.isArray(nextRecords)) {
 522:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 523:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_UPDATE_RESULT_INVALID",
 524:       "The Project updater did not return an array.",
 525:       "The Project Library could not be updated.",
 526:     );
 527:   }
 528: 
 529:   return replaceStoredProjects(nextRecords);
 530: }
 531: 
 532: /*
 533:  * =========================================
 534:  * Recovery Information
 535:  * =========================================
 536:  */
 537: 
 538: export function readProjectStorageRecovery() {
 539:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
 540: 
 541:   let rawRecoveryValue;
 542: 
 543:   try {
 544:     rawRecoveryValue = storage.getItem(
 545:       PROJECT_STORAGE_RECOVERY_KEY,
 546:     );
 547:   } catch {
 548:     return null;
 549:   }
 550: 
 551:   if (!rawRecoveryValue) {
 552:     return null;
 553:   }
 554: 
 555:   try {
 556:     return JSON.parse(rawRecoveryValue);
 557:   } catch {
 558:     return {
 559:       reason:
 560:         "PROJECT_STORAGE_RECOVERY_JSON_INVALID",
 561: 
 562:       rawValue: rawRecoveryValue,
 563:     };
 564:   }
 565: }
 566: 
 567: /*
 568:  * =========================================
 569:  * Clear Recovery Information
 570:  * =========================================
 571:  */
 572: 
 573: export function clearProjectStorageRecovery() {
 574:   const storage = getBrowserStorage();
 575: 
 576:   try {
 577:     storage.removeItem(
 578:       PROJECT_STORAGE_RECOVERY_KEY,
 579:     );
 580:   } catch (cause) {
 581:     throw createProjectStorageError(
 582:       "PROJECT_STORAGE_RECOVERY_CLEAR_FAILED",
 583:       "Unable to clear Project recovery information.",
 584:       "The Project recovery information could not be removed.",
 585:       cause,
 586:     );
 587:   }
 588: }

   1: import {
   2:   getProjectAwardPlacementLabel,
   3:   getProjectCategoryLabel,
   4:   getProjectLifecycleStatusLabel,
   5:   getProjectOwnershipLabel,
   6:   getProjectRecognitionTypeLabel,
   7:   getProjectSkillProficiencyLabel,

  12: /*
  13:  * =========================================
  14:  * Primitive Helpers
  15:  * =========================================
  16:  */
  17: 
  18: function getArray(value) {
  19:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  20: }
  21: 
  22: function getText(value) {
  23:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
  24: }
  25: 
  26: function getObject(value) {
  27:   return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
  28:     ? value
  29:     : {};
  30: }
  31: 
  32: function getSearchableText(value) {
  33:   return getText(value).normalize("NFKC").toLocaleLowerCase();
  34: }
  35: 
  36: /*
  37:  * =========================================
  38:  * Date Parsing
  39:  * =========================================
  40:  */
  41: 
  42: export function parseProjectDate(value) {
  43:   const normalizedValue = getText(value);
  44: 
  45:   if (!normalizedValue) {
  46:     return null;
  47:   }
  48: 
  49:   const match = normalizedValue.match(
  50:     /^(\d{4})-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))?$/,
  51:   );
  52: 
  53:   if (!match) {
  54:     return null;
  55:   }
  56: 
  57:   const year = Number(match[1]);
  58:   const month = Number(match[2]);
  59:   const day = match[3] ? Number(match[3]) : 1;
  60: 
  61:   const parsedDate = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  62: 
  63:   if (
  64:     match[3] &&
  65:     (parsedDate.getUTCFullYear() !== year ||
  66:       parsedDate.getUTCMonth() !== month - 1 ||
  67:       parsedDate.getUTCDate() !== day)
  68:   ) {
  69:     return null;
  70:   }
  71: 
  72:   return {
  73:     value: normalizedValue,
  74:     date: parsedDate,
  75:     timestamp: parsedDate.getTime(),
  76:     year,
  77:     month,
  78:     day,
  79:     hasDay: Boolean(match[3]),
  80:   };
  81: }
  82: 
  83: /*
  84:  * =========================================
  85:  * Date Sort Helpers
  86:  * =========================================
  87:  */
  88: 
  89: function getDateSortValue(value) {
  90:   return parseProjectDate(value)?.timestamp || 0;
  91: }
  92: 
  93: function getUpdatedSortValue(value) {
  94:   const timestamp = new Date(value || 0).getTime();
  95: 
  96:   return Number.isFinite(timestamp) ? timestamp : 0;
  97: }
  98: 
  99: /*
 100:  * =========================================
 101:  * Media Helpers
 102:  * =========================================
 103:  */
 104: 
 105: function isUsableMedia(item) {
 106:   return Boolean(
 107:     item &&
 108:     item.status !== "archived" &&
 109:     (getText(item.storageKey) || getText(item.externalUrl)),
 110:   );
 111: }
 112: 
 113: function isPublicUsableMedia(item) {
 114:   return Boolean(isUsableMedia(item) && item.visibility !== "private");
 115: }
 116: 
 117: /*
 118:  * =========================================
 119:  * Featured Media
 120:  * =========================================
 121:  */
 122: 
 123: export function getProjectFeaturedMedia(project) {
 124:   const activeMedia = getArray(project?.media).filter(isUsableMedia);
 125: 
 126:   return (
 127:     activeMedia.find(
 128:       (item) => item.id === project?.presentation?.featuredMediaId,
 129:     ) ||
 130:     activeMedia.find((item) => item.isFeatured) ||
 131:     activeMedia[0] ||
 132:     null
 133:   );
 134: }

 137:  * =========================================
 138:  * Public Featured Media
 139:  * =========================================
 140:  */
 141: 
 142: export function getProjectPublicFeaturedMedia(project) {
 143:   const activePublicMedia = getArray(project?.media).filter(
 144:     isPublicUsableMedia,
 145:   );
 146: 
 147:   return (
 148:     activePublicMedia.find(
 149:       (item) => item.id === project?.presentation?.featuredMediaId,
 150:     ) ||
 151:     activePublicMedia.find((item) => item.isFeatured) ||
 152:     activePublicMedia[0] ||
 153:     null
 154:   );
 155: }
 156: 
 157: /*
 158:  * =========================================
 159:  * Date Formatting
 160:  * =========================================
 161:  */
 162: 
 163: export function formatProjectDate(
 164:   value,
 165:   { locale = "en-US", includeDay = false, fallback = "" } = {},
 166: ) {
 167:   const parsedDate = parseProjectDate(value);
 168: 
 169:   if (!parsedDate) {
 170:     return fallback;
 171:   }
 172: 
 173:   const formatOptions =
 174:     includeDay && parsedDate.hasDay
 175:       ? {
 176:           month: "short",
 177:           day: "numeric",
 178:           year: "numeric",
 179:           timeZone: "UTC",
 180:         }
 181:       : {
 182:           month: "short",
 183:           year: "numeric",
 184:           timeZone: "UTC",
 185:         };
 186: 
 187:   try {
 188:     return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, formatOptions).format(
 189:       parsedDate.date,
 190:     );
 191:   } catch {
 192:     return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", formatOptions).format(
 193:       parsedDate.date,
 194:     );
 195:   }
 196: }
 197: 
 198: /*
 199:  * =========================================
 200:  * Date Range
 201:  * =========================================
 202:  */
 203: 
 204: export function getProjectDateRange(project, options = {}) {
 205:   const dates = getObject(project?.dates);
 206: 
 207:   const startDate = formatProjectDate(dates.startDate, options);
 208: 
 209:   const endDate = dates.isCurrent
 210:     ? "Present"
 211:     : formatProjectDate(dates.endDate, options);
 212: 
 213:   if (startDate && endDate) {
 214:     return `${startDate} â€“ ${endDate}`;
 215:   }
 216: 
 217:   if (startDate) {
 218:     return startDate;
 219:   }
 220: 
 221:   if (endDate) {
 222:     return endDate;
 223:   }
 224: 
 225:   return "Dates not provided";
 226: }
 227: 
 228: /*
 229:  * =========================================
 230:  * Project State Helpers
 231:  * =========================================
 232:  */
 233: 
 234: export function isProjectCurrent(project) {
 235:   return Boolean(
 236:     project?.dates?.isCurrent ||
 237:     ["in-progress", "maintained"].includes(project?.lifecycleStatus),
 238:   );
 239: }
 240: 
 241: export function isProjectCompleted(project) {
 242:   return project?.lifecycleStatus === "completed";
 243: }
 244: 
 245: export function hasProjectMedia(project) {
 246:   return getArray(project?.media).some(isUsableMedia);
 247: }
 248: 
 249: export function hasPublicProjectMedia(project) {
 250:   return getArray(project?.media).some(isPublicUsableMedia);
 251: }
 252: 
 253: export function hasProjectAwards(project) {
 254:   return getArray(project?.awards).length > 0;
 255: }
 256: 
 257: export function hasProjectDocuments(project) {
 258:   return getArray(project?.supportingDocuments).some(
 259:     (document) =>
 260:       document?.status !== "archived" &&
 261:       Boolean(getText(document?.storageKey) || getText(document?.externalUrl)),
 262:   );
 263: }
 264: 
 265: /*
 266:  * =========================================
 267:  * Relationship Count
 268:  * =========================================
 269:  */
 270: 
 271: export function getProjectRelationshipCount(project) {
 272:   const relatedRecords = getObject(project?.relatedRecords);
 273: 
 274:   const relatedRecordCount = [
 275:     relatedRecords.experienceRelationships,
 276:     relatedRecords.educationRelationships,
 277:     relatedRecords.trainingRelationships,
 278:     relatedRecords.certificationRelationships,
 279:   ].reduce((total, relationships) => total + getArray(relationships).length, 0);
 280: 
 281:   return relatedRecordCount + getArray(project?.skillRelationships).length;
 282: }
 283: 
 284: /*
 285:  * =========================================
 286:  * Impact Score
 287:  * =========================================
 288:  *
 289:  * This is a deterministic portfolio-evidence
 290:  * score used for sorting. It is not a claim
 291:  * about the project's real-world value.
 292:  */
 293: 
 294: export function getProjectImpactScore(project) {
 295:   const results = getObject(project?.results);
 296: 
 297:   const metrics = getArray(results.metrics);
 298: 
 299:   const awards = getArray(project?.awards);
 300: 
 301:   const documents = getArray(project?.supportingDocuments).filter(
 302:     (document) => document?.status !== "archived",
 303:   );
 304: 
 305:   let score = 0;
 306: 
 307:   if (getText(results.outcome)) {
 308:     score += 4;
 309:   }
 310: 
 311:   if (getText(results.problemsSolved)) {
 312:     score += 3;
 313:   }
 314: 
 315:   if (getText(results.impact)) {
 316:     score += 4;
 317:   }
 318: 
 319:   if (getText(results.beneficiariesAffected)) {
 320:     score += 2;
 321:   }
 322: 
 323:   score += Math.min(metrics.length, 5) * 3;
 324: 
 325:   score += Math.min(awards.length, 3) * 4;
 326: 
 327:   score += Math.min(documents.length, 3);
 328: 
 329:   if (project?.presentation?.isFeatured) {
 330:     score += 2;
 331:   }
 332: 
 333:   return score;
 334: }
 335: 
 336: /*
 337:  * =========================================
 338:  * Searchable Text
 339:  * =========================================
 340:  */
 341: 
 342: export function createProjectSearchText(project) {
 343:   const organization = getObject(project?.organization);
 344: 
 345:   const location = getObject(project?.location);
 346: 
 347:   const problem = getObject(project?.problem);
 348: 
 349:   const solution = getObject(project?.solution);
 350: 
 351:   const results = getObject(project?.results);
 352: 
 353:   const presentation = getObject(project?.presentation);
 354: 
 355:   const relatedRecords = getObject(project?.relatedRecords);
 356: 
 357:   const objectives = getArray(problem.objectives).map(
 358:     (item) => item?.text || item,
 359:   );
 360: 
 361:   const constraints = getArray(problem.constraints).map(
 362:     (item) => item?.text || item,
 363:   );
 364: 
 365:   const features = getArray(solution.features).map(
 366:     (item) => item?.text || item,
 367:   );
 368: 
 369:   const contributions = getArray(solution.contributions).map(
 370:     (item) => item?.text || item,
 371:   );
 372: 
 373:   const metrics = getArray(results.metrics).flatMap((metric) => [
 374:     metric?.label,
 375:     metric?.value,
 376:     metric?.description,
 377:   ]);
 378: 
 379:   const skills = getArray(project?.skillRelationships).flatMap(
 380:     (relationship) => [
 381:       relationship?.nameSnapshot,
 382:       relationship?.categorySnapshot,
 383:       relationship?.usageDescription,
 384: 
 385:       getProjectSkillProficiencyLabel(relationship?.demonstratedProficiency),
 386:     ],
 387:   );
 388: 
 389:   const technologies = getArray(project?.technologies).flatMap((technology) => [
 390:     technology?.name,
 391:     technology?.category,
 392:   ]);
 393: 
 394:   const relationships = [
 395:     ...getArray(relatedRecords.experienceRelationships),
 396: 
 397:     ...getArray(relatedRecords.educationRelationships),
 398: 
 399:     ...getArray(relatedRecords.trainingRelationships),
 400: 
 401:     ...getArray(relatedRecords.certificationRelationships),
 402:   ].flatMap((relationship) => [
 403:     relationship?.snapshot?.name,
 404:     relationship?.snapshot?.secondaryLabel,
 405:     relationship?.snapshot?.status,
 406:     relationship?.description,
 407:   ]);
 408: 
 409:   const awards = getArray(project?.awards).flatMap((award) => [
 410:     award?.name,
 411:     award?.organization,
 412: 
 413:     getProjectRecognitionTypeLabel(award?.recognitionType),
 414: 
 415:     getProjectAwardPlacementLabel(award?.placement),
 416: 
 417:     award?.description,
 418:   ]);
 419: 
 420:   const media = getArray(project?.media).flatMap((mediaItem) => [
 421:     mediaItem?.name,
 422:     mediaItem?.caption,
 423:     mediaItem?.altText,
 424:     mediaItem?.fileName,
 425:   ]);
 426: 
 427:   const documents = getArray(project?.supportingDocuments).flatMap(
 428:     (document) => [
 429:       document?.name,
 430:       document?.description,
 431:       document?.fileName,
 432:       document?.documentType,
 433:     ],
 434:   );
 435: 
 436:   return [
 437:     project?.title,
 438:     project?.role,
 439: 
 440:     organization.name,
 441:     organization.clientName,
 442: 
 443:     location.displayValue,
 444: 
 445:     getProjectCategoryLabel(project?.category),
 446: 
 447:     getProjectLifecycleStatusLabel(project?.lifecycleStatus),
 448: 
 449:     getProjectOwnershipLabel(project?.ownership),
 450: 
 451:     problem.statement,
 452:     problem.targetAudience,
 453:     problem.context,
 454:     problem.importance,
 455: 
 456:     solution.overview,
 457:     solution.approach,
 458:     solution.architecture,
 459: 
 460:     results.outcome,
 461:     results.problemsSolved,
 462:     results.impact,
 463:     results.beneficiariesAffected,
 464:     results.lessonsLearned,
 465:     results.futureImprovements,
 466: 
 467:     presentation.shortSummary,
 468:     presentation.caseStudy,
 469: 
 470:     ...objectives,
 471:     ...constraints,
 472:     ...features,
 473:     ...contributions,
 474:     ...metrics,
 475:     ...skills,
 476:     ...technologies,
 477:     ...relationships,
 478:     ...awards,
 479:     ...media,
 480:     ...documents,
 481:   ]
 482:     .map(getText)
 483:     .filter(Boolean)
 484:     .join(" ")
 485:     .normalize("NFKC")
 486:     .toLocaleLowerCase();
 487: }
 488: 
 489: /*
 490:  * =========================================
 491:  * Search Projects
 492:  * =========================================
 493:  */
 494: 
 495: export function searchProjects(projects, query) {
 496:   const normalizedQuery = getSearchableText(query);
 497: 
 498:   if (!normalizedQuery) {
 499:     return [...getArray(projects)];
 500:   }
 501: 
 502:   const queryTerms = normalizedQuery.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
 503: 
 504:   return getArray(projects).filter((project) => {
 505:     const searchableText = createProjectSearchText(project);
 506: 
 507:     return queryTerms.every((term) => searchableText.includes(term));
 508:   });
 509: }
 510: 
 511: /*
 512:  * =========================================
 513:  * Relationship Filter
 514:  * =========================================
 515:  */
 516: 
 517: function hasRelationshipType(project, relationshipType) {
 518:   switch (relationshipType) {
 519:     case "skill":
 520:       return getArray(project?.skillRelationships).length > 0;
 521: 
 522:     case "experience":
 523:       return (
 524:         getArray(project?.relatedRecords?.experienceRelationships).length > 0
 525:       );
 526: 
 527:     case "education":
 528:       return (
 529:         getArray(project?.relatedRecords?.educationRelationships).length > 0
 530:       );
 531: 
 532:     case "training":
 533:       return (
 534:         getArray(project?.relatedRecords?.trainingRelationships).length > 0
 535:       );
 536: 
 537:     case "certification":
 538:       return (
 539:         getArray(project?.relatedRecords?.certificationRelationships).length > 0
 540:       );
 541: 
 542:     default:
 543:       return true;
 544:   }
 545: }
 546: 
 547: /*
 548:  * =========================================
 549:  * Filter Projects
 550:  * =========================================
 551:  */
 552: 
 553: export function filterProjects(projects, filters = {}) {
 554:   const {
 555:     category = "all",
 556:     lifecycleStatus = "all",
 557:     ownership = "all",
 558:     featured = "all",
 559:     media = "all",
 560:     awarded = "all",
 561:     relationship = "all",
 562:   } = filters;
 563: 
 564:   return getArray(projects).filter((project) => {
 565:     if (category !== "all" && project.category !== category) {
 566:       return false;
 567:     }
 568: 
 569:     if (
 570:       lifecycleStatus !== "all" &&
 571:       project.lifecycleStatus !== lifecycleStatus
 572:     ) {
 573:       return false;
 574:     }
 575: 
 576:     if (ownership !== "all" && project.ownership !== ownership) {
 577:       return false;
 578:     }
 579: 
 580:     if (featured === "featured" && !project.presentation?.isFeatured) {
 581:       return false;
 582:     }
 583: 
 584:     if (featured === "not-featured" && project.presentation?.isFeatured) {
 585:       return false;
 586:     }
 587: 
 588:     const projectHasMedia = hasProjectMedia(project);
 589: 
 590:     if (media === "with-media" && !projectHasMedia) {
 591:       return false;
 592:     }
 593: 
 594:     if (media === "without-media" && projectHasMedia) {
 595:       return false;
 596:     }
 597: 
 598:     const projectHasAwards = hasProjectAwards(project);
 599: 
 600:     if (awarded === "awarded" && !projectHasAwards) {
 601:       return false;
 602:     }
 603: 
 604:     if (awarded === "not-awarded" && projectHasAwards) {
 605:       return false;
 606:     }
 607: 
 608:     if (relationship !== "all" && !hasRelationshipType(project, relationship)) {
 609:       return false;
 610:     }
 611: 
 612:     return true;
 613:   });
 614: }
 615: 
 616: /*
 617:  * =========================================
 618:  * Title Comparison
 619:  * =========================================
 620:  */
 621: 
 622: function compareProjectTitles(firstProject, secondProject) {
 623:   return getText(firstProject?.title).localeCompare(
 624:     getText(secondProject?.title),
 625:     undefined,
 626:     {
 627:       sensitivity: "base",
 628:     },
 629:   );
 630: }
 631: 
 632: /*
 633:  * =========================================
 634:  * Updated-Date Comparison
 635:  * =========================================
 636:  */
 637: 
 638: function compareRecentlyUpdated(firstProject, secondProject) {
 639:   const difference =
 640:     getUpdatedSortValue(secondProject?.updatedAt) -
 641:     getUpdatedSortValue(firstProject?.updatedAt);
 642: 
 643:   if (difference !== 0) {
 644:     return difference;
 645:   }
 646: 
 647:   return compareProjectTitles(firstProject, secondProject);
 648: }
 649: 
 650: /*
 651:  * =========================================
 652:  * Newest Start-Date Comparison
 653:  * =========================================
 654:  *
 655:  * Projects without a start date are placed
 656:  * at the bottom of the collection.
 657:  */
 658: 
 659: function compareNewestStarted(firstProject, secondProject) {
 660:   const firstStartDate = getDateSortValue(firstProject?.dates?.startDate);
 661: 
 662:   const secondStartDate = getDateSortValue(secondProject?.dates?.startDate);
 663: 
 664:   if (firstStartDate && !secondStartDate) {
 665:     return -1;
 666:   }
 667: 
 668:   if (!firstStartDate && secondStartDate) {
 669:     return 1;
 670:   }
 671: 
 672:   if (firstStartDate !== secondStartDate) {
 673:     return secondStartDate - firstStartDate;
 674:   }
 675: 
 676:   return compareRecentlyUpdated(firstProject, secondProject);
 677: }
 678: 
 679: /*
 680:  * =========================================
 681:  * Oldest Start-Date Comparison
 682:  * =========================================
 683:  *
 684:  * Projects without a start date remain at
 685:  * the bottom even when sorting oldest-first.
 686:  */
 687: 
 688: function compareOldestStarted(firstProject, secondProject) {
 689:   const firstStartDate = getDateSortValue(firstProject?.dates?.startDate);
 690: 
 691:   const secondStartDate = getDateSortValue(secondProject?.dates?.startDate);
 692: 
 693:   if (firstStartDate && !secondStartDate) {
 694:     return -1;
 695:   }
 696: 
 697:   if (!firstStartDate && secondStartDate) {
 698:     return 1;
 699:   }
 700: 
 701:   if (firstStartDate !== secondStartDate) {
 702:     return firstStartDate - secondStartDate;
 703:   }
 704: 
 705:   return compareProjectTitles(firstProject, secondProject);
 706: }
 707: 
 708: /*
 709:  * =========================================
 710:  * Portfolio Priority
 711:  * =========================================
 712:  */
 713: 
 714: function getPortfolioPriority(project) {
 715:   if (project?.presentation?.isFeatured) {
 716:     return 1;
 717:   }
 718: 
 719:   if (isProjectCurrent(project)) {
 720:     return 2;
 721:   }
 722: 
 723:   if (
 724:     isProjectCompleted(project) &&
 725:     (project?.dates?.endDate || project?.dates?.startDate)
 726:   ) {
 727:     return 3;
 728:   }
 729: 
 730:   if (project?.dates?.endDate || project?.dates?.startDate) {
 731:     return 4;
 732:   }
 733: 
 734:   return 5;
 735: }
 736: 
 737: /*
 738:  * =========================================
 739:  * Portfolio Priority Comparison
 740:  * =========================================
 741:  */
 742: 
 743: function comparePortfolioPriority(firstProject, secondProject) {
 744:   const firstPriority = getPortfolioPriority(firstProject);
 745: 
 746:   const secondPriority = getPortfolioPriority(secondProject);
 747: 
 748:   if (firstPriority !== secondPriority) {
 749:     return firstPriority - secondPriority;
 750:   }
 751: 
 752:   const currentDifference =
 753:     Number(isProjectCurrent(secondProject)) -
 754:     Number(isProjectCurrent(firstProject));
 755: 
 756:   if (currentDifference !== 0) {
 757:     return currentDifference;
 758:   }
 759: 
 760:   const firstRelevantDate = getDateSortValue(
 761:     firstProject?.dates?.isCurrent
 762:       ? firstProject?.dates?.startDate
 763:       : firstProject?.dates?.endDate || firstProject?.dates?.startDate,
 764:   );
 765: 
 766:   const secondRelevantDate = getDateSortValue(
 767:     secondProject?.dates?.isCurrent
 768:       ? secondProject?.dates?.startDate
 769:       : secondProject?.dates?.endDate || secondProject?.dates?.startDate,
 770:   );
 771: 
 772:   if (firstRelevantDate !== secondRelevantDate) {
 773:     return secondRelevantDate - firstRelevantDate;
 774:   }
 775: 
 776:   return compareRecentlyUpdated(firstProject, secondProject);
 777: }
 778: 
 779: /*
 780:  * =========================================
 781:  * Newest Completed Comparison
 782:  * =========================================
 783:  */
 784: 
 785: function compareNewestCompleted(firstProject, secondProject) {
 786:   const firstCompleted = isProjectCompleted(firstProject);
 787: 
 788:   const secondCompleted = isProjectCompleted(secondProject);
 789: 
 790:   if (firstCompleted !== secondCompleted) {
 791:     return Number(secondCompleted) - Number(firstCompleted);
 792:   }
 793: 
 794:   const firstDate = getDateSortValue(
 795:     firstProject?.dates?.endDate || firstProject?.dates?.startDate,
 796:   );
 797: 
 798:   const secondDate = getDateSortValue(
 799:     secondProject?.dates?.endDate || secondProject?.dates?.startDate,
 800:   );
 801: 
 802:   if (firstDate && !secondDate) {
 803:     return -1;
 804:   }
 805: 
 806:   if (!firstDate && secondDate) {
 807:     return 1;
 808:   }
 809: 
 810:   if (firstDate !== secondDate) {
 811:     return secondDate - firstDate;
 812:   }
 813: 
 814:   return compareProjectTitles(firstProject, secondProject);
 815: }
 816: 
 817: /*
 818:  * =========================================
 819:  * Sort Projects
 820:  * =========================================
 821:  */
 822: 
 823: export function sortProjects(projects, sortOption = "newest-started") {
 824:   const sortedProjects = [...getArray(projects)];
 825: 
 826:   sortedProjects.sort((firstProject, secondProject) => {
 827:     switch (sortOption) {
 828:       case "newest-started":
 829:         return compareNewestStarted(firstProject, secondProject);
 830: 
 831:       case "oldest":
 832:         return compareOldestStarted(firstProject, secondProject);
 833: 
 834:       case "title-ascending":
 835:         return compareProjectTitles(firstProject, secondProject);
 836: 
 837:       case "title-descending":
 838:         return compareProjectTitles(secondProject, firstProject);
 839: 
 840:       case "recently-updated":
 841:         return compareRecentlyUpdated(firstProject, secondProject);
 842: 
 843:       case "newest-completed":
 844:         return compareNewestCompleted(firstProject, secondProject);
 845: 
 846:       case "impact":
 847:         return (
 848:           getProjectImpactScore(secondProject) -
 849:             getProjectImpactScore(firstProject) ||
 850:           compareNewestStarted(firstProject, secondProject)
 851:         );
 852: 
 853:       case "portfolio-default":
 854:         return comparePortfolioPriority(firstProject, secondProject);
 855: 
 856:       default:
 857:         return compareNewestStarted(firstProject, secondProject);
 858:     }
 859:   });
 860: 
 861:   return sortedProjects;
 862: }
 863: 
 864: /*
 865:  * =========================================
 866:  * Search, Filter, and Sort
 867:  * =========================================
 868:  */
 869: 
 870: export function prepareProjectCollection({
 871:   projects = [],
 872:   query = "",
 873:   filters = {},
 874:   sortOption = "newest-started",
 875: } = {}) {
 876:   const searchedProjects = searchProjects(projects, query);
 877: 
 878:   const filteredProjects = filterProjects(searchedProjects, filters);
 879: 
 880:   return sortProjects(filteredProjects, sortOption);
 881: }
 882: 
 883: /*
 884:  * =========================================
 885:  * Project Statistics
 886:  * =========================================
 887:  */
 888: 
 889: export function getProjectStatistics(projects) {
 890:   const collection = getArray(projects);
 891: 
 892:   const active = collection.filter(
 893:     (project) => project.recordStatus !== "archived",
 894:   );
 895: 
 896:   const archived = collection.filter(
 897:     (project) => project.recordStatus === "archived",
 898:   );
 899: 
 900:   return {
 901:     total: collection.length,
 902: 
 903:     active: active.length,
 904: 
 905:     archived: archived.length,
 906: 
 907:     featured: active.filter((project) => project.presentation?.isFeatured)
 908:       .length,
 909: 
 910:     current: active.filter(isProjectCurrent).length,
 911: 
 912:     completed: active.filter(isProjectCompleted).length,
 913: 
 914:     awarded: active.filter(hasProjectAwards).length,
 915: 
 916:     withMedia: active.filter(hasProjectMedia).length,
 917: 
 918:     withPublicMedia: active.filter(hasPublicProjectMedia).length,
 919: 
 920:     withDocuments: active.filter(hasProjectDocuments).length,
 921: 
 922:     withRelationships: active.filter(
 923:       (project) => getProjectRelationshipCount(project) > 0,
 924:     ).length,
 925:   };
 926: }

 932:  */
 933: 
 934: export { createPublicProject };
