  17: /*
  18:  * =========================================
  19:  * Validation Result Helpers
  20:  * =========================================
  21:  */
  22: 
  23: function addError(errors, fieldErrors, field, code, message) {
  24:   errors.push({
  25:     field,
  26:     code,
  27:     message,
  28:   });
  29: 
  30:   if (!fieldErrors[field]) {
  31:     fieldErrors[field] = message;
  32:   }
  33: }
  34: 
  35: function addWarning(warnings, field, code, message) {
  36:   warnings.push({
  37:     field,
  38:     code,
  39:     message,
  40:   });
  41: }
  42: 
  43: /*
  44:  * =========================================
  45:  * Primitive Helpers
  46:  * =========================================
  47:  */
  48: 
  49: function getText(value) {
  50:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
  51: }
  52: 
  53: function getArray(value) {
  54:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  55: }
  56: 
  57: function getObject(value) {
  58:   return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
  59:     ? value
  60:     : {};
  61: }
  62: 
  63: /*
  64:  * =========================================
  65:  * URL Validation
  66:  * =========================================
  67:  */
  68: 
  69: function isValidUrl(value) {
  70:   const normalizedValue = getText(value);
  71: 
  72:   if (!normalizedValue) {
  73:     return true;
  74:   }
  75: 
  76:   try {
  77:     const url = new URL(normalizedValue);
  78: 
  79:     return url.protocol === "http:" || url.protocol === "https:";
  80:   } catch {
  81:     return false;
  82:   }
  83: }
  84: 
  85: /*
  86:  * =========================================
  87:  * Date Validation
  88:  * =========================================
  89:  */
  90: 
  91: function parseProjectDate(value) {
  92:   const normalizedValue = getText(value);
  93: 
  94:   if (!normalizedValue) {
  95:     return null;
  96:   }
  97: 
  98:   const match = normalizedValue.match(
  99:     /^(\d{4})-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])(?:-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))?$/,
 100:   );
 101: 
 102:   if (!match) {
 103:     return null;
 104:   }
 105: 
 106:   const year = Number(match[1]);
 107:   const month = Number(match[2]);
 108:   const day = match[3] ? Number(match[3]) : 1;
 109: 
 110:   const parsedDate = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
 111: 
 112:   if (
 113:     match[3] &&
 114:     (parsedDate.getUTCFullYear() !== year ||
 115:       parsedDate.getUTCMonth() !== month - 1 ||
 116:       parsedDate.getUTCDate() !== day)
 117:   ) {
 118:     return null;
 119:   }
 120: 
 121:   return parsedDate.getTime();
 122: }
 123: 
 124: /*
 125:  * =========================================
 126:  * Length Validation
 127:  * =========================================
 128:  */
 129: 
 130: function validateMaximumLength({
 131:   value,
 132:   maximumLength,
 133:   field,
 134:   label,
 135:   errors,
 136:   fieldErrors,
 137: }) {
 138:   if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > maximumLength) {
 139:     addError(
 140:       errors,
 141:       fieldErrors,
 142:       field,
 143:       "PROJECT_FIELD_TOO_LONG",
 144:       `${label} cannot exceed ${maximumLength} characters.`,
 145:     );
 146:   }
 147: }
 148: 
 149: /*
 150:  * =========================================
 151:  * Project Validation
 152:  * =========================================
 153:  */
 154: 
 155: export function validateProject(value, collections = {}) {
 156:   const source = getObject(value);
 157: 
 158:   const project = normalizeProject(source);
 159: 
 160:   const errors = [];
 161:   const warnings = [];
 162:   const fieldErrors = {};
 163: 
 164:   const sourceOrganization = getObject(source.organization);
 165: 
 166:   const sourceDates = getObject(source.dates);
 167: 
 168:   const sourceLinks = getObject(source.links);
 169: 
 170:   const sourceProblem = getObject(source.problem);
 171: 
 172:   const sourceSolution = getObject(source.solution);
 173: 
 174:   const sourceResults = getObject(source.results);
 175: 
 176:   const sourcePresentation = getObject(source.presentation);
 177: 
 178:   const sourcePrivateInformation = getObject(source.privateInformation);
 179: 
 180:   /*
 181:    * =========================================
 182:    * Required Fields
 183:    * =========================================
 184:    */
 185: 
 186:   if (!project.title) {
 187:     addError(
 188:       errors,
 189:       fieldErrors,
 190:       "title",
 191:       "PROJECT_TITLE_REQUIRED",
 192:       "Enter a project title.",
 193:     );
 194:   }
 195: 
 196:   if (!project.role) {
 197:     addError(
 198:       errors,
 199:       fieldErrors,
 200:       "role",
 201:       "PROJECT_ROLE_REQUIRED",
 202:       "Describe your role in this project.",
 203:     );
 204:   }
 205: 
 206:   if (!project.dates.startDate) {
 207:     addError(
 208:       errors,
 209:       fieldErrors,
 210:       "dates.startDate",
 211:       "PROJECT_START_DATE_REQUIRED",
 212:       "Enter the project start date.",
 213:     );
 214:   }
 215: 
 216:   /*
 217:    * =========================================
 218:    * Option Validation
 219:    * =========================================
 220:    */
 221: 
 222:   if (source.category && !isValidProjectCategory(source.category)) {
 223:     addError(
 224:       errors,
 225:       fieldErrors,
 226:       "category",
 227:       "PROJECT_CATEGORY_INVALID",
 228:       "Select a valid project category.",
 229:     );
 230:   }
 231: 
 232:   const suppliedLifecycleStatus =
 233:     source.lifecycleStatus || source.projectStatus;
 234: 
 235:   if (
 236:     suppliedLifecycleStatus &&
 237:     !isValidProjectLifecycleStatus(suppliedLifecycleStatus)
 238:   ) {
 239:     addError(
 240:       errors,
 241:       fieldErrors,
 242:       "lifecycleStatus",
 243:       "PROJECT_LIFECYCLE_INVALID",
 244:       "Select a valid project lifecycle status.",
 245:     );
 246:   }
 247: 
 248:   if (source.ownership && !isValidProjectOwnership(source.ownership)) {
 249:     addError(
 250:       errors,
 251:       fieldErrors,
 252:       "ownership",
 253:       "PROJECT_OWNERSHIP_INVALID",
 254:       "Select a valid project ownership type.",
 255:     );
 256:   }
 257: 
 258:   if (source.source && !isValidProjectSource(source.source)) {
 259:     addError(
 260:       errors,
 261:       fieldErrors,
 262:       "source",
 263:       "PROJECT_SOURCE_INVALID",
 264:       "Select a valid record source.",
 265:     );
 266:   }
 267: 
 268:   if (source.recordStatus && !isValidProjectRecordStatus(source.recordStatus)) {
 269:     addError(
 270:       errors,
 271:       fieldErrors,
 272:       "recordStatus",
 273:       "PROJECT_RECORD_STATUS_INVALID",
 274:       "Select a valid Project record status.",
 275:     );
 276:   }
 277: 
 278:   /*
 279:    * =========================================
 280:    * Date Validation
 281:    * =========================================
 282:    */
 283: 
 284:   const startDateValue = parseProjectDate(sourceDates.startDate);
 285: 
 286:   const endDateValue = parseProjectDate(sourceDates.endDate);
 287: 
 288:   if (sourceDates.startDate && startDateValue === null) {
 289:     addError(
 290:       errors,
 291:       fieldErrors,
 292:       "dates.startDate",
 293:       "PROJECT_START_DATE_INVALID",
 294:       "Enter a valid project start date.",
 295:     );
 296:   }
 297: 
 298:   if (sourceDates.endDate && endDateValue === null) {
 299:     addError(
 300:       errors,
 301:       fieldErrors,
 302:       "dates.endDate",
 303:       "PROJECT_END_DATE_INVALID",
 304:       "Enter a valid project end date.",
 305:     );
 306:   }
 307: 
 308:   if (
 309:     !project.dates.isCurrent &&
 310:     ["completed", "cancelled"].includes(project.lifecycleStatus) &&
 311:     !project.dates.endDate
 312:   ) {
 313:     addError(
 314:       errors,
 315:       fieldErrors,
 316:       "dates.endDate",
 317:       "PROJECT_END_DATE_REQUIRED",
 318:       "Enter the project completion or cancellation date.",
 319:     );
 320:   }
 321: 
 322:   if (
 323:     startDateValue !== null &&
 324:     endDateValue !== null &&
 325:     endDateValue < startDateValue
 326:   ) {
 327:     addError(
 328:       errors,
 329:       fieldErrors,
 330:       "dates.endDate",
 331:       "PROJECT_DATE_ORDER_INVALID",
 332:       "The end date cannot be earlier than the start date.",
 333:     );
 334:   }
 335: 
 336:   if (project.dates.isCurrent && project.dates.endDate) {
 337:     addError(
 338:       errors,
 339:       fieldErrors,
 340:       "dates.endDate",
 341:       "PROJECT_CURRENT_END_DATE_CONFLICT",
 342:       "An ongoing project cannot have an end date.",
 343:     );
 344:   }
 345: 
 346:   if (
 347:     project.dates.isCurrent &&
 348:     !["in-progress", "maintained"].includes(project.lifecycleStatus)
 349:   ) {
 350:     addError(
 351:       errors,
 352:       fieldErrors,
 353:       "lifecycleStatus",
 354:       "PROJECT_CURRENT_LIFECYCLE_CONFLICT",
 355:       "An ongoing project must be In Progress or Maintained.",
 356:     );
 357:   }
 358: 
 359:   if (
 360:     ["in-progress", "maintained"].includes(project.lifecycleStatus) &&
 361:     !project.dates.isCurrent
 362:   ) {
 363:     addWarning(
 364:       warnings,
 365:       "dates.isCurrent",
 366:       "PROJECT_CURRENT_STATUS_RECOMMENDED",
 367:       "Consider marking an In Progress or Maintained project as ongoing.",
 368:     );
 369:   }
 370: 
 371:   /*
 372:    * =========================================
 373:    * URL Validation
 374:    * =========================================
 375:    */
 376: 
 377:   Object.entries(sourceLinks).forEach(([fieldName, urlValue]) => {
 378:     if (!isValidUrl(urlValue)) {
 379:       addError(
 380:         errors,
 381:         fieldErrors,
 382:         `links.${fieldName}`,
 383:         "PROJECT_URL_INVALID",
 384:         "Enter a complete HTTP or HTTPS address.",
 385:       );
 386:     }
 387:   });
 388: 
 389:   if (!isValidUrl(sourceOrganization.website)) {
 390:     addError(
 391:       errors,
 392:       fieldErrors,
 393:       "organization.website",
 394:       "PROJECT_ORGANIZATION_URL_INVALID",
 395:       "Enter a complete HTTP or HTTPS organization address.",
 396:     );
 397:   }
 398: 
 399:   /*
 400:    * =========================================
 401:    * Maximum Field Lengths
 402:    * =========================================
 403:    */
 404: 
 405:   [
 406:     [source.title, PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.title, "title", "Project title"],
 407:     [source.role, PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.role, "role", "Project role"],
 408:     [
 409:       sourceOrganization.name,
 410:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.organizationName,
 411:       "organization.name",
 412:       "Organization name",
 413:     ],
 414:     [
 415:       sourceOrganization.clientName,
 416:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.clientName,
 417:       "organization.clientName",
 418:       "Client name",
 419:     ],
 420:     [
 421:       sourceProblem.statement,
 422:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.problemStatement,
 423:       "problem.statement",
 424:       "Problem statement",
 425:     ],
 426:     [
 427:       sourceProblem.targetAudience,
 428:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.targetAudience,
 429:       "problem.targetAudience",
 430:       "Target audience",
 431:     ],
 432:     [
 433:       sourceProblem.context,
 434:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.context,
 435:       "problem.context",
 436:       "Project context",
 437:     ],
 438:     [
 439:       sourceProblem.importance,
 440:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.importance,
 441:       "problem.importance",
 442:       "Project importance",
 443:     ],
 444:     [
 445:       sourceSolution.overview,
 446:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.overview,
 447:       "solution.overview",
 448:       "Solution overview",
 449:     ],
 450:     [
 451:       sourceSolution.approach,
 452:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.approach,
 453:       "solution.approach",
 454:       "Implementation approach",
 455:     ],
 456:     [
 457:       sourceSolution.architecture,
 458:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.architecture,
 459:       "solution.architecture",
 460:       "Architecture summary",
 461:     ],
 462:     [
 463:       sourceResults.outcome,
 464:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.outcome,
 465:       "results.outcome",
 466:       "Project outcome",
 467:     ],
 468:     [
 469:       sourceResults.problemsSolved,
 470:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.problemsSolved,
 471:       "results.problemsSolved",
 472:       "Problems solved",
 473:     ],
 474:     [
 475:       sourceResults.impact,
 476:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.impact,
 477:       "results.impact",
 478:       "Project impact",
 479:     ],
 480:     [
 481:       sourceResults.beneficiariesAffected,
 482:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.beneficiariesAffected,
 483:       "results.beneficiariesAffected",
 484:       "Affected beneficiaries",
 485:     ],
 486:     [
 487:       sourcePresentation.shortSummary,
 488:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.shortSummary,
 489:       "presentation.shortSummary",
 490:       "Portfolio summary",
 491:     ],
 492:     [
 493:       sourcePresentation.caseStudy,
 494:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.caseStudy,
 495:       "presentation.caseStudy",
 496:       "Detailed case study",
 497:     ],
 498:     [
 499:       sourcePrivateInformation.notes,
 500:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.privateNotes,
 501:       "privateInformation.notes",
 502:       "Private notes",
 503:     ],
 504:   ].forEach(([fieldValue, limit, field, label]) => {
 505:     validateMaximumLength({
 506:       value: fieldValue,
 507:       maximumLength: limit,
 508:       field,
 509:       label,
 510:       errors,
 511:       fieldErrors,
 512:     });
 513:   });
 514: 
 515:   /*
 516:    * =========================================
 517:    * Collection Limits
 518:    * =========================================
 519:    */
 520: 
 521:   const collectionLimits = [
 522:     [
 523:       sourceProblem.objectives,
 524:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumObjectives,
 525:       "problem.objectives",
 526:       "objectives",
 527:     ],
 528:     [
 529:       sourceProblem.constraints,
 530:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumConstraints,
 531:       "problem.constraints",
 532:       "constraints",
 533:     ],
 534:     [
 535:       sourceSolution.features,
 536:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumFeatures,
 537:       "solution.features",
 538:       "features",
 539:     ],
 540:     [
 541:       sourceSolution.contributions,
 542:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumContributions,
 543:       "solution.contributions",
 544:       "contributions",
 545:     ],
 546:     [
 547:       sourceResults.metrics,
 548:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumMetrics,
 549:       "results.metrics",
 550:       "metrics",
 551:     ],
 552:     [
 553:       source.skillRelationships,
 554:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumSkills,
 555:       "skillRelationships",
 556:       "related Skills",
 557:     ],
 558:     [
 559:       source.technologies,
 560:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumTechnologies,
 561:       "technologies",
 562:       "technologies",
 563:     ],
 564:     [source.awards, PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumAwards, "awards", "awards"],
 565:     [source.media, PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumMedia, "media", "media items"],
 566:     [
 567:       source.supportingDocuments,
 568:       PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.maximumDocuments,
 569:       "supportingDocuments",
 570:       "documents",
 571:     ],
 572:   ];
 573: 
 574:   collectionLimits.forEach(([collection, maximum, field, label]) => {
 575:     if (Array.isArray(collection) && collection.length > maximum) {
 576:       addError(
 577:         errors,
 578:         fieldErrors,
 579:         field,
 580:         "PROJECT_COLLECTION_LIMIT_EXCEEDED",
 581:         `Add no more than ${maximum} ${label}.`,
 582:       );
 583:     }
 584:   });
 585: 
 586:   /*
 587:    * =========================================
 588:    * Skills
 589:    * =========================================
 590:    */
 591: 
 592:   const availableSkillIds = Array.isArray(collections.skills)
 593:     ? new Set(collections.skills.map((skill) => skill?.id).filter(Boolean))
 594:     : null;
 595: 
 596:   project.skillRelationships.forEach((relationship, index) => {
 597:     if (!relationship.skillId && !relationship.nameSnapshot) {
 598:       addError(
 599:         errors,
 600:         fieldErrors,
 601:         `skillRelationships.${index}`,
 602:         "PROJECT_SKILL_IDENTITY_REQUIRED",
 603:         "A related Skill must have an identifier or readable snapshot.",
 604:       );
 605:     }
 606: 
 607:     if (
 608:       relationship.demonstratedProficiency &&
 609:       !isValidProjectSkillProficiency(relationship.demonstratedProficiency)
 610:     ) {
 611:       addError(
 612:         errors,
 613:         fieldErrors,
 614:         `skillRelationships.${index}.demonstratedProficiency`,
 615:         "PROJECT_SKILL_PROFICIENCY_INVALID",
 616:         "Select a valid demonstrated proficiency.",
 617:       );
 618:     }
 619: 
 620:     /*
 621:      * A missing central record is a warning.
 622:      * The snapshot keeps the Project readable.
 623:      */
 624: 
 625:     if (
 626:       availableSkillIds &&
 627:       relationship.skillId &&
 628:       !availableSkillIds.has(relationship.skillId)
 629:     ) {
 630:       addWarning(
 631:         warnings,
 632:         `skillRelationships.${index}`,
 633:         "PROJECT_SKILL_NOT_FOUND",
 634:         `The Skill â€œ${
 635:           relationship.nameSnapshot || relationship.skillId
 636:         }â€ is no longer available in the Skill Library. Its snapshot will be preserved.`,
 637:       );
 638:     }
 639:   });
 640: 
 641:   /*
 642:    * =========================================
 643:    * Related Professional Records
 644:    * =========================================
 645:    */
 646: 
 647:   const relationshipCollections = [
 648:     {
 649:       relationshipName: "experienceRelationships",
 650:       idField: "experienceId",
 651:       records: collections.experiences,
 652:       label: "Experience",
 653:     },
 654:     {
 655:       relationshipName: "educationRelationships",
 656:       idField: "educationId",
 657:       records: collections.educationRecords,
 658:       label: "Education",
 659:     },
 660:     {
 661:       relationshipName: "trainingRelationships",
 662:       idField: "trainingId",
 663:       records: collections.trainingRecords,
 664:       label: "Training",
 665:     },
 666:     {
 667:       relationshipName: "certificationRelationships",
 668:       idField: "certificationId",
 669:       records: collections.certifications,
 670:       label: "Certification",
 671:     },
 672:   ];
 673: 
 674:   relationshipCollections.forEach(
 675:     ({ relationshipName, idField, records, label }) => {
 676:       if (!Array.isArray(records)) {
 677:         return;
 678:       }
 679: 
 680:       const availableIds = new Set(
 681:         records.map((record) => record?.id).filter(Boolean),
 682:       );
 683: 
 684:       project.relatedRecords[relationshipName].forEach(
 685:         (relationship, index) => {
 686:           if (!relationship[idField]) {
 687:             addError(
 688:               errors,
 689:               fieldErrors,
 690:               `relatedRecords.${relationshipName}.${index}`,
 691:               "PROJECT_RELATIONSHIP_ID_REQUIRED",
 692:               `A related ${label} record must have an identifier.`,
 693:             );
 694: 
 695:             return;
 696:           }
 697: 
 698:           if (!availableIds.has(relationship[idField])) {
 699:             addWarning(
 700:               warnings,
 701:               `relatedRecords.${relationshipName}.${index}`,
 702:               "PROJECT_RELATIONSHIP_NOT_FOUND",
 703:               `A related ${label} record is no longer available. Its readable snapshot will be preserved.`,
 704:             );
 705:           }
 706:         },
 707:       );
 708:     },
 709:   );
 710: 
 711:   /*
 712:    * =========================================
 713:    * Awards and Recognition
 714:    * =========================================
 715:    */
 716: 
 717:   const documentIds = new Set(
 718:     project.supportingDocuments.map((documentRecord) => documentRecord.id),
 719:   );
 720: 
 721:   project.awards.forEach((award, index) => {
 722:     if (
 723:       award.recognitionType &&
 724:       !isValidProjectRecognitionType(award.recognitionType)
 725:     ) {
 726:       addError(
 727:         errors,
 728:         fieldErrors,
 729:         `awards.${index}.recognitionType`,
 730:         "PROJECT_RECOGNITION_TYPE_INVALID",
 731:         "Select a valid recognition type.",
 732:       );
 733:     }
 734: 
 735:     if (award.placement && !isValidProjectAwardPlacement(award.placement)) {
 736:       addError(
 737:         errors,
 738:         fieldErrors,
 739:         `awards.${index}.placement`,
 740:         "PROJECT_AWARD_PLACEMENT_INVALID",
 741:         "Select a valid award placement.",
 742:       );
 743:     }
 744: 
 745:     if (!isValidUrl(award.url)) {
 746:       addError(
 747:         errors,
 748:         fieldErrors,
 749:         `awards.${index}.url`,
 750:         "PROJECT_AWARD_URL_INVALID",
 751:         "Enter a complete HTTP or HTTPS award address.",
 752:       );
 753:     }
 754: 
 755:     award.supportingDocumentIds.forEach((documentId) => {
 756:       if (!documentIds.has(documentId)) {
 757:         addError(
 758:           errors,
 759:           fieldErrors,
 760:           `awards.${index}.supportingDocumentIds`,
 761:           "PROJECT_AWARD_DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND",
 762:           "An award references a Project document that is no longer available.",
 763:         );
 764:       }
 765:     });
 766:   });
 767: 
 768:   /*
 769:    * =========================================
 770:    * Media
 771:    * =========================================
 772:    */
 773: 
 774:   project.media.forEach((mediaItem, index) => {
 775:     if (!isValidProjectMediaType(mediaItem.type)) {
 776:       addError(
 777:         errors,
 778:         fieldErrors,
 779:         `media.${index}.type`,
 780:         "PROJECT_MEDIA_TYPE_INVALID",
 781:         "Select a valid project media type.",
 782:       );
 783:     }
 784: 
 785:     if (!mediaItem.storageKey && !mediaItem.externalUrl) {
 786:       addError(
 787:         errors,
 788:         fieldErrors,
 789:         `media.${index}`,
 790:         "PROJECT_MEDIA_SOURCE_REQUIRED",
 791:         "Project media must have a stored file or external address.",
 792:       );
 793:     }
 794: 
 795:     if (!isValidUrl(mediaItem.externalUrl)) {
 796:       addError(
 797:         errors,
 798:         fieldErrors,
 799:         `media.${index}.externalUrl`,
 800:         "PROJECT_MEDIA_URL_INVALID",
 801:         "Enter a complete HTTP or HTTPS media address.",
 802:       );
 803:     }
 804: 
 805:     if (mediaItem.type !== "video" && !mediaItem.altText) {
 806:       addWarning(
 807:         warnings,
 808:         `media.${index}.altText`,
 809:         "PROJECT_MEDIA_ALT_TEXT_RECOMMENDED",
 810:         `Add alternative text for â€œ${
 811:           mediaItem.name || "this image"
 812:         }â€ to improve accessibility.`,
 813:       );
 814:     }
 815:   });
 816: 
 817:   const activeMedia = project.media.filter(
 818:     (mediaItem) => mediaItem.status !== "archived",
 819:   );
 820: 
 821:   const featuredMediaId = project.presentation.featuredMediaId;
 822: 
 823:   if (
 824:     featuredMediaId &&
 825:     !activeMedia.some((mediaItem) => mediaItem.id === featuredMediaId)
 826:   ) {
 827:     addError(
 828:       errors,
 829:       fieldErrors,
 830:       "presentation.featuredMediaId",
 831:       "PROJECT_FEATURED_MEDIA_NOT_FOUND",
 832:       "The selected featured media is no longer available.",
 833:     );
 834:   }
 835: 
 836:   /*
 837:    * =========================================
 838:    * Supporting Documents
 839:    * =========================================
 840:    */
 841: 
 842:   project.supportingDocuments.forEach((documentRecord, index) => {
 843:     if (!isValidProjectDocumentType(documentRecord.documentType)) {
 844:       addError(
 845:         errors,
 846:         fieldErrors,
 847:         `supportingDocuments.${index}.documentType`,
 848:         "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_TYPE_INVALID",
 849:         "Select a valid Project document type.",
 850:       );
 851:     }
 852: 
 853:     if (!documentRecord.storageKey && !documentRecord.externalUrl) {
 854:       addError(
 855:         errors,
 856:         fieldErrors,
 857:         `supportingDocuments.${index}`,
 858:         "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_SOURCE_REQUIRED",
 859:         "A Project document must have a stored file or external address.",
 860:       );
 861:     }
 862: 
 863:     if (!isValidUrl(documentRecord.externalUrl)) {
 864:       addError(
 865:         errors,
 866:         fieldErrors,
 867:         `supportingDocuments.${index}.externalUrl`,
 868:         "PROJECT_DOCUMENT_URL_INVALID",
 869:         "Enter a complete HTTP or HTTPS document address.",
 870:       );
 871:     }
 872:   });
 873: 
 874:   /*
 875:    * =========================================
 876:    * Professional Completeness Warnings
 877:    * =========================================
 878:    */
 879: 
 880:   if (!project.problem.statement) {
 881:     addWarning(
 882:       warnings,
 883:       "problem.statement",
 884:       "PROJECT_PROBLEM_RECOMMENDED",
 885:       "Add the problem or opportunity the project addressed.",
 886:     );
 887:   }
 888: 
 889:   if (!project.problem.importance) {
 890:     addWarning(
 891:       warnings,
 892:       "problem.importance",
 893:       "PROJECT_IMPORTANCE_RECOMMENDED",
 894:       "Explain why this project was professionally important.",
 895:     );
 896:   }
 897: 
 898:   if (!project.solution.overview) {
 899:     addWarning(
 900:       warnings,
 901:       "solution.overview",
 902:       "PROJECT_SOLUTION_RECOMMENDED",
 903:       "Add a concise explanation of the solution.",
 904:     );
 905:   }
 906: 
 907:   if (project.solution.contributions.length === 0) {
 908:     addWarning(
 909:       warnings,
 910:       "solution.contributions",
 911:       "PROJECT_CONTRIBUTIONS_RECOMMENDED",
 912:       "Describe your personal contributions to the project.",
 913:     );
 914:   }
 915: 
 916:   if (project.lifecycleStatus === "completed" && !project.results.outcome) {
 917:     addWarning(
 918:       warnings,
 919:       "results.outcome",
 920:       "PROJECT_OUTCOME_RECOMMENDED",
 921:       "Add the completed Projectâ€™s final outcome.",
 922:     );
 923:   }
 924: 
 925:   if (
 926:     project.lifecycleStatus === "completed" &&
 927:     !project.results.problemsSolved
 928:   ) {
 929:     addWarning(
 930:       warnings,
 931:       "results.problemsSolved",
 932:       "PROJECT_PROBLEMS_SOLVED_RECOMMENDED",
 933:       "Explain which problems the completed project solved.",
 934:     );
 935:   }
 936: 
 937:   if (
 938:     project.lifecycleStatus === "completed" &&
 939:     project.results.metrics.length === 0
 940:   ) {
 941:     addWarning(
 942:       warnings,
 943:       "results.metrics",
 944:       "PROJECT_METRICS_RECOMMENDED",
 945:       "Add measurable evidence of the projectâ€™s result when available.",
 946:     );
 947:   }
 948: 
 949:   if (!project.presentation.shortSummary) {
 950:     addWarning(
 951:       warnings,
 952:       "presentation.shortSummary",
 953:       "PROJECT_SUMMARY_RECOMMENDED",
 954:       `Add a portfolio summary of up to ${PROJECT_FIELD_LIMITS.shortSummary} characters.`,
 955:     );
 956:   }
 957: 
 958:   if (activeMedia.length === 0) {
 959:     addWarning(
 960:       warnings,
 961:       "media",
 962:       "PROJECT_MEDIA_RECOMMENDED",
 963:       "Add a featured image or video to strengthen the portfolio presentation.",
 964:     );
 965:   }
 966: 
 967:   if (project.presentation.isFeatured && activeMedia.length === 0) {
 968:     addWarning(
 969:       warnings,
 970:       "presentation.isFeatured",
 971:       "PROJECT_FEATURED_MEDIA_RECOMMENDED",
 972:       "A featured Project should have a strong public image or video.",
 973:     );
 974:   }
 975: 
 976:   return {
 977:     isValid: errors.length === 0,
 978: 
 979:     project,
 980: 
 981:     errors,
 982: 
 983:     warnings,
 984: 
 985:     fieldErrors,
 986:   };
 987: }
 988: 
 989: /*
 990:  * =========================================
 991:  * Validation Summary
 992:  * =========================================
 993:  */
 994: 
 995: export function getProjectValidationSummary(value) {
 996:   const project = normalizeProject(value);
 997: 
 998:   const activePublicMedia = project.media.filter(
 999:     (mediaItem) =>
1000:       mediaItem.status !== "archived" && mediaItem.visibility === "public",
1001:   );
1002: 
1003:   const checks = [
1004:     {
1005:       id: "identity",
1006:       complete: Boolean(project.title && project.role),
1007:     },
1008:     {
1009:       id: "timeline",
1010:       complete: Boolean(
1011:         project.dates.startDate &&
1012:         (project.dates.isCurrent ||
1013:           project.dates.endDate ||
1014:           !["completed", "cancelled"].includes(project.lifecycleStatus)),
1015:       ),
1016:     },
1017:     {
1018:       id: "problem",
1019:       complete: Boolean(project.problem.statement),
1020:     },
1021:     {
1022:       id: "importance",
1023:       complete: Boolean(project.problem.importance),
1024:     },
1025:     {
1026:       id: "solution",
1027:       complete: Boolean(project.solution.overview),
1028:     },
1029:     {
1030:       id: "contributions",
1031:       complete: project.solution.contributions.length > 0,
1032:     },
1033:     {
1034:       id: "results",
1035:       complete: Boolean(
1036:         project.results.outcome || project.lifecycleStatus !== "completed",
1037:       ),
1038:     },
1039:     {
1040:       id: "evidence",
1041:       complete:
1042:         project.results.metrics.length > 0 ||
1043:         project.supportingDocuments.length > 0,
1044:     },
1045:     {
1046:       id: "skills",
1047:       complete: project.skillRelationships.length > 0,
1048:     },
1049:     {
1050:       id: "technologies",
1051:       complete: project.technologies.length > 0,
1052:     },
1053:     {
1054:       id: "summary",
1055:       complete: Boolean(project.presentation.shortSummary),
1056:     },
1057:     {
1058:       id: "media",
1059:       complete: activePublicMedia.length > 0,
1060:     },
1061:   ];
1062: 
1063:   const completedCount = checks.filter((check) => check.complete).length;
1064: 
1065:   const percentage = Math.round((completedCount / checks.length) * 100);
1066: 
1067:   let level = "Basic";
1068: 
1069:   if (percentage >= 90) {
1070:     level = "Portfolio Ready";
1071:   } else if (percentage >= 70) {
1072:     level = "Strong";
1073:   } else if (percentage >= 50) {
1074:     level = "Developing";
1075:   }
1076: 
1077:   return `${completedCount} of ${checks.length} professional elements completed Â· ${level}`;
1078: }
